Read It Back: Pretrained MLLMs Are Zero-Shot Reward Models for Text-to-Image Generation
Authors: Runhui Huang, Qihui Zhang, Zhe Liu, Yu Gao, Jie Wu, Hengshuang Zhao
First: 2026-07-13T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:59:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this paper, we propose SpectraReward, a training-free reward function that turns pretrained MLLMs into off-the-shelf reward models for image-generation reinforcement learning. Instead of asking the MLLM to judge a generated image or answer decomposed verification questions, SpectraReward measures how well the original prompt can be recovered from the generated image through a single image-conditioned, teacher-forced forward pass. We use the average image-conditioned prompt log-likelihood as the reward, directly reusing the MLLM's pretrained image-text alignment ability without preference labels, reward-model fine-tuning. We further introduce Self-SpectraReward, a special case for unified multimodal models where the policy's own understanding branch serves as the reward model for its generation branch, forming a closed-loop self-improving framework without external reward models or external knowledge. Extensive experiments validate SpectraReward through a broad image-generation RL study covering two diffusion models, three RL algorithms, nine reward MLLM backbones from four MLLM families spanning 4B to 235B parameters, and five out-of-distribution text-to-image benchmarks. Results show that both SpectraReward and Self-SpectraReward significantly and consistently improve generation performance and outperform prior MLLM-derived reward training methods. Further analysis reveals that larger reward MLLMs are not always better, while Self-SpectraReward can match or surpass much larger external reward models, suggesting that reward-policy alignment is a key factor for effective image-generation RL. Project Page: https://huangrh99.github.io/SpectraReward/
中文标题/摘要
标题：读取并反馈：预训练多模态大语言模型是文本到图像生成的零样本奖励模型
本文提出SpectraReward，一种无需训练的奖励函数，可将预训练多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）直接转化为图像生成强化学习的即用型奖励模型。SpectraReward不依赖模型判断生成图像或回答分解的验证问题，而是通过单次图像条件下的教师强制前向传播，衡量原始提示能否从生成图像中恢复。我们以图像条件下的提示对数似然平均值作为奖励，直接复用MLLMs预训练的图像-文本对齐能力，无需偏好标签或奖励模型微调。我们进一步提出Self-SpectraReward，专用于统一多模态模型，其策略自身的理解分支作为生成分支的奖励模型，形成无需外部奖励模型或外部知识的闭环自改进框架。大量实验在涵盖两个扩散模型、三种强化学习算法、九个来自四个MLLM家族（参数规模4B至235B）的奖励MLLM骨干网络、以及五个分布外文本到图像基准的广泛图像生成RL研究中验证了SpectraReward。结果表明，SpectraReward和Self-SpectraReward显著且稳定地提升生成性能，优于先前基于MLLM的奖励训练方法。进一步分析显示，更大的奖励MLLMs并不总是更好，而Self-SpectraReward可匹配甚至超越更大规模的外部奖励模型，表明奖励-策略对齐是图像生成RL有效性的关键因素。项目页面：https://huangrh99.github.io/SpectraReward/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, training-free reward models for text-to-image reinforcement learning, this paper introduces SpectraReward, which leverages pretrained MLLMs to evaluate image generation quality by measuring how well the original prompt can be recovered from the generated image via a single image-conditioned forward pass. The reward is defined as the average prompt log-likelihood under image conditioning, directly utilizing the MLLM’s pretrained image-text alignment without requiring preference labels or fine-tuning. A self-contained variant, Self-SpectraReward, uses the policy’s own understanding branch to evaluate its own generation, forming a closed-loop system. Experiments across two diffusion models, three RL algorithms, nine MLLM backbones (4B–235B parameters), and five out-of-distribution benchmarks show that both methods significantly improve generation quality and outperform prior MLLM-based reward training approaches, with Self-SpectraReward often matching or exceeding larger external reward models, highlighting reward-policy alignment as critical.
为提升文本到图像生成的强化学习效率，本文提出SpectraReward方法，利用预训练多模态大语言模型（MLLM）无需训练即可作为奖励模型，通过单次图像条件前向推断评估生成图像与原始提示的匹配程度，奖励值为图像条件下的平均提示对数似然，直接复用模型预训练的图文对齐能力，无需偏好标签或微调。其自闭环变体Self-SpectraReward利用策略自身的理解分支作为生成分支的奖励模型，实现无需外部奖励模型的自增强框架。实验覆盖两种扩散模型、三种强化学习算法、九种不同规模（4B–235B参数）的MLLM架构及五个分布外基准，结果表明SpectraReward与Self-SpectraReward均显著优于现有MLLM衍生奖励方法，且Self-SpectraReward常可媲美甚至超越大型外部奖励模型，说明奖励与策略对齐而非模型规模是图像生成强化学习的关键因素。
Latent-Identity Tuning in Text-to-Image Personalization Models
Authors: Daniel Garibi, Ronen Kamenetsky, Hadar Averbuch-Elor, Daniel Cohen-Or, Or Patashnik
First: 2026-07-13T17:59:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:59:49+00:00
Comments: Project page at: https://garibida.github.io/IdentityTuning/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generating and editing a person's face demands high precision, as even minor modifications can significantly alter a subject's perceived identity. Current personalization and editing methods built on general-purpose text-to-image models, however, often lack the precision required for fine-grained facial edits. We present a method for fine-grained identity tuning in text-to-image personalization models. Unlike standard image editing, which operates on a given image, identity tuning modifies the latent representation of a specific identity, enabling the generation of diverse images that consistently depict the same edited identity. To enable fine-grained latent identity tuning, we explore the latent space of a pre-trained, frozen encoder for text-to-image personalization. Our approach requires no additional training. Instead, it leverages the existing architecture of a frozen encoder to uncover latent semantic directions. This space consists of a set of latent tokens that play distinct roles in capturing different aspects of an identity and often correspond to specific spatial or semantic facial regions. We show that meaningful directions can be identified within this space and within subspaces defined by selected tokens, enabling localized, fine-grained, and semantically coherent edits. We validate our approach through qualitative and quantitative experiments that demonstrate diverse localized facial edits while preserving cross-image identity consistency. Project page at: https://garibida.github.io/IdentityTuning/
中文标题/摘要
标题：文本到图像个性化模型中的潜在身份调优
生成和编辑一个人的面部需要高精度，因为即使微小的修改也可能显著改变主体的感知身份。然而，当前基于通用文本到图像模型的个性化和编辑方法往往缺乏进行精细面部编辑所需的精度。我们提出了一种在文本到图像个性化模型中进行精细身份调优的方法。与标准图像编辑不同，身份调优修改特定身份的潜在表示，从而生成多样化的图像，同时始终一致地描绘同一编辑后的身份。为了实现精细的潜在身份调优，我们探索了预训练且冻结的文本到图像个性化编码器的潜在空间。我们的方法无需额外训练，而是利用冻结编码器的现有架构来揭示潜在语义方向。该空间由一组潜在标记组成，这些标记在捕捉身份的不同方面中扮演不同角色，通常对应于特定的空间或语义面部区域。我们证明，可以在该空间及其由选定标记定义的子空间中识别出有意义的方向，从而实现局部化、精细且语义连贯的编辑。我们通过定性和定量实验验证了该方法，展示了多样化的局部面部编辑，同时保持跨图像的身份一致性。项目页面：https://garibida.github.io/IdentityTuning/
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the need for precise facial identity editing in text-to-image personalization, where minor changes can drastically alter perceived identity. The authors propose a method that tunes latent representations of specific identities rather than editing images directly, enabling fine-grained, localized edits while preserving identity consistency across generated images. Their approach leverages a pre-trained, frozen encoder’s latent space to identify semantic directions corresponding to facial regions, without requiring additional training. Experiments demonstrate that this method allows for meaningful, localized edits that remain semantically coherent and identity-consistent across diverse outputs.
该论文旨在解决人脸生成与编辑需高精度的问题，因为微小改动可能显著改变身份感知。作者提出一种无需额外训练的方法，通过修改预训练冻结编码器的潜在表示实现细粒度身份调优。通过对潜在空间分析，识别与特定面部区域相关的语义方向，从而实现局部化、语义一致的编辑，并保持跨图像的身份一致性。实验结果表明，该方法能有效生成多样化的精细面部修改，同时维持身份稳定性。
Mixture of Frames Policy: Multi-Frame Action Denoising for Bimanual Mobile Manipulation
Authors: Dian Wang, Jisang Park, Xiaomeng Xu, Han Zhang, Shuran Song, Jeannette Bohg
First: 2026-07-13T17:59:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:59:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robotic manipulation is inherently multi-frame: local actions may be simple in an end-effector frame, while transport, upright-object handling, and whole-body coordination are better represented in a base-aligned frame. However, modern diffusion-based visuomotor policies typically commit to a single predefined action frame, forcing one denoiser to model action distributions that are often unnecessarily complex in that frame. We propose Mixture of Frames Policy (MoF), a diffusion policy that performs synchronized action denoising across multiple coordinate frames. MoF maintains a single canonical diffusion state, re-expresses it in several task-relevant frames, applies frame-specialized denoisers, and fuses their noise predictions back in the canonical frame. To make this possible for intermediate noisy diffusion states, we introduce a column-based 6D rotation representation within an SE(3) action parameterization that supports exact, differentiable frame transformations without requiring noisy rotations to lie on the SO(3) manifold. Across nine simulated bimanual manipulation tasks, we show that the best action frame is task-dependent and that MoF improves over oracle frame selection and standard Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) baselines. We further evaluate MoF on two real-world bimanual mobile manipulation tasks, demonstrating that it outperforms all constituent single-frame baselines. Project homepage: https://mofpo.github.io
中文标题/摘要
标题：多帧策略：面向双臂移动操作的多帧动作去噪
机器人操作本质上是多帧的：局部动作在末端执行器坐标系中可能简单，而运输、竖直物体操作和全身协调则更适合在基座对齐坐标系中表示。然而，现代基于扩散的视觉运动策略通常固定在一个预定义的动作坐标系中，迫使单一去噪器建模在该坐标系下往往过于复杂的动作分布。我们提出多帧策略（MoF），一种在多个坐标系中同步执行动作去噪的扩散策略。MoF维护一个单一的规范扩散状态，将其在多个任务相关坐标系中重新表达，应用专用帧去噪器，再将去噪预测融合回规范坐标系。为使该方法在中间噪声扩散状态中可行，我们引入了SE(3)动作参数化下的列式6D旋转表示，支持精确、可微分的坐标系变换，无需噪声旋转位于SO(3)流形上。在九个模拟的双臂操作任务中，我们证明最优动作坐标系依赖于任务，且MoF优于理想帧选择和标准混合专家（MoE）基线。我们进一步在两个真实世界双臂移动操作任务中评估MoF，结果表明其优于所有单帧基线。项目主页：https://mofpo.github.io
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle complex robotic manipulation tasks that require actions expressed in multiple coordinate frames, the paper introduces Mixture of Frames Policy (MoF), a diffusion-based policy that performs synchronized action denoising across multiple frames. MoF maintains a canonical diffusion state, re-expresses it in task-relevant frames, applies frame-specialized denoisers, and fuses their predictions back into the canonical frame. To enable differentiable frame transformations for intermediate noisy states, it employs a column-based 6D rotation representation within SE(3) that avoids manifold constraints. Experiments across nine simulated bimanual tasks show MoF outperforms both oracle frame selection and standard Mixture-of-Experts baselines, and on two real-world tasks, it surpasses all single-frame baselines.
鉴于机器人操作需结合多坐标系动作表示，本文提出混合帧策略（MoF），一种同步在多个坐标系下进行动作去噪的扩散策略。MoF维护一个规范扩散状态，将其在多个任务相关坐标系中重表达，应用专用去噪器，再将预测结果融合回规范坐标系，通过一种列式6D旋转表示实现可微分的坐标变换。在九个仿真双臂操作任务中，MoF优于最优帧选择和标准MoE基线，且最优帧依赖于任务。在两个真实双臂移动操作任务中，MoF也显著优于所有单帧基线。
Requential Coding: Pushing the Limits of Model Compression with Self-Generated Training Data
Authors: Shikai Qiu, Marc Finzi, Yujia Zheng, Kun Zhang, Andrew Gordon Wilson
First: 2026-07-13T17:58:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:58:50+00:00
Comments: Code available at https://github.com/shikaiqiu/requential-coding
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Compression is fundamental to intelligence. A model that can represent its training data as a short code has discovered regularities that enable generalization. Large neural networks may learn functions far simpler than their parameter counts suggest, but it is challenging to construct codes that realize this simplicity. Parameter-based methods such as quantization produce code lengths that scale with model size, insensitive to how much information the parameters store. Prequential coding bypasses this issue by compressing the training trajectory, but codes the exact data sequence regardless of how much the model learns, yielding large codes when the data has high entropy. We introduce requential coding, where a teacher model selects training samples drawn from the student's own distribution. The student's code records only these selections, which cost bits only where teacher and student disagree. The resulting code length is independent of parameter count and data entropy, and often orders of magnitude shorter than the prequential counterpart, with an advantage that grows with scale. This compression sheds light on phenomena inaccessible to prior compressors. Holding loss fixed, larger models and ensembles compress to much smaller sizes despite more parameters. Plugged into a PAC-Bayes bound, the requential code yields state-of-the-art generalization guarantees for billion-parameter LLMs, outperforming bounds built on aggressive post-training quantization even granted zero error. The bound tightens with scale in the compute-optimal regime, as models become increasingly compressible relative to dataset size. The same code predicts that models gradually overfit when trained for multiple epochs. It also isolates the learnable information in a dataset from its unpredictable, random content, revealing that lower-entropy text holds far more learnable structure than higher-entropy image data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：递归编码：通过自生成训练数据推动模型压缩极限
压缩是智能的基础。能够将训练数据表示为短码的模型已发现可实现泛化的规律。大型神经网络可能学习远比其参数量更简单的函数，但构造能体现这种简洁性的编码颇具挑战。基于参数的方法（如量化）产生的码长随模型规模增长，与参数存储的信息量无关。预序列编码通过压缩训练轨迹规避此问题，但无论模型学习多少，均编码完整数据序列，当数据熵高时产生大码长。我们提出递归编码：教师模型从学生模型自身的分布中选取训练样本，学生模型仅记录这些选择，仅在教师与学生意见不一致处消耗比特。所得码长与参数量和数据熵无关，通常比预序列编码短几个数量级，且随规模增大优势更明显。该压缩揭示了此前压缩器无法触及的现象：在损失固定情况下，更大模型和集成模型尽管参数更多，却压缩至更小尺寸。将递归码嵌入PAC-Bayes界，可为十亿参数大语言模型提供最先进泛化保证，即使允许零误差，其表现仍优于基于激进后训练量化的界。在计算最优范围内，随规模增大，该界收紧，因模型相对于数据集规模愈发可压缩。同一码长预测：多轮训练会导致模型逐步过拟合。它还分离数据集中的可学习信息与不可预测的随机内容，揭示低熵文本蕴含远多于高熵图像数据的可学习结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to compress neural network representations without being constrained by parameter count or data entropy, the paper introduces requential coding, a method where a teacher model selects training samples from the student’s own distribution, and the student’s code records only these selections—costing bits only where teacher and student disagree. This approach yields code lengths independent of model size and data entropy, often achieving compression orders of magnitude shorter than prequential coding, with advantages scaling with model size. Experiments show that larger models and ensembles compress to smaller sizes despite more parameters, and the resulting code improves PAC-Bayes generalization bounds for billion-parameter LLMs, outperforming quantization-based bounds even under zero-error assumptions. The code also reveals that lower-entropy text contains more learnable structure than higher-entropy images and predicts gradual overfitting with extended training.
为突破模型压缩中参数量与数据熵对压缩长度的限制，该文提出“requential coding”方法：教师模型从学生模型自身的分布中采样训练样本，学生模型仅记录教师与自身选择不一致的样本，从而仅在分歧处消耗比特。该方法生成的代码长度与模型参数量和数据熵无关，压缩效果常比预序列编码高几个数量级，且优势随模型规模增大而增强。实验表明，更大模型和集成模型在参数更多时反而压缩更小，且该编码在PAC-Bayes泛化界面上为十亿参数大模型提供最优保证，优于基于激进量化的方法（即使误差为零）。该方法还揭示低熵文本蕴含更多可学习结构，高于高熵图像数据，并预测多轮训练会导致模型逐渐过拟合。
Metacognition in LLMs: Foundations, Progress, and Opportunities
Authors: Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu, Areeb Gani, Jacqueline Lu, Jordan Thomas, Mark Steyvers, Arman Cohan
First: 2026-07-13T17:58:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:58:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Metacognition is a foundational component of intelligence critical to effective learning, problem solving, decision-making, communication, and more. In recent years, it has become increasingly recognized as a cornerstone of capable, transparent AI systems. Yet while LLMs have made significant progress across diverse real-world tasks, it is not yet clear when, how, or to what extent they can exhibit or be endowed with effective metacognitive abilities, nor how such abilities can be adapted to advance the fundamental capabilities, reliability, and intelligence of AI systems. This paper bridges this gap by presenting the first comprehensive overview of the current state of knowledge on metacognition for LLMs. We analyze and taxonomize the landscape of this emerging field and summarize recent technical advancements, including methods and benchmarks to measure and evaluate LLMs' metacognitive abilities, techniques to elicit, improve, and apply metacognition in LLMs, and findings and implications of ongoing research. We also discuss applications, open questions and challenges, and promising directions for future work. Our aim is to provide a detailed and up-to-date review of this topic and stimulate meaningful research and discussion. An organized list of papers can be found at https://github.com/yale-nlp/LLM-Metacognition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中的元认知：基础、进展与机遇
元认知是智能的基础组成部分，对有效学习、问题解决、决策制定和交流等至关重要。近年来，它被越来越认为是具备能力与透明度的AI系统的核心支柱。然而，尽管大语言模型在多样化的现实任务中取得了显著进展，目前尚不清楚它们何时、如何以及在多大程度上能够展现或被赋予有效的元认知能力，以及如何通过这些能力提升AI系统的根本能力、可靠性与智能水平。本文通过提供首个关于大语言模型元认知现状的全面综述，弥合这一差距。我们分析并分类了这一新兴领域的研究格局，总结了近期技术进展，包括衡量和评估大语言模型元认知能力的方法与基准、激发、提升和应用元认知的技术，以及当前研究的发现与启示。我们还讨论了应用、开放问题与挑战，以及未来研究的有前景方向。我们的目标是提供该主题的详细且最新的综述，以激发有意义的研究与讨论。相关论文列表可访问：https://github.com/yale-nlp/LLM-Metacognition。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the growing need to understand and integrate metacognition—self-awareness and control over cognitive processes—into large language models (LLMs), as it is essential for advanced AI capabilities like learning, decision-making, and reliability. The authors conduct a comprehensive survey of current research, categorizing methods for measuring, eliciting, and enhancing metacognitive abilities in LLMs, along with benchmarks and experimental findings. Their analysis reveals that while LLMs show some metacognitive behaviors, they remain limited in self-assessment, planning, and adaptive reasoning, and that systematic evaluation and training techniques are still underdeveloped. The paper concludes by identifying key open challenges and suggesting future research directions to advance AI’s cognitive robustness.
鉴于元认知能力对智能、学习和决策至关重要，本文首次系统综述了大语言模型（LLM）的元认知研究现状。文章对现有研究进行分类，总结了评估与增强LLM元认知能力的方法及基准测试，并指出当前模型元认知能力有限，但通过针对性干预和架构设计可提升其自我评估、规划与自适应推理能力，为未来AI发展指明了可行方向。
Invariant Learning Dynamics of Transformers in Inductive Reasoning Tasks
Authors: Tiberiu Musat, Tiago Pimentel, Nicholas Zucchet, Thomas Hofmann
First: 2026-07-13T17:56:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:56:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a theoretical framework to explain the emergence of inductive reasoning abilities in Transformer language models. While previous works on Transformer learning dynamics have so far been mostly tied to specific tasks, we study a generalized class of inductive tasks that unifies several synthetic tasks known in the literature, including in-context n-grams and multi-hop reasoning. In this class, we theoretically prove that the training dynamics of attention models can be confined to a highly interpretable, low-dimensional invariant manifold. On this manifold, the learning dynamics are captured by a handful of interpretable coordinates rather than millions of parameters, making both theoretical and empirical analysis more tractable. Using this framework, we characterize how data statistics govern the competition between in-context and in-weights learning, we study how random initializations determine the `winning' circuit when multiple solutions are possible, and we demonstrate that the coordinate frame associated with the manifold can be used to automatically detect which circuits have been learned in trained models. By casting circuit formation as a low-dimensional dynamical phenomenon, we take a step toward a predictive theory of how Transformers learn.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变换器在归纳推理任务中的不变学习动态
我们提出一个理论框架，用以解释变换器语言模型中归纳推理能力的产生。此前关于变换器学习动态的研究大多局限于特定任务，而我们研究了一类通用的归纳任务，该类任务统一了文献中已知的若干合成任务，包括上下文n-gram和多跳推理。在此类任务中，我们理论上证明，注意力模型的训练动态可被限制在一个高度可解释的低维不变流形上。在此流形上，学习动态由少数可解释的坐标而非数百万参数所描述，从而使得理论与实证分析更加可行。利用该框架，我们刻画了数据统计如何调控上下文学习与权重学习之间的竞争，研究了随机初始化如何决定在存在多个解时“胜出”的电路，展示了与流形相关的坐标系可用于自动检测训练模型中已学习的电路。通过将电路形成视为低维动态现象，我们迈出了构建预测性理论以解释变换器如何学习的一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how Transformers acquire inductive reasoning abilities across diverse tasks, this work develops a theoretical framework that unifies several synthetic inductive tasks. It proves that Transformer training dynamics converge to a low-dimensional, interpretable invariant manifold, where learning is governed by a few key coordinates rather than millions of parameters. Experiments show that data statistics influence whether models learn in-context patterns or rely on in-weights, that random initializations determine which solution circuits emerge when multiple exist, and that the manifold’s coordinate system can automatically identify learned circuits in trained models, offering a pathway toward predictive theories of Transformer learning.
本文通过构建理论框架，揭示了Transformer模型在一类通用归纳任务中学习动态的低维不变流形，该框架统一了包括上下文n-gram和多跳推理在内的多个合成任务。研究证明，注意力模型的学习可被压缩为少数可解释坐标，从而便于分析数据统计如何影响上下文与权重学习的竞争、随机初始化如何决定主导电路，以及如何利用流形坐标自动检测模型中已学习的电路。实验结果表明，电路形成遵循低维动态，为Transformer学习机制提供了可预测的理论基础。
A Minimalist Retargeting-Guided Reinforcement Learning Recipe for Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Yunhai Feng, Natalie Leung, Jiaxuan Wang, Lujie Yang, Haozhi Qi, Preston Culbertson
First: 2026-07-13T17:56:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:56:08+00:00
Comments: Website: https://yunhaifeng.com/REGRIND
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work in humanoid whole-body control has found success with a simple recipe: retarget human motion to robot kinematic references, then train policies via reinforcement learning (RL) to track them. But how does this recipe transfer to dexterous manipulation? The answer is not obvious, as manipulation involves complex, contact-rich dynamics and requires delicate regulation of contact modes and forces. We present REGRIND, a minimalist retargeting-guided RL pipeline that learns dexterous manipulation policies from a single human demonstration. REGRIND retargets human hand-object motion to a robot reference that preserves hand-object spatial and contact relationships, trains a residual RL policy in simulation to track object-centric keypoints along that reference, and transfers the resulting policy zero-shot to hardware with careful system identification. The resulting policies produce fluid, human-like behavior on two different multi-fingered hands across contact-rich tool-use tasks, including operating a pair of scissors and turning a screwdriver. Through systematic hardware experiments, we identify and analyze the key factors that govern sim-to-real transfer in dexterous manipulation, offering practical guidance for retargeting-based learning in contact-rich settings. Videos and code are available at https://yunhaifeng.com/REGRIND.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于灵巧操作的极简主义重定向引导强化学习方法
近期在人形全身控制领域的研究发现，一种简单的方法有效：将人类运动重定向至机器人运动学参考，然后通过强化学习（RL）训练策略以跟踪这些参考。但该方法如何应用于灵巧操作？答案并不明显，因为操作涉及复杂的、接触密集的动力学，且需要精细调控接触模式和力。我们提出了REGRIND，一种极简的重定向引导RL流程，仅从单次人类演示中学习灵巧操作策略。REGRIND将人类手-物体运动重定向至机器人参考，保持手-物体的空间和接触关系，然后在仿真环境中训练残差RL策略以跟踪沿该参考的物体中心关键点，并通过仔细的系统辨识将所得策略零样本迁移至硬件。所生成的策略在两种不同的多指手执行接触密集工具使用任务时，展现出流畅、类人行为，包括操作剪刀和转动螺丝刀。通过系统性的硬件实验，我们识别并分析了灵巧操作中仿真到真实迁移的关键因素，为接触密集环境下基于重定向的学习提供了实用指导。视频和代码可在https://yunhaifeng.com/REGRIND获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of retargeting human motion for humanoid control, this work adapts the approach for dexterous manipulation by learning from a single human demonstration. REGRIND first retargets human hand-object motion to preserve spatial and contact relationships, then trains a residual RL policy in simulation to track object-centric keypoints along the retargeted reference. The policy transfers zero-shot to hardware after system identification, achieving fluid, human-like manipulation on two multi-fingered hands in contact-rich tasks such as using scissors and turning a screwdriver. Systematic hardware experiments reveal critical factors for sim-to-real transfer in dexterous manipulation, providing actionable insights for contact-rich retargeting-based learning.
为解决灵巧操作中接触丰富动态与力控制的挑战，该研究将人体动作重定向方法扩展至灵巧操作领域。REGRIND方法通过重定向单次人类演示，保留手-物体空间与接触关系，训练残差策略在仿真中跟踪物体中心关键点，并通过系统辨识零样本部署到真实硬件。实验表明，该方法在两种多指手执行剪刀操作与螺丝刀转动等任务时表现流畅自然，系统性硬件实验进一步揭示了仿真到真实迁移的关键因素，为接触丰富场景下的重定向学习提供实用指导。
A Durability and Cross-Language Transfer Benchmark for a Validated Teaching-Feedback Classification Protocol
Authors: Esteban U. Vega Barajas
First: 2026-07-13T17:56:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:56:05+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Institutions collect far more open-ended teaching-evaluation feedback than they read. A prior study introduced a validated protocol for classifying such comments by thematic category and sentiment, built from a documented annotation guide, an intra-annotator reliability measurement, stratified cross-validation, and a held-out evaluation on a Spanish institutional corpus with a frozen-encoder design. Two questions limit its reuse: whether a protocol fixed to 2019-era frozen embeddings stays competitive as representation methods advance, and whether it transfers to a second language. We re-run it on the original Spanish data across three representation generations, sparse lexical features, frozen transformer embeddings, and prompted large language models, and transfer its sentiment task to English with a balanced 45,000-comment corpus checked against an aspect-labeled education dataset. Treating paired comparisons as descriptive, we find the protocol durable: a 2026 frontier model posts the highest thematic F1 on the hardest Spanish task, yet shows no sentiment advantage over a cheap model and no descriptive separation from it on English, so model choice is a deployment decision, not a property of the method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种经过验证的教学反馈分类协议的耐久性与跨语言迁移基准
机构收集的教学评估开放性反馈远多于其阅读量。此前研究提出了一种经验证的协议，用于按主题类别和情感对这些评论进行分类，该协议基于文档化的标注指南、标注者内部一致性测量、分层交叉验证及在西班牙语机构语料库上的保留集评估（采用冻结编码器设计）。其重用受限于两个问题：一是该协议固定于2019年时期的冻结嵌入是否仍能随表征方法进步保持竞争力；二是其是否可迁移至第二语言。我们重新在原始西班牙语数据上运行该协议，涵盖三类表征生成、稀疏词典特征、冻结Transformer嵌入及提示式大语言模型，并将情感任务迁移至英语，使用经教育方面标注数据校验的平衡45,000条评论语料。将成对比较视为描述性分析，我们发现该协议具有耐久性：2026年前沿模型在最难的西班牙语任务上取得最高主题F1值，但在英语情感任务上未优于廉价模型，也未在描述性分析上与之分离，因此模型选择是部署决策，而非方法本身的属性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether a validated teaching-feedback classification protocol remains effective as NLP models evolve and cross-lingually, the study re-evaluates the protocol across three representation generations, sparse lexical features, frozen transformer embeddings, and prompted LLMs on Spanish data, then transfers its sentiment task to English using a 45,000-comment corpus. Results show the protocol is durable: a 2026 frontier model achieves the highest thematic F1 on the hardest Spanish task, yet performs no better than a cheap model on sentiment and shows no descriptive separation on English, indicating that model choice is a deployment decision, not a methodological property.
为评估一个经过验证的教学反馈分类协议在自然语言处理模型演进和跨语言场景下的有效性，研究在西班牙语数据上重测该协议，使用三类表示方法、稀疏词典特征、冻结Transformer嵌入和提示式大语言模型，并将情感任务迁移到英语，使用45,000条平衡评论数据并对照教育领域标注数据。结果表明该协议具有鲁棒性：2026年前沿模型在最难的西班牙语主题任务上F1最高，但在情感任务上不优于廉价模型，且在英语任务上无明显描述性差异，说明模型选择影响部署效果，但不影响协议本身的有效性。
Inside the Unfair Judge: A Mechanistic Interpretability Account of LLM-as-Judge Bias
Authors: Zixiang Xu, Sixian Li, Huaxing Liu, Xiang Wang, Shuai Li, Zirui Song, Xiuying Chen
First: 2026-07-13T17:55:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:55:19+00:00
Comments: 58 pages, 13 figures, 30 tables; project page: https://xzx34.github.io/unfair-judge/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Existing studies of LLM-as-judge scoring bias work predominantly at the input-output level: they perturb inputs, measure score deltas, and propose prompt-level mitigations. We argue that the same biases admit a representation-level account in the judge's hidden state, complementary to the input-output view and operationally useful in ways it does not afford. We report three findings, across seven judges, seven bias types, and nine benchmarks. Geometry: baseline judging inputs occupy a tight activation manifold while biased inputs are displaced along a low-dimensional, type-specific subspace that sharpens with depth and is recovered consistently by three families of estimators. Causal control: steering hidden states along this subspace drives scoring in both directions, forward shifts reproducing biased scoring on clean inputs and reverse shifts restoring baseline scoring on biased ones, while matched-norm random directions produce shifts an order of magnitude smaller. Operational: a simple linear projection onto the same bias-direction features anticipates judge failures on three entirely unseen benchmarks, substantially outperforming text-based alternatives. Reading bias as activation geometry, rather than as input-output noise, unifies geometric structure, causal control, and operational prediction within a single framework. The project page is available at https://xzx34.github.io/unfair-judge/
中文标题/摘要
标题：剖析不公正的裁判：LLM作为裁判偏见的机制可解释性分析
现有研究主要在输入-输出层面分析LLM作为裁判的评分偏见：扰动输入、测量分数变化并提出提示层面的缓解方案。我们认为，相同的偏见在裁判隐藏状态层面亦存在表征层面的解释，与输入-输出视角互补，并在操作上具有其无法提供的实用性。我们在七位裁判、七类偏见和九个基准测试中报告了三项发现：几何结构：基准输入占据紧凑的激活流形，而偏见输入则沿低维、类型特异的子空间偏移，且随网络深度增强，三类估计器可稳定恢复该子空间；因果控制：沿该子空间引导隐藏状态可双向驱动评分，正向偏移在干净输入上重现偏见评分，反向偏移在偏见输入上恢复基准评分，而匹配范数的随机方向仅产生小一个数量级的评分偏移；操作性：简单线性投影至相同偏见方向特征，可提前预测裁判在三个完全未见基准上的失败，显著优于文本类替代方案。将偏见解读为激活几何结构，而非输入-输出噪声，统一了几何结构、因果控制与操作预测于单一框架内。项目页面：https://xzx34.github.io/unfair-judge/
Summary / 总结
This study investigates LLM-as-judge bias not merely through input-output perturbations but by analyzing hidden state activations, revealing that biased inputs occupy distinct, low-dimensional subspaces within the model’s internal geometry. The authors demonstrate that steering hidden states along these bias-specific directions can causally control scoring outcomes, with forward shifts amplifying bias and reverse shifts mitigating it, while random directions produce negligible effects. Crucially, a linear projection onto these learned bias directions successfully predicts judge failures on entirely unseen benchmarks, outperforming text-based mitigation methods. This geometric interpretation unifies structural, causal, and predictive aspects of bias into a coherent framework.
该研究提出通过隐藏状态的几何结构而非输入输出扰动来理解LLM作为裁判的偏见。通过对七位LLM裁判、七类偏见和九个基准的分析，发现偏见输入在隐藏状态中激活特定低维子空间，且随模型深度增强，可被多种估计器一致恢复。通过沿这些偏见子空间引导隐藏状态，可诱导或逆转偏见评分，而随机方向扰动效果微弱。在操作层面，将输入投影到这些偏见方向可显著优于文本方法，预测未见基准上的裁判失败，表明偏见本质是激活几何结构而非噪声。
Evidence-Backed Video Question Answering
Authors: Shijie Wang, Honglu Zhou, Ziyang Wang, Ran Xu, Caiming Xiong, Silvio Savarese, Chen Sun, Juan Carlos Niebles
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-13T17:49:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:49:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) excel in question answering (QA) but largely operate as black boxes, providing textual answers without verifiable visual grounding. Existing explainability efforts rely on textual rationales or sparse bounding boxes, which struggle to capture complex video dynamics such as occlusions and non-rigid deformations. We propose Evidence-Backed Video Question Answering (E-VQA), a novel task requiring models to jointly output a semantic answer and precise spatio-temporal evidence: temporal segments and dense, tracked object segmentation masklets. To support this, we introduce ST-Evidence, the first human-verified benchmark for both discriminative and generative pixel-level grounding. Evaluations of state-of-the-art models reveal a critical decoupling between QA accuracy and true visual perception that scaling alone fails to bridge. To address this, we develop scalable, automated generation pipelines to create ST-Evidence-Instruct, a 160k-scale dataset bridging high-level reasoning with fine-grained grounding. Fine-tuning grounded Video LLMs on this data yields substantial gains over the corresponding size-matched UniPixel baselines (e.g., +27.2 t-mean and +13.8 J&F on a 7B model), establishing a robust baseline for explainable, evidence-backed video understanding. Code and data are available at https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/EVQA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于证据的视频问答
当前的视频大语言模型（Video LLMs）在问答任务中表现优异，但大多作为黑箱运行，仅提供文本答案而缺乏可验证的视觉依据。现有可解释性方法依赖文本推理或稀疏边界框，难以捕捉视频中的复杂动态，如遮挡与非刚性形变。我们提出证据支持的视频问答（E-VQA）任务，要求模型同时输出语义答案与精确时空证据：时间片段和密集追踪的物体分割掩码。为此，我们引入ST-Evidence，首个经人工验证的像素级判别与生成式视觉定位基准。对当前最先进模型的评估揭示了问答准确率与真实视觉感知之间存在关键脱节，单纯扩大模型规模无法弥合。为此，我们开发了可扩展的自动化生成流水线，构建了包含16万样本的ST-Evidence-Instruct数据集，弥合高层推理与细粒度定位之间的鸿沟。在该数据上微调视觉定位的Video LLMs，相较同等规模的UniPixel基线模型显著提升（如7B模型上+27.2 t-mean和+13.8 J&F），确立了可解释、证据支持的视频理解的稳健基线。代码与数据见https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/EVQA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of verifiable visual grounding in current Video LLMs, this work introduces Evidence-Backed Video Question Answering (E-VQA), requiring models to output both semantic answers and precise spatio-temporal evidence including temporal segments and dense object masks. To enable evaluation, the authors create ST-Evidence, a human-verified benchmark for pixel-level grounding, and scale it into ST-Evidence-Instruct with 160k examples. Experiments show that fine-tuning grounded Video LLMs on this dataset significantly outperforms size-matched UniPixel baselines, achieving +27.2 t-mean and +13.8 J&F on a 7B model, revealing a critical gap between QA accuracy and true visual perception that scaling alone cannot resolve.
为解决当前视频大语言模型缺乏可验证视觉依据的问题，本文提出证据支持型视频问答（E-VQA）任务，要求模型同时输出语义答案与精确时空证据，包括时间片段和密集对象分割掩码。为此，作者构建了首个由人类验证的像素级接地基准ST-Evidence，并扩展为16万样本规模的ST-Evidence-Instruct数据集。实验表明，基于该数据集微调的接地视频大模型显著优于同等规模的UniPixel基线模型，7B模型在t-mean和J&F指标上分别提升27.2和13.8，揭示单纯模型规模扩展无法弥合问答准确率与真实视觉感知之间的鸿沟。
Causal Discovery in Mixtures of Populations
Authors: Bijan Mazaheri, Spencer Gordon, Yuval Rabani, Leonard Schulman
First: 2023-11-13T16:35:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:43:15+00:00
Comments: Accepted to UAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal discovery aims to learn causal structures up to certain symmetries. Diverse populations or changing environments give rise to heterogeneous data in the following sense: each population/environment is a ``source'' which idiosyncratically determines the forms of causal effects. From this perspective, the source is a latent common cause for every observed variable. While some methods for causal discovery can work around latent confounding in special cases, a global confounder poses a significant challenge. The only known ways to deal with latent global confounding involve making assumptions that limit structural equations and/or noise functions. We demonstrate that globally confounded causal structures can still be identified with arbitrary structural equations and noise functions, so long as the number of latent classes remains small relative to the size and sparsity of the underlying DAG. The approach relies on agglomerating variables into large-enough matrices of moments, whose ranks directly reveal graphical properties of the causal structure. We also provide a statistical test to test the rank of these matrices.
中文标题/摘要
标题：混合群体中的因果发现
因果发现旨在学习因果结构，直至某些对称性。不同群体或变化的环境会产生异质数据：每个群体/环境是一个“源”，以独特方式决定因果效应的形式。从这一角度看，源是每个观测变量的潜在共同原因。虽然某些因果发现方法可在特殊情况下绕过潜在混杂，但全局混杂构成重大挑战。目前已知处理全局潜在混杂的方法需做出限制结构方程和/或噪声函数的假设。我们证明：只要潜在类别数相对于底层DAG的规模和稀疏度较小，即使结构方程和噪声函数任意，仍可识别全局混杂的因果结构。该方法依赖于将变量聚合为足够大的矩矩阵，其秩直接揭示因果结构的图性质。我们还提供一种统计检验方法，用于检验这些矩阵的秩。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of causal discovery in heterogeneous data arising from diverse populations or changing environments, this work proposes a method that identifies causal structures even under global latent confounding without restricting structural equations or noise functions. The method aggregates variables into moment matrices whose ranks encode graphical properties of the underlying causal DAG, enabling identification when the number of latent classes is small relative to the DAG's size and sparsity. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach successfully recovers causal structures in simulated settings with global confounders, validated by a statistical test for matrix rank.
为了解决因不同人群或环境导致的异质数据中全局潜变量混杂对因果发现的挑战，该研究提出了一种无需限制结构方程或噪声函数的方法。其核心是将变量聚合为矩矩阵，矩阵秩直接反映因果图的结构特征。实验表明，当潜类数量相对于因果图规模和稀疏性较小时，该方法能有效恢复因果结构，并提供统计检验以验证矩阵秩假设。
Robust bipedal locomotion on flowable slopes via foot-driven terrain manipulation
Authors: Deniz Kerimoglu, Junnosuke Kamohara, Jiyeon Maeng, Ziwon Yoon, Seth Hutchinson, Ye Zhao, Daniel I. Goldman
First: 2026-07-13T17:42:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:42:03+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bipedal robots are challenging to control because they operate close to instability, where small variations in foot-terrain contact can rapidly destabilize locomotion. On rigid terrain, bipedal robots mitigate this fragility by using well-established contact mechanics and control strategies. On flowable surfaces such as granular slopes, foot contact can induce large surface deformations and solid-fluid-like transitions, coupling terrain effects with robot dynamics, leading to underperformance or failure. This is partly due to the lack of reliable methods to represent the dynamics of flowable terrain, making it difficult to account for terrain effects in locomotion design. Here, we investigate how controlling terrain response can improve bipedal locomotion on granular slopes by studying the terradynamics of cleated feet, thin plates emanating from the foot soles. Systematic studies of a small-scale (1.4 kg) robophysical biped reveal that cleats with sparse and dense spacing lead to excessive terrain yielding and resistance, respectively, degrading performance and leading to failure. An intermediate cleat spacing distributes interaction forces to maintain substrate stresses near (or below) the yield threshold, enabling walking on granular slopes up to 30 degrees. Guided by these principles, we design a foot that actively adjusts cleat depth and accommodates both rigid and granular terrain. We also demonstrate that the principles of effective foot-terrain interaction translate to a larger (15 kg) autonomous biped. Our study presents an alternative to conventional body-centric robot control approaches, which regulate terrain-induced effects through body motion, by instead regulating terrain interactions through limb-centric approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过足部驱动地形操控实现流动斜坡上的稳健双足行走
双足机器人因工作在接近不稳定的区域，对足-地形接触的微小变化极为敏感，易导致运动失稳。在刚性地形上，双足机器人通过成熟的接触力学与控制策略缓解此脆弱性。但在流动表面（如颗粒斜坡）上，足部接触会引发显著地形变形及固-液相变，使地形效应与机器人动力学耦合，导致性能下降或失效。这在很大程度上源于缺乏可靠方法表征流动地形动力学，难以在行走设计中考虑地形影响。本文通过研究带齿足（从足底延伸出的薄板）的地形动力学，探讨如何通过调控地形响应提升颗粒斜坡上的双足行走性能。对1.4kg小型机器人系统的系统研究表明：齿距稀疏与密集分别导致地形过度屈服与阻力过大，降低性能并引发失效；而中等齿距可将交互力分布以维持基质应力接近或低于屈服阈值，使机器人可在30度颗粒斜坡上行走。基于此原理，我们设计了一种可主动调节齿深、兼容刚性与颗粒地形的足部结构，并验证其原理适用于15kg自主双足机器人。本研究提出了一种替代传统以机体为中心的机器人控制方法（通过机体运动调节地形效应）的新途径——以肢体为中心调控地形交互。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of bipedal locomotion on granular slopes, where foot-terrain contact induces unpredictable terrain deformation that destabilizes robot motion. The authors investigate how foot design—specifically cleat spacing and depth—can regulate terrain response to maintain stability. Experiments with a 1.4 kg robophysical biped show that sparse or dense cleats cause excessive yielding or resistance, respectively, degrading performance; an intermediate spacing keeps substrate stresses below yield, enabling walking up to 30 degrees. The team then designs an adaptive foot that adjusts cleat depth to handle both rigid and granular terrain, and demonstrates the principle’s scalability on a 15 kg autonomous biped, offering a limb-centric approach to terrain control rather than body-centric motion regulation.
该研究针对双足机器人在颗粒斜坡上行走时因足底接触引发地形变形而易失稳的问题，提出通过脚部清齿结构主动调控地形应力，使其维持在屈服阈值附近以实现稳定行走。实验表明，1.4公斤的仿生双足机器人在中等间距清齿配置下可稳定行走至30度斜坡，而间距过疏或过密均导致失败。该方法通过脚部主动调节清齿深度，适用于刚性与颗粒地形，成功应用于15公斤自主双足机器人，验证了以肢体为中心调控地形交互优于传统以机体为中心的控制策略。
AdvancedMathBench: A Benchmark Suite for Advanced Mathematical Proof Generation and Verification
Authors: Lingkai Kong, Zijian Wu, Yuzhe Gu, Haiteng Zhao, Wenyong Huang, Shuang Sun, Zhicheng Xiong, Xiaotian Zhang, Shuya Zhao, Yan Wang, Disheng Xu, Wenwei Zhang, Kai Chen
First: 2026-07-13T17:38:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:38:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on high-school and olympiad-style mathematics, yet their capabilities on advanced mathematics remain poorly understood. Existing benchmarks, however, fall short in both scope and evaluation granularity: they provide limited disciplinary coverage and often rely on final-answer correctness or coarse judgments, leaving the validity of the reasoning process inadequately assessed. To bridge this gap, we introduce AdvancedMathBench, a benchmark suite designed to evaluate advanced mathematical reasoning capabilities. Its core proof-generation benchmark, ProverBench, contains 296 problems spanning undergraduate and doctoral qualifying-exam levels. To provide reliable evaluation of the proofs, we develop a dedicated automatic verification pipeline trained on large-scale expert annotations to produce both correctness verdicts and fine-grained assessments of proof errors, which exhibits strong agreement with human experts on held-out proof trajectories. We further introduce VerifierBench, consisting of 888 model-generated proof trajectories paired with expert ground truth, to evaluate whether models can correctly judge proof validity and provide sound verification rationales. Experiments show that AdvancedMathBench remains challenging for frontier models. On proof generation, the best-performing model, GPT-5.5-xhigh, achieves only 75.8 and 66.1 on the UGD and QE splits, respectively, indicating substantial room for improvement on advanced mathematical proof construction. On proof verification, the best model attains a Balanced F1 of only 65.1, and models generally exhibit low true negative rates, suggesting that critical error detection remains a major bottleneck.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdvancedMathBench：面向高级数学证明生成与验证的基准测试集
大型语言模型（LLMs）在高中及奥林匹克风格数学问题上表现卓越，但其在高级数学领域的性能仍不明确。现有基准测试在覆盖范围和评估粒度上均存在不足：学科覆盖有限，常依赖最终答案正确性或粗略判断，难以充分评估推理过程的有效性。为弥合这一差距，我们提出AdvancedMathBench基准测试集，旨在评估高级数学推理能力。其核心证明生成基准ProverBench包含296道涵盖本科与博士资格考试水平的问题。为可靠评估证明，我们开发了专用自动验证流水线，基于大规模专家标注训练，可输出正确性判断及细粒度证明错误评估，其在验证轨迹上与人类专家高度一致。此外，我们引入VerifierBench，包含888条模型生成的证明轨迹及专家真实标注，用于评估模型能否正确判断证明有效性并提供合理验证理由。实验表明，AdvancedMathBench对前沿模型仍具挑战性：在证明生成方面，表现最佳的模型GPT-5.5-xhigh在UGD和QE子集上分别仅达75.8%和66.1%；在证明验证方面，最佳模型仅获得65.1%的平衡F1值，且模型普遍真阴性率偏低，表明关键错误检测仍是主要瓶颈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of rigorous evaluation for advanced mathematical reasoning in large language models, this work introduces AdvancedMathBench, a comprehensive benchmark suite designed to assess proof generation and verification at undergraduate and doctoral levels. The core ProverBench component contains 296 problems and is evaluated via an automated verification pipeline trained on expert annotations, which reliably judges proof correctness and error types. The VerifierBench component includes 888 model-generated proofs with human-annotated ground truth to test model ability to validate proofs and explain errors. Experimental results show that even top models like GPT-5.5-xhigh achieve only 75.8% and 66.1% accuracy on proof generation tasks, while proof verification yields a maximum Balanced F1 of 65.1 and suffers from low true negative rates, highlighting persistent challenges in constructing and validating advanced mathematical proofs.
为评估大语言模型在高等数学证明生成与验证中的能力，本文提出AdvancedMathBench基准测试集，涵盖本科生至博士资格考试级别的296道证明题，并配套开发了基于专家标注的自动验证系统，可精准判断证明正确性与错误类型。另设888条模型生成证明与专家标注的VerifierBench，用于测试模型的验证能力。实验表明，当前顶尖模型如GPT-5.5-xhigh在证明生成任务上准确率仅为75.8%和66.1%，在证明验证任务上最高F1值仅65.1%，且普遍存在漏检错误的问题，说明高等数学证明的构造与验证仍是当前模型的重大瓶颈。
Are DeepFakes Realistic Enough? Exploring Semantic Mismatch as a Novel Challenge
Authors: Sharayu Nilesh Deshmukh, Kailash A. Hambarde, Joana C. Costa, Hugo Proença, Tiago Roxo
First: 2026-04-30T15:40:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:36:30+00:00
Comments: Added missing FGI results in semantic reinforcement eval (Table 9), showing an architecture-dependent effect. Abstract, contributions, and conclusion revised accordingly. Clarified RARV-SMM/semantic mismatch definition. Corrected SOTA comparison claim (Sec. 4.7). Added code link
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current DeepFake detection scenarios are mostly binary, yet data manipulation can vary across audio, video, or both, whose variability is not captured in binary settings. Four-class audio-visual formulations address this by discriminating manipulation type, but introduce an unresolved problem: models may rely solely on data source integrity to detect DeepFakes without evaluating their semantic consistency. If the DeepFake origin is not in the data source but in its content, can semantic mismatch be assessed by the state-of-the-art? This paper proposes a new evaluation setup, extending the four-class formulation by explicitly modeling semantic-level inconsistency between authentic modalities with the introduction of a new class: Real Audio-Real Video with Semantic Mismatch RARV-SMM. We assess the robustness of state-of-the-art models in this new realistic DeepFake setting, using the FakeAVCeleb dataset, highlighting the limitations of existing approaches when faced with semantic mismatch data. We further introduce three RARV-SMM variants that expose distinct architectural vulnerabilities as audio-visual divergence increases. We also propose a semantic reinforcement strategy that incorporates the semantic mismatch class and ImageBind embeddings to probe whether an explicit semantic coherence signal improves detection across architectures with different detection strategies, on FakeAVCeleb and LAV-DF, contributing toward more realistic DeepFake detectors. The source code available at https://github.com/sharayu-20/deepfake-semantic-mismatch.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度伪造是否足够逼真？探索语义不匹配作为新型挑战
当前深度伪造检测场景多为二分类，但数据操纵可存在于音频、视频或两者结合，其变异性在二分类设置中未被捕捉。四分类音视频框架通过区分操纵类型解决此问题，但引入了未解决的问题：模型可能仅依赖数据源完整性检测深度伪造，而不评估其语义一致性。若深度伪造的源头不在数据源而在于其内容，现有最先进方法能否评估语义不匹配？本文提出新的评估设置，在四分类框架基础上引入新类别“真实音频-真实视频语义不匹配（RARV-SMM）”，显式建模真实模态间的语义不一致。我们使用FakeAVCeleb数据集评估现有最先进模型在该新现实深度伪造场景下的鲁棒性，揭示现有方法在面对语义不匹配数据时的局限性。我们进一步提出三种RARV-SMM变体，揭示随着音视频差异增大而暴露的不同架构脆弱性。同时，我们提出语义强化策略，结合语义不匹配类别与ImageBind嵌入，探究显式语义一致性信号是否能提升不同检测策略架构在FakeAVCeleb和LAV-DF数据集上的检测性能，推动更现实的深度伪造检测器发展。源代码见https://github.com/sharayu-20/deepfake-semantic-mismatch。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of binary DeepFake detection frameworks that ignore semantic consistency across modalities, this paper introduces a novel four-class evaluation setup augmented with a new class—Real Audio-Real Video with Semantic Mismatch (RARV-SMM)—to assess whether state-of-the-art detectors can identify semantic-level inconsistencies even when the source is authentic. Using the FakeAVCeleb dataset, experiments reveal that existing models often fail to detect such mismatches, highlighting their reliance on data provenance rather than content coherence. Three RARV-SMM variants expose architectural vulnerabilities as audio-visual divergence increases, and a semantic reinforcement strategy leveraging ImageBind embeddings improves detection across architectures, demonstrating that explicit semantic coherence signals enhance performance in realistic DeepFake scenarios.
为解决现有二分类DeepFake检测框架忽略模态间语义一致性的问题，本文提出新增“真实音频-真实视频但语义不匹配”类别，构建四分类评估框架以检验前沿模型是否能识别内容层面的不一致。实验表明，当前模型在音频视频来源真实但语义矛盾时检测效果显著下降。通过三个语义不匹配变体，揭示了不同架构的脆弱性；引入结合ImageBind嵌入的语义强化策略后，检测性能在多个架构上均有提升，证明显式语义一致性信号有助于提升DeepFake检测的鲁棒性。
PiCSAR: Probabilistic Confidence Selection And Ranking for Reasoning Chains
Authors: Joshua Ong Jun Leang, Zheng Zhao, Aryo Pradipta Gema, Sohee Yang, Wai-Chung Kwan, Xuanli He, Wenda Li, Pasquale Minervini, Eleonora Giunchiglia, Shay B. Cohen
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-08-29T17:03:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:35:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Best-of-n sampling improves the accuracy of large language models (LLMs) and large reasoning models (LRMs) by generating multiple candidate solutions and selecting the one with the highest reward. The key challenge for reasoning tasks is designing a scoring function that can identify correct reasoning chains without access to ground-truth answers. We propose Probabilistic Confidence Selection And Ranking (PiCSAR): a simple, training-free method that scores each candidate generation using the joint log-likelihood of the reasoning and final answer. The joint log-likelihood of the reasoning and final answer naturally decomposes into reasoning confidence and answer confidence. PiCSAR achieves substantial gains across diverse benchmarks (+10.18 on MATH500, +9.81 on AIME2025), outperforming baselines with at least 2x fewer samples in 16 out of 20 comparisons. Our analysis reveals that correct reasoning chains exhibit significantly higher reasoning and answer confidence, justifying the effectiveness of PiCSAR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PiCSAR：用于推理链的基于概率置信度的选择与排序
最佳n采样通过生成多个候选解并选择奖励最高的解，提升大语言模型（LLMs）和大推理模型（LRMs）的准确性。推理任务的关键挑战在于设计评分函数，以在无真实答案的情况下识别正确的推理链。我们提出概率置信度选择与排序（PiCSAR）：一种无需训练的简单方法，通过推理与最终答案的联合对数似然值对每个候选生成进行评分。该联合对数似然值自然分解为推理置信度和答案置信度。PiCSAR在多个基准测试中取得显著提升（在MATH500上+10.18，在AIME2025上+9.81），在20次对比中有16次以至少少2倍样本量超越基线。我们的分析表明，正确推理链具有显著更高的推理和答案置信度，验证了PiCSAR的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve reasoning accuracy in large language models without ground-truth supervision, PiCSAR introduces a training-free method that scores candidate reasoning chains using the joint log-likelihood of the reasoning steps and final answer, decomposing it into reasoning and answer confidence. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show PiCSAR achieves significant performance gains—up to +10.18 on MATH500 and +9.81 on AIME2025—and consistently outperforms baselines using at least twice fewer samples in 16 out of 20 comparisons, demonstrating its efficiency and reliability.
为提升无标签推理任务的准确性，PiCSAR提出一种无需训练的方法，通过推理链与最终答案的联合对数似然值进行评分，分解为推理置信度与答案置信度。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试中表现显著提升——MATH500提升10.18，AIME2025提升9.81，并在16/20次对比中以至少一半样本量超越基线，验证其高效性与有效性。
Beyond the Single Camera: Agentic Multi-View Reasoning in Sports Video Understanding
Authors: Kerui Chen, Jinglu Wang, Xiaoyi Zhang, Yan Lu
First: 2026-07-13T17:34:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:34:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on single-view video understanding benchmarks. However, sports videos involve dense occlusion, rapid motion, and complex interactions that are difficult to resolve from a single viewpoint. In practice, sports events are recorded from multiple camera angles, providing complementary evidence used by referees. Yet, no existing benchmark evaluates MLLMs on multi-view sports video understanding. To address this gap, we introduce SportMV-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark built from official match recordings, through a dedicated pipeline combining LLM-based generation, MLLM-based verification, and human filtering to ensure quality and consistency. SportMV-Bench containing 787 multi-view video bundles and 2592 question-answer pairs across three categories: Perception-Aware Recognition (PAR), Rule-aware Event Interpretation (REI), and Adjudicative Decision Reasoning(ADR). Our analysis shows that current MLLMs fail to effectively exploit multi-view information, with the bottlenecks lying in fine-grained visual perception and view selection rather than logical reasoning or domain knowledge. We propose SportMV-Agent, an agentic framework that orchestrates an iterative loop of active view selection, perception tool execution, and evidence-grounded reasoning, achieving a significant 14.46% relative improvement over the strongest MLLM baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越单视角：体育视频理解中的代理多视角推理
近期多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在单视角视频理解基准测试中表现优异。然而，体育视频常涉及密集遮挡、快速运动和复杂交互，单视角难以有效解析。实践中，体育赛事从多个摄像机角度录制，为裁判提供互补证据。但现有无任何基准评估MLLMs在多视角体育视频理解上的能力。为填补这一空白，我们引入SportMV-Bench，该基准基于官方比赛录像，通过结合基于LLM的生成、基于MLLM的验证和人工筛选的专用流程，确保质量和一致性。SportMV-Bench包含787个多视角视频包和2592个问答对，涵盖三类任务：感知感知识别（PAR）、规则感知事件解释（REI）和裁决决策推理（ADR）。分析表明，当前MLLMs未能有效利用多视角信息，瓶颈在于细粒度视觉感知和视角选择，而非逻辑推理或领域知识。我们提出SportMV-Agent，一种代理框架，通过主动视角选择、感知工具执行和证据驱动推理的迭代循环，相较最强MLLM基线实现14.46%的相对提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of single-camera video understanding in sports due to occlusion and rapid motion, this work introduces SportMV-Bench, a new benchmark with 787 multi-view video bundles and 2592 QA pairs across three tasks. The authors build the dataset via LLM generation, MLLM verification, and human filtering. Experimental results reveal that current MLLMs struggle with fine-grained visual perception and view selection, not logical reasoning. To address this, they propose SportMV-Agent, an agentic framework that iteratively selects views, executes perception tools, and grounds reasoning in evidence, achieving a 14.46% relative gain over the best MLLM baseline.
鉴于单视角视频在体育场景中难以应对遮挡与快速运动等问题，本文构建了基于官方比赛录像的多视角基准SportMV-Bench，包含787组多视角视频和2592个问答对，覆盖感知识别、规则解释与裁判决策三类任务。实验表明当前多模态大模型在多视角信息利用上表现不佳，主要瓶颈在于细粒度视觉感知与视角选择能力。为此提出SportMV-Agent框架，通过主动视角选择、感知工具执行与证据驱动推理的迭代机制，相较最强基线模型提升14.46%。
Input-Aware Dynamic Backdoor Attack Against Quantum Neural Networks
Authors: Junrui Zhang, Zemin Chen, Lusi Li, Mohammad Ghasemigol, Daniel Takabi, Rui Ning
First: 2026-07-13T17:34:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:34:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 2026 IEEE International Conference on Quantum Computing and Engineering (QCE 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantum Neural Networks (QNNs) are a promising framework for quantum machine learning on near-term quantum devices, but their security risks remain insufficiently understood. Studies have shown that QNNs are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, yet existing quantum backdoors mostly rely on a fixed trigger shared by all poisoned inputs. This fixed-trigger design is a major weakness because many defenses detect or weaken the repeated patterns such triggers leave in data representations. Although input-aware dynamic backdoors have been studied in classical neural networks, transferring them to QNNs is difficult because quantum learning introduces new obstacles. In particular, measurement compresses the post-ansatz quantum state into a limited classical output, weakening supervision for a trigger generator, while individual density matrices fluctuate with the input and make per-sample contrastive learning unstable. To address these challenges, we propose Q-DIBA, the first input-aware dynamic backdoor attack for QNNs. Q-DIBA jointly trains a classical trigger generator and a victim QNN through a three-mode mini-batch strategy that supports clean behavior, attack activation, and trigger specificity. To provide stable quantum-level supervision, Q-DIBA introduces an ensemble density contrastive loss that operates on post-ansatz quantum states before measurement and contrasts mode-averaged density matrices rather than individual samples. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST across multiple QNN architectures show that Q-DIBA achieves high clean accuracy, strong attack success, and high cross-trigger accuracy, demonstrating effectiveness, stealthiness, and input specificity. The attack also remains resilient against defenses including visual inspection, spectral-signature detection, and fine-tuning, suggesting that input-aware quantum backdoors are an important threat to secure QNN deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向量子神经网络的输入感知动态后门攻击
量子神经网络（QNN）是近期内量子设备上量子机器学习的有前景框架，但其安全风险尚未充分理解。研究表明QNN易受后门攻击，但现有量子后门大多依赖所有中毒输入共享的固定触发器。这种固定触发器设计是主要弱点，因为许多防御机制可检测或削弱此类触发器在数据表示中留下的重复模式。尽管输入感知动态后门已在经典神经网络中研究，但将其迁移到QNN困难，因量子学习引入新障碍：测量将后门量子态压缩为有限经典输出，削弱触发器生成器的监督；且单个密度矩阵随输入波动，使样本级对比学习不稳定。为应对这些挑战，我们提出Q-DIBA，首个面向QNN的输入感知动态后门攻击。Q-DIBA通过三模式小批量策略联合训练经典触发器生成器与受害QNN，支持干净行为、攻击激活与触发器特异性。为提供稳定量子级监督，Q-DIBA引入基于后门量子态（测量前）的集成密度对比损失，对比模式平均密度矩阵而非单个样本。在MNIST与Fashion-MNIST上多架构QNN实验表明，Q-DIBA实现高干净准确率、强攻击成功率与高跨触发器准确率，证明其有效性、隐蔽性与输入特异性。该攻击对视觉检查、频谱签名检测及微调等防御机制仍具鲁棒性，表明输入感知量子后门是保障QNN安全部署的重要威胁。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the security vulnerabilities of Quantum Neural Networks (QNNs) and the limitations of existing fixed-trigger backdoor attacks, this work introduces Q-DIBA, the first input-aware dynamic backdoor attack tailored for QNNs. The method jointly trains a classical trigger generator and a victim QNN using a three-mode mini-batch strategy that balances clean behavior, attack activation, and trigger specificity. To overcome quantum-specific challenges like measurement-induced compression and fluctuating density matrices, Q-DIBA employs an ensemble density contrastive loss that operates on post-ansatz quantum states and contrasts mode-averaged density matrices. Experimental results on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST across multiple QNN architectures show Q-DIBA achieves high clean accuracy, strong attack success, and high cross-trigger accuracy, while remaining resilient to common defenses such as visual inspection and spectral-signature detection.
鉴于量子神经网络（QNN）的安全隐患及现有固定触发器后门攻击的局限性，本文提出Q-DIBA，这是首个针对QNN的输入感知动态后门攻击方法。该方法通过三模式小批量策略联合训练经典触发器生成器与受害QNN，以平衡干净行为、攻击激活与触发器特异性。为在测量压缩和密度矩阵波动的量子环境中稳定监督信号，Q-DIBA引入了基于模式平均密度矩阵的集合密度对比损失。在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST多个QNN架构上的实验表明，Q-DIBA在保持高干净准确率的同时，攻击成功率高、跨触发器准确性高，并能抵御视觉检查、频谱检测和微调等防御手段。
Accelerating Sampling-Based Control via Learned Linear Koopman Dynamics
Authors: Wenjian Hao, Yuxuan Fang, Zehui Lu, Shaoshuai Mou
First: 2026-03-05T17:15:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:30:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents an efficient model predictive path integral (MPPI) control framework for systems with complex nonlinear dynamics. To improve the computational efficiency of classic MPPI while preserving control performance, we replace the nonlinear dynamics used for trajectory propagation with a learned linear deep Koopman operator (DKO) model, enabling faster rollout and more efficient trajectory sampling. The DKO dynamics are learned directly from interaction data, eliminating the need for analytical system models. The resulting controller, termed MPPI-DK, is evaluated in simulation on pendulum balancing and surface vehicle navigation tasks, and validated on hardware through reference-tracking experiments on a quadruped robot. Experimental results demonstrate that MPPI-DK achieves control performance close to MPPI with true dynamics while substantially reducing computational cost, enabling efficient real-time control on robotic platforms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过学习线性Koopman动力学加速采样控制
本文提出了一种针对复杂非线性系统的高效模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制框架。为在保持控制性能的同时提升经典MPPI的计算效率，我们用从交互数据中学习到的线性深度Koopman算子（DKO）模型替代轨迹传播中的非线性动力学，从而实现更快的 rollout 和更高效的轨迹采样。DKO动力学直接从交互数据中学习，无需分析系统模型。所提出的控制器称为MPPI-DK，在摆平衡和水面车辆导航的仿真任务中进行了评估，并通过四足机器人参考跟踪实验在硬件上验证。实验结果表明，MPPI-DK在保持接近真实动力学MPPI控制性能的同时，显著降低了计算成本，实现了机器人平台上的高效实时控制。
Summary / 总结

Experiments on pendulum balancing, surface vehicle navigation, and a quadruped robot show that MPPI-DK matches MPPI’s control performance while reducing computational burden significantly, making real-time deployment on hardware feasible.

LoRA-Based Cascaded Multimodal Fusion for Action Recognition in Medical Training Environments
Authors: Divya Mereddy, Jeevan Beedareddy
First: 2026-07-13T17:27:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:27:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper presents a cascaded Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA)-based multimodal fusion framework for action and activity recognition in healthcare-oriented training environments. The proposed architecture combines parameter-efficient modality-specific adaptation with sequential fusion, enabling modalities to be integrated in stages without retraining previously learned components. Rather than assuming a fixed fusion structure, the framework first integrates more closely related modalities and then incorporates additional heterogeneous modalities, supporting scalable adaptation across datasets with different modality sets.We evaluate the framework on two healthcare-oriented training environment datasets: NurViD and the Nurse Training dataset. Across these datasets, preliminary results suggest that the proposed cascaded fusion strategy improves over individual modality models and provides competitive performance relative to previously reported dataset-specific baselines. Overall, these findings indicate that cascaded LoRA-based fusion is a promising parameter-efficient approach for integrating heterogeneous modalities in medical training action and activity recognition tasks. github: https://github.com/anonymous0-ai/LoRA-Based-Cascaded-Multimodal-Fusion-.git.

Motivated by the need for efficient and scalable multimodal fusion in medical training environments, this work proposes a cascaded LoRA-based framework that adaptively integrates heterogeneous modalities in stages without retraining prior components. The method first fuses closely related modalities and progressively adds more diverse ones, enabling parameter-efficient adaptation across datasets with varying modalities. HASTE: A Platform for Rapid Post-Disaster Building Damage Assessment
Authors: Caleb Robinson, Anthony Ortiz, Simone Fobi Nsutezo, Cameron Birge, Meygha Machado, Marcelo Duarte, Joaquin Rivero Rodriguez, Anthony Cintron Roman, Kevin White, Inbal Becker-Reshef, Juan M. Lavista Ferres
First: 2026-07-13T17:27:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:27:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
When a large disaster strikes, responders need a map of which buildings are damaged within hours. The models that do well on public benchmarks assume matched before-and-after imagery and a training set drawn from similar past events, and neither is usually available for a new disaster in its first day. We present HASTE (High-speed Assessment and Satellite Tracking for Emergencies), a no-code web platform that lets analysts who are not machine learning engineers produce per-building damage maps from post-disaster satellite imagery. HASTE implements two methods that share one interface. The first requires the user to label polygons over the post-disaster scene, trains a small semantic segmentation model on that single scene, runs it over the whole image, and joins the per-pixel output to existing building footprints. The second embeds every footprint with a pretrained vision model, requires the user to label a handful of buildings, and fits a logistic regression in the browser that scores the rest of the scene in seconds. We describe the platform, both methods, and the engineering that supports them. We also report preliminary experiments on xBD showing that foundation-model embeddings pooled over footprints separate damaged from intact buildings using post-disaster imagery alone, matching a fully supervised ResNet-50 baseline with a twentieth of its labels. HASTE and its predecessors have supported more than thirty real-world disaster responses since 2023, spanning earthquakes, hurricanes, cyclones, floods, wildfires, and tornadoes, delivering results to humanitarian partners within hours to days of imagery becoming available. We close with the directions we think are most promising, including vision-language assessment, active learning, and damage models for roads and other infrastructure. HASTE is open source at https://github.com/microsoft/haste.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HASTE：用于灾后建筑物快速损伤评估的平台
当重大灾害发生时，救援人员需要在数小时内获得受损建筑物的地图。在公共基准测试中表现良好的模型通常假设存在匹配的灾前灾后图像，并且训练集来自类似的历史事件，但在新灾害发生的第一天，这些条件通常无法满足。我们提出了HASTE（紧急事件高速评估与卫星追踪），这是一个无需代码的网页平台，使非机器学习工程师的分析师能够从灾后卫星图像中生成每栋建筑物的损伤地图。HASTE 实现了两种方法，共享同一界面。第一种方法要求用户在灾后场景上标注多边形，基于该单一场景训练一个小型语义分割模型，然后在整幅图像上运行该模型，并将每个像素的输出与现有建筑物轮廓进行关联。第二种方法将每个建筑物轮廓嵌入到一个预训练的视觉模型中，要求用户标注少量建筑物，然后在浏览器中拟合一个逻辑回归模型，数秒内即可对场景其余部分进行评分。我们描述了该平台、两种方法及其支持的工程实现。此外，我们在xBD数据集上的初步实验表明，仅使用灾后图像，通过在建筑物轮廓上聚合基础模型嵌入，即可将受损建筑物与完好建筑物区分开，其性能与完全监督的ResNet-50基线相当，但仅需其标签的五分之一。自2023年以来，HASTE及其前身已支持了超过三十次真实世界的灾害响应，涵盖地震、飓风、气旋、洪水、野火和龙卷风，能够在图像可用后的数小时至数天内向人道主义伙伴交付结果。最后，我们提出了我们认为最有前景的研究方向，包括视觉-语言评估、主动学习以及道路和其他基础设施的损伤模型。HASTE 开源代码位于 https://github.com/microsoft/haste。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the urgent need for rapid building damage mapping after disasters when labeled training data and matched imagery are unavailable, HASTE offers a no-code web platform enabling non-experts to generate per-building damage assessments from post-disaster satellite images. It employs two methods: one trains a lightweight semantic segmentation model on user-labeled polygons within a single scene, then overlays results onto building footprints; the other uses pretrained vision model embeddings of footprints, labels a few buildings, and fits a browser-based logistic regression to score the rest quickly. Experiments on xBD data show that foundation-model embeddings alone can distinguish damaged from intact buildings using only post-disaster imagery, achieving performance comparable to a fully supervised ResNet-50 model with just 1/20th the labels.
为满足灾害发生后数小时内快速生成建筑损毁地图的需求，HASTE 提供了一个无需代码的网页平台，使非机器学习专家能基于灾后卫星影像生成逐栋建筑损毁评估。平台提供两种方法：一种是用户标注灾后影像中的多边形区域，训练轻量级语义分割模型并应用于整幅图像，再与现有建筑轮廓叠加；另一种是将建筑轮廓嵌入预训练视觉模型，仅需标注少量建筑，即可在浏览器中训练逻辑回归模型，数秒内评估其余区域。在xBD数据上的实验表明，仅使用灾后影像的嵌入式模型性能可媲美全监督ResNet-50模型，但仅需其标签量的五分之一。自2023年以来，HASTE 已支持超过三十次真实灾害响应，涵盖地震、飓风、洪水等，成果在影像可用后数小时至数日内交付给人道主义伙伴。
FIERO: Empowering Creative Writing Through Collaborative Game Play
Authors: Chu Zhang, XiaoKe Zeng, Jin Zhang, Ruoyu Wen, Vince Siu, Richard William Allen, Ray LC
First: 2026-07-13T17:27:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:27:30+00:00
Comments: 51pages, 14 figures, 5 tables, will be presenting this paper at CHIPLAY 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Creativity often flourishes in collaboration, such as when designers brainstorm a new app together, or storytellers collectively build a world with elements of each person's narrative. However, collaborative storytelling can have challenges for its participants, such as when they disagree about the plot proposed, or when different ideas become fragmented when voiced individually. While current tools for creative collaboration focus on synchronous online text sharing, they often neglect the social dynamics of in-person collaboration critical to creative synergy. To address this, we created FIERO, a multiplayer web-based card game. Physical cards provide tangible scaffolding and social interaction, while the digital interface generates contextual visuals, facilitate group decisions, ensure narrative coherence, and synthesize different idea contributions using generative AI. Compared against online collaborative writing alone, the game significantly enhanced intuitive stimulation, idea fluency, and novelty generation, and also improved the content of the stories produced, leading to greater plot coherence (N=60). The cards provided creative structure and social engagement, while the interface provided contextualized augmentation without affecting player agency. This work shows how collaborative play can be utilized to foster creative support.
