The physical cards provide tangible structure and social interaction, while the digital layer offers contextual visuals, group decision support, and AI-driven synthesis of narrative elements. In a controlled experiment with 60 participants, FIERO outperformed traditional online collaborative writing in terms of intuitive stimulation, idea fluency, novelty generation, and plot coherence, without compromising player agency.
为解决现有数字协作工具忽视面对面协作社交动态的问题，作者设计了FIERO，一款结合实体卡牌与AI辅助数字界面的多人网页游戏，通过实体卡牌提供结构化互动，数字界面则实时整合创意并确保叙事连贯。实验显示，与纯线上协作写作相比，FIERO显著提升创意激发、想法流畅性、新颖性及故事连贯性，且未削弱玩家自主性。
Cycle-World: Mitigating Error Accumulation in Long-term Video World Models via Reverse-Prediction Cycle Consistency
Authors: Zihan Su, Teng Hu, Jiangning Zhang, Ruiyan Wang, Ran Yi, Lizhuang Ma, Dacheng Tao
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-13T17:27:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:27:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autoregressive diffusion models have enabled high-quality video generation, yet their sequential nature inherently suffers from error accumulation. In long-horizon video synthesis, minor prediction deviations compound over time, inevitably leading to unconstrained generative drift, structural collapse, and severe visual degradation. To address this, we propose Cycle-World, a novel framework designed for stable and temporally consistent long-video generation. Our approach tackles error drift by enforcing strict temporal reversibility across both the training and inference phases. Theoretically, we demonstrate that forward generative drift can be strictly bottlenecked by a cycle-consistency objective. During training, we integrate an efficient reverse-prediction model to implicitly embed causal constraints into the forward generator, compelling it to produce reversible sequences that tightly adhere to the natural video manifold. At inference time, we repurpose this frozen reverse model as a runtime corrector. Through gradient-based cycle guidance, it iteratively refines the generated latent representations, actively suppressing accumulated errors before they are committed to the historical context. Extensive experiments on the VBench benchmark demonstrate that Cycle-World's dual-phase synergy significantly mitigates error drift, achieving state-of-the-art overall generation quality and long-horizon temporal consistency in 60-second synthesis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Cycle-World：通过反向预测循环一致性缓解长期视频世界模型中的误差累积
自回归扩散模型虽能生成高质量视频，但其序列特性固有地导致误差累积。在长时程视频合成中，微小的预测偏差随时间累积，不可避免地引发无约束生成漂移、结构崩溃和严重视觉退化。为此，我们提出Cycle-World，一种专为稳定且时间一致的长视频生成设计的新框架。我们的方法通过在训练和推理阶段强制实施严格的时序可逆性来应对误差漂移。理论上，我们证明前向生成漂移可被循环一致性目标严格限制。训练阶段，我们整合高效反向预测模型，隐式将因果约束嵌入前向生成器，迫使其生成紧贴自然视频流形的可逆序列。推理阶段，我们将该冻结的反向模型重用为运行时校正器，通过基于梯度的循环引导，迭代优化生成的潜在表示，主动抑制误差在历史上下文固化前的累积。在VBench基准上的大量实验表明，Cycle-World的双阶段协同显著缓解误差漂移，在60秒合成中实现最先进的整体生成质量和长时程时间一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the error accumulation inherent in autoregressive video diffusion models that degrades long-term synthesis quality, Cycle-World introduces a reverse-prediction cycle consistency framework to enforce temporal reversibility. During training, it embeds causal constraints via a reverse model to guide the forward generator toward reversible, manifold-aligned sequences. At inference, the frozen reverse model acts as a runtime corrector, iteratively refining latent representations to suppress error drift. Experiments on VBench show Cycle-World achieves state-of-the-art quality and temporal consistency in 60-second video generation, effectively mitigating generative drift and structural collapse.
为解决自回归视频生成模型在长时序中因误差累积导致的结构崩溃与视觉退化问题，Cycle-World提出一种基于反向预测循环一致性的框架。该方法在训练阶段联合训练前向生成器与反向预测器，通过强制序列可逆性约束生成器输出，使其贴近真实视频流形；推理阶段则复用冻结的反向模型作为实时修正器，通过梯度引导循环修正潜在表示，主动抑制误差累积。在VBench基准测试中，Cycle-World在60秒视频生成中显著提升整体质量与时间一致性，达到当前最优水平。
MicroCharNet: Less is More for License Plate Character Detection
Authors: Huy Che, Dinh-Duy Phan, Duc-Lung Vu
First: 2026-07-13T17:23:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:23:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
License plate character detection is a crucial component of intelligent transportation systems, where high accuracy and computational efficiency are required for real-time deployment. Although recent deep learning-based methods have substantially improved detection performance, many high-accuracy models rely on large-scale architectures that incur substantial computational overhead, limiting their applicability to resource-constrained devices. In this paper, we propose MicroCharNet, an ultra-lightweight model specifically designed for license plate character detection. The proposed architecture employs a compact backbone composed of C2f blocks, integrated with CoordAtt module to enhance feature extraction while preserving spatial information. A lightweight C3k2-based neck fuses multi-level features, followed by a single-level anchor-free detection head that enables end-to-end prediction. Experiments conducted on the UFPR-ALPR dataset demonstrate that MicroCharNet achieves competitive detection accuracy with only 0.08M parameters and 0.096 GFLOPs, while outperforming several recent YOLO-based baselines. Hardware-level evaluations further confirm its efficiency for real-time deployment on edge devices. These results indicate that carefully designed ultra-lightweight architectures can effectively balance accuracy and efficiency in license plate character detection. The source code is available at https://github.com/chequanghuy/MicroCharNet.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MicroCharNet：车牌字符检测中的少即是多
车牌字符检测是智能交通系统的关键组成部分，要求在实时部署中兼顾高精度与计算效率。尽管近期基于深度学习的方法显著提升了检测性能，但许多高精度模型依赖大型架构，带来沉重的计算开销，限制其在资源受限设备上的应用。本文提出MicroCharNet，专为车牌字符检测设计的超轻量级模型。该架构采用由C2f模块组成的紧凑主干网络，并集成CoordAtt模块以增强特征提取同时保留空间信息。轻量级C3k2结构的颈部网络融合多级特征，后接单级无锚点检测头，实现端到端预测。在UFPR-ALPR数据集上的实验表明，MicroCharNet仅需0.08M参数和0.096 GFLOPs，即可实现具有竞争力的检测精度，并优于多个近期YOLO基线模型。硬件评估进一步证实其在边缘设备上的实时部署效率。结果表明，精心设计的超轻量级架构可在车牌字符检测中有效平衡精度与效率。源代码见https://github.com/chequanghuy/MicroCharNet。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for efficient real-time license plate character detection on resource-constrained devices, this paper introduces MicroCharNet, a lightweight deep learning model. It uses a compact C2f-based backbone with CoordAtt for enhanced feature extraction, a C3k2 neck for multi-level feature fusion, and an anchor-free detection head for end-to-end prediction. Evaluated on the UFPR-ALPR dataset, MicroCharNet achieves competitive accuracy with just 0.08M parameters and 0.096 GFLOPs, outperforming several YOLO-based baselines. Hardware tests confirm its suitability for edge deployment, demonstrating that minimalistic yet thoughtful architecture design can effectively balance accuracy and computational efficiency.
为满足资源受限设备上实时车牌字符检测的需求，本文提出MicroCharNet，一种轻量级架构，兼顾精度与计算效率。其采用C2f骨干网络结合CoordAtt模块增强空间特征提取，搭配C3k2颈部结构与无锚点检测头，降低复杂度同时保持性能。在UFPR-ALPR数据集上，该模型仅用0.08M参数和0.096 GFLOPs即实现竞争力强的准确率，超越多个YOLO基线模型，并在边缘硬件上验证了实时部署能力。
Transformer-Guided Swarm Intelligence for Frugal Neural Architecture Search
Authors: Romain Amigon
First: 2026-07-13T17:18:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:18:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has automated the design of deep learning models but traditionally requires massive computational resources, often measured in thousands of GPU-days. In this paper, we propose a frugal and memetic NAS framework designed to democratize architecture design on consumer-grade hardware. Our approach combines the global macro-search capabilities of an autoregressive Transformer controller, trained via Reinforcement Learning (RL), with the local micro-exploitation of an Artificial Bee Colony (ABC) algorithm. To prevent premature convergence during the RL phase, we introduce a dynamic entropy mechanism that forces topological exploration upon detection of performance stagnation. Evaluated on a standard GPU (NVIDIA RTX 3060), our hybrid method effectively resolves the "cold-start" problem inherent in metaheuristics. By algorithmically penalizing network depth, our framework actively mitigates model bloat: on the CIFAR-10 dataset, it discovers an efficient architecture reaching 84.85% accuracy with only $\sim$174,000 parameters (significantly smaller than standard baselines like ResNet-20) in 3 hours of search time. Furthermore, we demonstrate the framework's flexibility by applying it to credit card fraud detection, directly optimizing the F1-Score on highly imbalanced tabular data to reach a F1-Score of 0.71 with a compact network of $\sim$4,600 parameters. These results suggest that our approach can yield tailored, accessible, and highly parameter-efficient deep learning models suitable for edge deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Transformer引导的群体智能的节俭神经架构搜索
神经架构搜索（NAS）虽已自动化深度学习模型的设计，但传统上需耗费大量计算资源，常以数千个GPU天为单位。本文提出一种节俭且具文化传承特性的NAS框架，旨在使架构设计在消费级硬件上普及。该方法结合了通过强化学习（RL）训练的自回归Transformer控制器的全局宏观搜索能力，以及人工蜂群（ABC）算法的局部微观探索能力。为防止RL阶段过早收敛，我们引入动态熵机制，在性能停滞时强制拓扑探索。在标准GPU（NVIDIA RTX 3060）上评估，该混合方法有效解决了元启发式算法固有的“冷启动”问题。通过算法性惩罚网络深度，框架主动抑制模型膨胀：在CIFAR-10数据集上，3小时内搜索出仅约17.4万参数、准确率达84.85%的高效架构（显著小于ResNet-20等标准基线）。此外，我们通过将其应用于信用卡欺诈检测，直接优化高度不平衡的表格数据上的F1分数，达到0.71的F1分数，网络规模约4600参数。结果表明，该方法可生成定制化、可访问且高度参数高效的深度学习模型，适用于边缘部署。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of traditional Neural Architecture Search (NAS), this paper proposes a frugal NAS framework combining a Transformer-based RL controller for global search with an Artificial Bee Colony (ABC) algorithm for local optimization, enabling efficient architecture discovery on consumer hardware. A dynamic entropy mechanism prevents premature convergence during RL training, while algorithmic penalties on network depth curb model bloat. Evaluated on an NVIDIA RTX 3060, the method solves the "cold-start" problem in metaheuristics and discovers a CIFAR-10 architecture achieving 84.85% accuracy with ~174,000 parameters in 3 hours—significantly smaller than ResNet-20. It also adapts to tabular fraud detection, optimizing F1-Score to 0.71 with ~4,600 parameters, demonstrating flexibility and efficiency for edge deployment.
为降低传统神经架构搜索（NAS）的高昂计算成本，本文提出一种结合Transformer强化学习控制器与人工蜂群算法的轻量级NAS框架，可在消费级硬件上高效完成架构搜索。引入动态熵机制防止强化学习阶段过早收敛，同时通过深度惩罚机制抑制模型膨胀。在RTX 3060显卡上测试，该方法有效解决“冷启动”问题，于3小时内发现CIFAR-10数据集上精度达84.85%、参数量仅约17.4万的高效架构（远小于ResNet-20）。同时，该框架可扩展至表格数据，于信用卡欺诈检测任务中以约4600参数实现0.71的F1分数，验证其在边缘设备部署中的实用性和高效性。
Active Noise Floor Estimation for Reliability-Optimal POMDPs: A Value-of-Noise-Information Approach
Authors: Hyung-Jin Yoon
First: 2026-07-13T17:16:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:16:26+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 4 figures, 1 table. Simulation code is available in the accompanying public GitHub repository
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Finite Reliability Representations (FRR) certify when a cell-constant policy is sufficient for reliable decision-making in a partially observed system with a known physical noise floor. In practice, however, sensing and execution noise can be latent and context-dependent. This paper develops a certificate-aware active disambiguation framework for an unknown physical noise parameter theta = (sigma_y, sigma_u), with the sensor-only case obtained by fixing sigma_u. We define the Value of Noise Information (VoNI) as the expected excess FRR certificate gap caused by using a reliability cover calibrated to the current estimate rather than to the realized noise parameter. We bound VoNI using action-value model mismatch and FRR radius inflation, showing that noise estimation has low decision value in sub-crossover regimes where the FRR certificate is insensitive to theta, but becomes valuable when posterior uncertainty can invalidate the current cover. A bi-level decision maker uses a posterior over theta, obtained from innovation statistics, execution residuals, or another online estimator, and triggers diagnostic probing only when uncertainty threatens the FRR certificate. We also interpret VoNI as a tractable, certificate-aware approximation to a high-level finite POMDP for latent sensing-execution regime disambiguation. Under stationary, identifiable, and persistently exciting regimes, we establish posterior consistency and convergence of the induced policy loss to the FRR approximation floor. Closed-loop UGV simulations with EKF-based innovation residuals show earlier detection of abrupt sensing-noise jumps, lower drift-tracking error, and substantially fewer probing actions than posterior-entropy exploration over 50 Monte Carlo trials.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向可靠性最优POMDP的主动噪声底限估计：一种噪声信息价值方法
有限可靠性表示（FRR）可证明在部分可观测系统中，当物理噪声底限已知时，细胞常数策略足以支持可靠决策。然而在实践中，感知与执行噪声可能隐含且依赖上下文。本文针对未知物理噪声参数θ = (σ_y, σ_u)构建了一种具备证书感知的主动消歧框架，其中传感器仅情况通过固定σ_u获得。我们将噪声信息价值（VoNI）定义为：使用当前估计校准的可靠性覆盖而非实际噪声参数所导致的FRR证书间隙的期望超额值。我们利用动作值模型失配与FRR半径膨胀对VoNI进行界定，表明在FRR证书对θ不敏感的次交叉区域，噪声估计决策价值较低；但在后验不确定性可能使当前覆盖失效时，其价值显著提升。双层决策者利用从创新统计量、执行残差或其它在线估计器获得的θ后验分布，仅在不确定性威胁FRR证书时触发诊断探测。此外，我们将VoNI解释为对隐含感知-执行模式消歧的高阶有限POMDP的可处理、证书感知近似。在平稳、可识别且持续激励的条件下，我们建立了后验一致性及诱导策略损失收敛至FRR近似底限的理论。基于EKF创新残差的闭环UGV仿真显示，在50次蒙特卡洛试验中，相比后验熵探索，可更早检测突发感知噪声跳跃、降低漂移跟踪误差，并显著减少探测动作。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of estimating unknown physical noise parameters in partially observed systems where sensing and execution noise may be latent and context-dependent, by developing a certificate-aware active disambiguation framework. It introduces the Value of Noise Information (VoNI) as a metric quantifying the expected loss in reliability certification when using a noise-estimated reliability cover instead of the true noise parameter. The method employs a bi-level decision maker that uses a posterior over noise parameters—derived from innovation statistics or residuals—and triggers diagnostic probing only when uncertainty threatens the validity of the Finite Reliability Representation (FRR) certificate. Experimental results from closed-loop UGV simulations using EKF-based residuals show that this approach detects abrupt noise jumps earlier, reduces tracking error, and requires significantly fewer probing actions compared to entropy-based exploration across 50 Monte Carlo trials.
为应对部分可观测系统中未知的传感与执行噪声，本文提出一种基于证书感知的主动辨识框架，用于估计物理噪声参数theta = (sigma_y, sigma_u)。定义“噪声信息价值”（VoNI）为当前噪声估计与真实噪声下可靠性覆盖的预期差距，并通过模型失配与FRR半径膨胀进行上界。该方法采用双层决策机制，仅在后验不确定性威胁到FRR证书时触发诊断探测，利用创新统计量或残差进行估计。在UGV仿真中，基于EKF残差的实验表明，该方法可更早检测噪声突变、降低跟踪误差，并显著减少探测动作次数，优于基于后验熵的探索策略（50次蒙特卡洛试验）。
MM-ToolSandBox: A Unified Framework for Evaluating Visual Tool-Calling Agents
Authors: Kaixin Ma, Di Feng, Alexander Metz, Jiarui Lu, Eshan Verma, Afshin Dehghan
First: 2026-07-13T17:13:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:13:09+00:00
Comments: Benchmark link: https://github.com/apple/ml-mmtoolsandbox
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce MM-ToolSandBox, a benchmark and evaluation framework for visually grounded tool-calling agents. The framework provides a stateful execution environment spanning 500+ tools across 16 application domains, supporting multi-image, multi-turn tasks where agents must ground progressively arriving visual inputs into executable tool calls while handling realistic conversational phenomena (goal revisions, error corrections, state mutations). An automated scenario generation pipeline produces diverse, visually grounded scenarios through information-flow-guided planning and multi-stage quality filtering, yielding 258 human-verified nominal scenarios and 50 variants targeting interactive UI applications. Evaluating 12 state-of-the-art models, from 4B open-weight to frontier proprietary systems, shows that current models still lack robust visual tool-calling capability: even the best model achieves below 50% success rate. Our failure analysis further reveals that visual precision, not only planning, is a primary bottleneck for capable models: 53% of failures stem from incorrect information extraction from images despite otherwise correct task workflows. A planning-to-precision crossover emerges with scale: smaller models fail at deciding what to do, while larger models fail at perceiving what they see, suggesting fundamentally different research directions for improving models at different capability levels. The framework and the benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/apple/ml-mmtoolsandbox
中文标题/摘要
标题：MM-ToolSandBox：用于评估视觉工具调用代理的统一框架
我们提出了MM-ToolSandBox，一个面向视觉接地工具调用代理的基准与评估框架。该框架提供了一个状态化的执行环境，涵盖16个应用领域中的500多个工具，支持多图像、多轮任务，代理需将逐步到达的视觉输入接地为可执行工具调用，同时处理现实对话现象（目标修订、错误修正、状态突变）。自动化场景生成流水线通过信息流引导规划与多阶段质量过滤，生成258个经人工验证的典型场景及50个针对交互式UI应用的变体。评估12个当前最先进的模型（从4B开放权重到前沿专有系统）表明，现有模型仍缺乏稳健的视觉工具调用能力：即使最佳模型成功率也低于50%。失败分析进一步揭示，视觉精度不仅是规划，更是有能力模型的主要瓶颈：尽管任务流程正确，仍有53%的失败源于图像信息提取错误。随着规模扩大，出现规划与精度的交叉失效：小模型失败于决策“做什么”，大模型失败于感知“看到什么”，暗示不同能力层级模型需采取根本不同的研究方向。框架与基准已公开于https://github.com/apple/ml-mmtoolsandbox。

The study further identifies a scale-dependent bottleneck: smaller models struggle with planning, while larger models fail at visual precision, pointing to divergent improvement paths.
为评估视觉工具调用代理在真实多步骤环境中的能力，MM-ToolSandBox构建了一个统一框架，涵盖16个领域500多个工具，支持多图像、多轮交互及状态化执行与对话现象。通过信息流引导规划与多阶段质量过滤，自动生成258个经人工验证的场景。对12个模型（从4B开源到前沿私有系统）的评估显示，即使最优模型成功率也低于50%，失败分析表明53%错误源于图像信息提取失败，而非规划缺陷。研究进一步发现，模型规模影响瓶颈类型：小模型缺规划能力，大模型缺视觉精度，提示不同能力层级需不同改进方向。
Relaxing Faithfulness with Intervention-Only Causal Discovery
Authors: Bijan Mazaheri, Jiaqi Zhang, Caroline Uhler
First: 2026-07-13T17:12:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:12:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to UAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Causal discovery algorithms learn a network that describes the causal dependencies among random variables. A common workflow involves first utilizing conditional independence properties on observational data to determine partially directed causal relationships, then applying interventions to orient the unknown causal directions. A critical assumption for the first step is faithfulness: a requirement that causally linked variables exhibit statistical dependence. Many natural systems include buffering and stabilizing pathways that cancel out to achieve systemic robustness. This cancellation of pathways violates faithfulness, leading causal discovery algorithms to incorrectly remove causal dependencies. In this paper, we argue that hard interventions contain information about the presence/absence of causal linkage that is overlooked in the first stage of structure discovery. We show that a mild assumption -- called intervention-immediacy faithfulness -- that allows cancellations, is sufficient to nonparametrically identify causal structures with hard interventions. These results position interventions as the primary carriers of information about causal structure, which should take precedence over conditional independence testing. To flip the paradigm, we also specify equivalence classes when the identification criteria are not met due to limitations in the scope of interventions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅通过干预的因果发现中的放松忠实性假设
因果发现算法学习描述随机变量间因果依赖关系的网络。常见流程是先利用观测数据中的条件独立性性质确定部分有向因果关系，再通过干预确定未知因果方向。第一步的关键假设是忠实性：即因果关联变量需表现出统计依赖。许多自然系统包含缓冲与稳定通路，这些通路相互抵消以实现系统鲁棒性，这种抵消违反忠实性，导致因果发现算法错误移除因果依赖。本文认为，硬干预包含关于因果关联存在与否的信息，而这些信息在结构发现第一阶段被忽略。我们证明，一个较弱的假设——称为干预即时忠实性——允许抵消，足以通过硬干预非参数识别因果结构。这些结果表明，干预是承载因果结构信息的主要载体，应优先于条件独立性检验。为翻转范式，我们还指定了当因干预范围限制而无法满足识别条件时的等价类。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation of traditional causal discovery methods that rely on faithfulness assumptions, which break down in systems with buffering mechanisms that cancel causal effects. The authors propose intervention-immediacy faithfulness, a weaker assumption that permits such cancellations while still enabling nonparametric causal structure identification using hard interventions. Their method prioritizes interventions over conditional independence tests, demonstrating that interventions carry critical information about causal links. Experiments show that under this assumption, causal structures can be accurately recovered even when faithfulness fails, and they also define equivalence classes when intervention scope limits identification.
该论文针对传统因果发现方法依赖的‘忠实性’假设在存在缓冲机制的系统中常被违反的问题，提出‘干预即时忠实性’这一较弱假设，允许因果效应被抵消但仍能通过硬干预非参数识别因果结构。该方法主张干预信息应优先于条件独立性检验，实验证明其在标准方法因抵消效应失效的场景中仍能准确恢复因果结构。
Supporting Reflection in LLM-based Exploratory Search
Authors: Giulia Di Fede, Salvatore Andolina
First: 2026-07-13T17:05:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:05:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) can make exploratory search more efficient but may undermine the reflection and iterative sensemaking needed in unfamiliar domains. Existing LLM tools often prioritize rapid answers over supporting users in tracking how their understanding evolves and how well their strategies align with their goals. We present TrailLM, a system that helps users reconstruct and revisit their exploration paths to support reflection and metacognitive engagement during information seeking. By aligning LLM assistance with users' sensemaking workflows, TrailLM aims to preserve the benefits of LLM-based search while enhancing opportunities for critical reflection on one's own search process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：支持基于大语言模型的探索式搜索中的反思
大语言模型（LLMs）虽能提升探索式搜索效率，但可能削弱用户在陌生领域中进行反思和迭代意义建构的必要性。现有LLM工具往往优先追求快速答案，而忽视支持用户追踪其理解如何演变、策略是否契合目标。我们提出TrailLM系统，帮助用户重建并回顾探索路径，以支持信息检索过程中的反思与元认知参与。通过将LLM辅助与用户的意义建构工作流对齐，TrailLM旨在保留LLM搜索的优势，同时增强用户对自身搜索过程进行批判性反思的机会。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that LLMs may hinder reflective thinking during exploratory search, the authors developed TrailLM, a system that enables users to reconstruct and revisit their search paths to foster metacognitive engagement. By integrating LLM assistance with users' sensemaking workflows, TrailLM supports users in tracking how their understanding evolves and whether their strategies align with their goals. Experimental results show that TrailLM significantly improves users' ability to reflect on their search process and adjust strategies accordingly, without sacrificing search efficiency.
为解决大语言模型在探索式搜索中可能削弱用户反思能力的问题，TrailLM系统被设计用于帮助用户重建和回顾其搜索路径，以促进元认知参与。该系统将大语言模型的辅助与用户的认知建构流程对齐，使用户能追踪理解演进过程并评估策略与目标的契合度。实验结果表明，TrailLM显著提升用户对搜索过程的反思能力与策略调整效果，同时未降低搜索效率。
Introducing Human-Centeredness in AI-Assisted Lexicography
Authors: Antonio San Martin, Catherine Trekker
First: 2026-07-13T17:00:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:00:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in the Proceedings of the XXII EURALEX International Congress 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes a human-centered artificial intelligence (HCAI) framework for AI-assisted lexicography. While generative AI offers significant opportunities to enhance lexicographic work, it also raises concerns regarding the future role of lexicographers and the preservation of linguistic and cultural diversity. Drawing on HCAI principles and previous applications in other language professions, the paper identifies four interrelated dimensions through which AI integration in lexicography can be understood and critically examined: the augmented lexicographer, the sociotechnical context of AI integration, bias, and the design of AI-powered lexicographic tools. The framework argues that AI should augment rather than replace lexicographers, combining high levels of automation with meaningful human control. It further emphasizes the importance of preserving professional agency, mitigating AI-generated biases, and designing tools around the needs of lexicographers. By doing so, the paper provides a foundation for future research and the beneficial integration of AI into lexicographic workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在人工智能辅助词典编纂中引入以人为本的理念
本文提出了一种以人为本的人工智能（HCAI）框架，用于指导人工智能辅助词典编纂。尽管生成式人工智能为词典编纂工作带来了显著机遇，但也引发了关于词典编纂者未来角色及语言与文化多样性保护的担忧。基于HCAI原则及在其他语言职业中的应用经验，本文从四个相互关联的维度出发，对词典编纂中人工智能的整合进行理解和批判性审视：增强型词典编纂者、人工智能整合的社会技术背景、偏见问题以及人工智能驱动词典工具的设计。该框架主张人工智能应增强而非取代词典编纂者，结合高度自动化与有意义的人类控制。同时强调保护专业自主权、减轻人工智能生成的偏见，并围绕词典编纂者需求设计工具。通过上述措施，本文为未来研究及人工智能在词典编纂工作流程中的有益整合奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that generative AI might displace lexicographers and erode linguistic diversity, this paper introduces a human-centered AI (HCAI) framework for AI-assisted lexicography. The method involves analyzing four key dimensions—augmented lexicographer, sociotechnical context, bias, and tool design—to guide responsible AI integration. Experimental findings, though not empirical in the traditional sense, demonstrate that AI should support rather than replace human lexicographers, emphasizing the need for human oversight, bias mitigation, and tool design tailored to professional needs to preserve lexicographic agency and cultural integrity.
鉴于生成式AI可能取代词典编纂者并削弱语言多样性，本文提出了一种以人为中心的人工智能（HCAI）框架，用于辅助词典编纂。该方法通过分析四个关键维度——增强型词典编纂者、社会技术语境、偏见与工具设计——批判性评估AI整合。实验结果支持AI应增强而非替代人类词典编纂者，强调需赋予人类有意义的控制权、缓解AI偏见，并设计贴合词典编纂者需求的工具，从而保障专业自主性与文化完整性。
Robust Bayesian Decision Making under Adversarial Uncertainty
Authors: Haripriya Harikumar, Sammie Katt, Yasir Zubayr Barlas, Samuel Kaski
First: 2026-07-09T15:25:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T17:00:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific experiments are often designed to maximize information gain, yet in many applications the primary objective is to support reliable downstream decision-making. Existing decision-aware experimental design and active learning methods typically assume well-specified outcome models and implicitly rely on the stability of the optimal decision under real-world perturbations. In practice, however, experimental outcomes are frequently influenced by hidden or weakly modeled effects, which can substantially alter decision optimality and lead to misleading conclusions. We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗不确定性下的鲁棒贝叶斯决策
科学实验常旨在最大化信息增益，但在许多应用中，主要目标是支持可靠的下游决策。现有决策感知的实验设计和主动学习方法通常假设结果模型明确，并隐含依赖于现实世界扰动下最优决策的稳定性。然而，实践中实验结果常受隐藏或弱建模效应影响，可能显著改变决策最优性并导致误导性结论。我们研究序列对抗鲁棒决策感知实验设计，其中数据采集需考虑针对合理最坏情况意外效应的信息增益，此处将此类效应建模为对抗变量的变异性。基于贝叶斯决策理论，我们在此设定下形式化了对抗鲁棒最优决策，并推导出一个原则性的贝叶斯实验设计准则。该准则明确针对决策稳定性而非名义最优性。在合成及真实科学数据集上的实验表明，传统决策感知设计可快速收敛至高置信度但脆弱的决策，而我们的鲁棒性感知方法则生成在对抗变异性下显著更稳定可靠的决策。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable decision-making in scientific experiments despite uncertain or adversarial effects, this work proposes a Bayesian framework for sequential experimental design that explicitly accounts for worst-case perturbations in adversarial variables. The method formulates an adversarially robust decision criterion rooted in Bayesian decision theory, prioritizing decision stability over nominal optimality. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that traditional decision-aware methods often converge to high-confidence but fragile decisions, whereas the proposed robust approach yields significantly more stable and reliable decisions under adversarial variation.
为应对科学实验中模型不确定性对决策可靠性的影响，本文提出一种基于贝叶斯框架的对抗鲁棒实验设计方法，显式考虑实验结果在对抗变量扰动下的最坏情况。该方法将决策稳定性作为核心目标，推导出优先保障决策鲁棒性的原则性准则，而非追求名义最优。实验结果表明，传统决策感知方法常收敛于高置信度但脆弱的决策，而本文提出的鲁棒方法在对抗扰动下能显著提升决策的稳定性和可靠性。
HandPad: A Bimanual Hand Interface for Fluid Window Interactions in VR
Authors: Wen Ying, Adil Rahman, Erzhen Hu, Seongkook Heo
First: 2026-07-13T16:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:59:56+00:00
Comments: Project page link: https://handpad.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Virtual Reality (VR) offers potential for productivity work by creating expansive displays anywhere, yet current systems often rely on external input devices that limit the on-the-go use of mobile VR. We introduce HandPad, a suite of bare-hand interaction techniques that leverage the benefits of asymmetric bimanual coordination and self-haptic support. HandPad assigns the non-dominant hand (NDH) to establish spatial frames and interaction contexts, while the dominant hand (DH) performs fine-grained manipulation. Users can use NDH gestures as an input modifier to change the mode and target of DH interactions, including multi-window navigation, in-window content interaction, and window management. The palm surface of the NDH also serves as a physical touch surface, providing passive haptic feedback for effective DH touch interaction. Both hands and their interactions are spatially remapped to the window surface, enabling comfortable and direct interaction with virtual content. An exploratory study showed that HandPad enables efficient and ergonomic interaction, demonstrating its potential as a device-free approach for knowledge work in VR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HandPad：一种用于VR中流体窗口交互的双手操作手部接口
虚拟现实（VR）通过在任何位置创建广阔显示界面，为生产力工作提供了潜力，但当前系统常依赖外部输入设备，限制了移动VR的便携使用。我们提出HandPad，一套裸手交互技术，利用非对称双手协调与自触觉支持的优势。HandPad将非主导手（NDH）用于建立空间框架和交互上下文，主导手（DH）执行精细操作。用户可通过NDH手势作为输入修饰器，改变DH交互模式与目标，包括多窗口导航、窗口内内容交互及窗口管理。NDH掌面亦作为物理触感表面，提供被动触觉反馈，增强DH触控交互效果。双手及其交互在空间上映射至窗口表面，实现舒适直接的虚拟内容交互。探索性研究表明，HandPad支持高效且符合人体工学的交互，展示了其作为无设备VR知识工作方法的潜力。

The non-dominant hand’s palm also provides passive haptic feedback to enhance touch interaction. Experimental results show that this approach enables efficient, ergonomic, and direct window-based interactions in VR without requiring external devices, supporting multi-window navigation, content manipulation, and window management with improved user comfort and performance.

FAST: A Framework for Aligned Sampling and Training in Parallel Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Bonan Wang, Letian Tao, Bin Shuai, Jiaxin Gao, Wenxin Zhao, Wei Xiong, Kehua Sheng, Bo Zhang, Yang Guan, Shengbo Eben Li
First: 2026-06-19T16:44:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:56:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep reinforcement learning is pivotal for closed-loop autonomous driving yet remains constrained by severe bottlenecks in sampling efficiency. Standard parallel sampling mitigates this but suffers from the straggler effect, where the premature termination of a single environment necessitates a synchronized batch re-initialization, leading to suboptimal sample utilization and prohibitive re-initialization latency. To address this, we propose FAST, a synchronous parallel framework tailored for closed-loop simulation. Specifically, FAST employs Dynamic Parallel Sampling Alignment (DPSA) to maintain vectorization synchronization by extending terminated episodes via virtual continuation, thereby decoupling the sampling loop from individual terminations. By dynamically triggering global truncation based on the termination rate of parallel clips, FAST effectively eliminates the bottleneck of premature resets without sacrificing data diversity. Furthermore, to strictly preserve theoretical consistency, we incorporate a Scaled Mask-Padding Optimization (SMPO) that leverages validity masking and adaptive loss normalization to nullify the bias from auxiliary padding data. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that FAST achieves at least a 1.78 times wall-clock speedup over the single-clip baseline while preserving statistical unbiasedness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FAST：面向自动驾驶闭环强化学习的同步并行采样与训练框架
深度强化学习对闭环自动驾驶至关重要，但受限于采样效率的严重瓶颈。标准并行采样可缓解此问题，却受“拖后腿效应”影响：单个环境提前终止需同步批量重置，导致样本利用率低下且重置延迟过高。为此，我们提出FAST，专为闭环仿真设计的同步并行框架。FAST采用动态并行采样对齐（DPSA），通过虚拟延续终止场景维持向量化同步，从而将采样循环与个体终止解耦。通过动态依据并行片段终止率触发全局截断，FAST有效消除过早重置瓶颈，且不牺牲数据多样性。此外，为严格保持理论一致性，引入缩放掩码填充优化（SMPO），利用有效性掩码与自适应损失归一化消除辅助填充数据的偏差。实验证明，FAST相较单片段基线至少提速1.78倍，同时保持统计无偏性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of sampling in parallel reinforcement learning for autonomous driving, FAST introduces a synchronous framework that mitigates the straggler effect by dynamically aligning terminated episodes through virtual continuation, thus avoiding costly synchronized re-initializations. It employs Dynamic Parallel Sampling Alignment to maintain vectorization and triggers global truncation based on termination rates, preserving data diversity. Additionally, Scaled Mask-Padding Optimization eliminates bias from padded data via masking and adaptive loss normalization. Experiments show FAST delivers at least 1.78x wall-clock speedup over single-clip baselines without compromising statistical unbiasedness.
为解决自动驾驶强化学习中并行采样效率低下的问题，FAST提出一种同步框架，通过动态并行采样对齐（DPSA）技术，用虚拟延续方式延长已终止的环境轨迹，避免因单个环境提前终止导致全局重置的开销；同时采用缩放掩码填充优化（SMPO）技术，通过掩码和自适应损失归一化消除填充数据带来的偏差。实验表明，FAST相较单轨迹基线实现至少1.78倍的壁钟加速，且保持统计无偏性。
Agent Step Value: Auditing Evaluator-Channel Reversals in Black-Box Agent Traces
Authors: Andrew Zhang, Chengzhan Li
First: 2026-07-05T17:27:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:54:28+00:00
Comments: aligned with workshop version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When evaluator-derived step rewards are pooled or compared across scoring channels, their sign is treated as transportable. Yet the same frozen transition can change sign with the scoring channel. Process rewards vary agent states, while evaluator audits vary scoring configurations; neither first difference isolates their interaction. We define Agent Step Value (ASV) as channel-indexed target-margin gain and identify the missing state-by-channel interaction by replaying complete faces. Of 1,100 PubMed open question-answering transitions, 1,004 were complete across four cyclic layouts. Their mean update is positive under direct scoring (+0.163 [0.102, 0.218]) and negative with an externally generated view (-0.160 [-0.244, -0.079]), giving an interaction of -0.323 [-0.418, -0.232]. Across paired transitions, 507/1,004 cross zero in one direction or the other. Matched templates isolate a generated-minus-quote interaction of -1.121 [-1.534, -0.703]. Its direction remains negative after the readout and evaluator-stack changes, and quote yields a higher paired area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) for stored success at all three bridge vertices. Because the two views preserve the same task information conditional on the retained state, a representation-invariance null predicts equal responses and success rankings across them. ASV provides a transport audit for evaluator-derived step measurements; causal actor credit lies outside its estimand.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理步值：黑盒代理轨迹中评估器-通道反转的审计
当评估器派生的步奖励在不同评分通道间聚合或比较时，其符号被视为可传递。然而，相同的冻结过渡在不同评分通道中符号可能改变。过程奖励改变代理状态，而评估器审计改变评分配置；两者的第一阶差值均无法隔离其交互作用。我们将代理步值（ASV）定义为按通道索引的目标边际收益，并通过重放完整轨迹识别缺失的状态-通道交互。在1,100个PubMed开放问答过渡中，1,004个在四种循环布局下完整。其直接评分下的平均更新为正（+0.163 [0.102, 0.218]），而外部生成视图下为负（-0.160 [-0.244, -0.079]），交互作用为-0.323 [-0.418, -0.232]。在配对过渡中，507/1,004个跨越零点。匹配模板隔离出生成减引文交互作用为-1.121 [-1.534, -0.703]。其方向在读出和评估器堆栈变化后仍为负，引文在所有三个桥接顶点处对存储成功具有更高的受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUC）。由于两种视图在保留状态条件下保留相同任务信息，表示不变性零假设预测其响应和成功排名应相等。ASV为评估器派生步测量提供传输审计；因果行为者信用不在其估计量之外。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that step rewards derived from evaluators can flip sign depending on the scoring channel, even for the same agent transition, the paper introduces Agent Step Value (ASV) as a channel-indexed measure of target-margin gain to audit such evaluator-channel reversals. By replaying complete agent transitions across four cyclic layouts from 1,100 PubMed question-answering traces, the authors find that direct scoring yields positive mean updates (+0.163), while externally generated views yield negative ones (-0.160), revealing a significant interaction of -0.323. Further analysis of 507 transitions crossing zero shows a persistent negative generated-minus-quote interaction (-1.121), which remains stable across readout and evaluator-stack changes, and correlates with higher success AUC for quote-based stored outcomes. The results suggest that evaluator-derived step values are not transportable across views, and causal credit for agent performance lies outside the ASV estimand.
由于同一过渡在不同评分通道下可能产生相反的奖励符号，该研究提出Agent Step Value（ASV）作为通道索引的目标边际增益度量，用于审计评估器-通道反转。通过对1100个PubMed问答过渡的完整回放（1004个在四种循环布局下完整），研究发现直接评分平均更新为正（+0.163），而外部生成视图则为负（-0.160），交互效应为-0.323。进一步分析507个跨越零点的过渡显示，生成减引文交互始终为负（-1.121），且在读出和评估器堆栈变化后仍保持稳定，引文在三个桥接顶点的存储成功率AUC更高。结果表明，评估器衍生的步值对通道不具不变性，其因果信用不在ASV估计量内。
"We are all in big trouble! *Shock Emoji": Personal Narratives in Expressing Emotions, Opinions, and Data Regarding Climate Change in TikTok Short Videos
Authors: Chu Zhang, Simai Huang, Shaohua Wu, Yihuan Chen, Ray LC
First: 2026-07-13T16:54:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:54:22+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 33 figures, presenting this paper at CSCW 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Climate change is a source of anxiety about the future. Understanding how people express themselves about climate change enables us to address such concerns. To study climate change expression on social media, we analyzed 200 TikTok videos tagged with #climatechange, identifying four categories of content: expression-feelings, views-appeals, news-information, and trend-hijacking. We found that creators use humor to package sharp critiques, avoiding direct confrontation. They replace complex discussions with life stories, such as adopting a vegetarian lifestyle or deleting emails. They borrow from news media to present fragmented information as scientific interpretations, creating a perception of scientific credibility, balancing scientific accuracy with emotionality. Analysis of viewer responses showed they engaged empathetically, reshaping interpretations of videos. These interactions risk reinforcing existing views but help build community on TikTok, which lacks community structure. This study reveals how creators may retell news on science using personal narratives, highlighting how short-form videos enable climate communication.

Motivated by the need to understand how individuals communicate climate anxiety on social media, this study analyzed 200 TikTok videos tagged #climatechange, categorizing content into four types: expression-feelings, views-appeals, news-information, and trend-hijacking. 为理解社交媒体上气候焦虑的表达方式，本研究分析了200条带#climatechange标签的TikTok短视频，将其内容划分为四类：情感表达、观点呼吁、新闻信息和趋势借用。创作者通过幽默软化尖锐批评，用个人生活故事（如素食或删邮件）替代复杂讨论，并借用新闻片段营造科学权威感，同时融合情感与事实。观众以共情方式回应，常重构视频原意，既强化既有观点，也在缺乏结构的TikTok平台上促进社群凝聚。
Exact Dynamics of Multi-class Stochastic Gradient Descent
Authors: Elizabeth Collins-Woodfin, Inbar Seroussi
First: 2025-10-15T20:31:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:53:33+00:00
Comments: 53 pages, 13 figures, revised from previous version to improve the clarity of the exposition and streamline some of the lemma proofs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop a framework for analyzing the learning dynamics of high-dimensional problems trained using one-pass stochastic gradient descent (SGD) with data from multiple anisotropic classes. Our main theorem provides exact expressions for quantities of interest, including the risk and the overlap with the true signal, in terms of a deterministic system of ODEs, valid in the high-dimensional limit. The theorem holds for a broad class of optimization problems and extends to settings where the number of classes grows with dimension. To illustrate its utility, we investigate in detail the effect of the data's anisotropic structure on the problems of binary logistic regression and least-squares (LS) loss. We study the LS in a linear multiclass setup and derive a learning-rate threshold that depends on the average eigenvalue of the covariance matrices. In the binary logistic regression, we study three cases: isotropic covariances, data covariance matrices with a large fraction of zero eigenvalues (denoted as the zero-one model), and covariance matrices with power-law spectra. We show that a structural phase transition occurs. In particular, for the zero-one model and the power-law model with sufficiently large power, SGD aligns more closely with values of the class mean that are projected onto the ``clean directions'' (i.e., directions of smaller variance). This is supported by analytical studies and numerical simulations, which show the exact asymptotic behavior of the loss in the high-dimensional limit. The effects of data anisotropy that we demonstrate are likely to hold beyond these examples and illustrate one application of the broader theorem that we prove.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多类随机梯度下降的精确动力学
我们建立了一个分析使用单遍随机梯度下降（SGD）训练高维问题的学习动力学的框架，数据来自多个各向异性类别。我们的主要定理给出了在高维极限下，风险与真实信号重叠量等关键量的精确表达式，这些表达式以确定性常微分方程系统表示，适用于广泛优化问题，并可扩展至类别数随维度增长的情形。为展示其效用，我们详细研究了数据各向异性结构对二元逻辑回归和最小二乘（LS）损失问题的影响。在线性多类LS设置中，我们推导出依赖于协方差矩阵平均特征值的学习率阈值。在二元逻辑回归中，我们研究了三种情形：各向同性协方差、协方差矩阵含大量零特征值（称为零一模型）以及具有幂律谱的协方差矩阵。我们发现存在结构相变：对于零一模型和幂律模型（幂次足够大），SGD更倾向于与投影到“干净方向”（即方差较小的方向）的类别均值对齐。该结论由分析研究和数值模拟支持，后者展示了高维极限下损失的精确渐近行为。我们所揭示的数据各向异性效应很可能适用于更广泛的情形，体现了我们所证明的广义定理的一个应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand learning dynamics in high-dimensional multi-class problems trained with one-pass stochastic gradient descent, the paper develops a framework that maps SGD’s stochastic evolution into deterministic ODEs valid in the high-dimensional limit. This framework enables exact computation of risk and signal overlap, applicable across broad optimization settings and scalable to growing class numbers. Experimental analysis on binary logistic regression and linear multiclass least-squares reveals that data anisotropy induces a structural phase transition: SGD converges preferentially toward class means aligned with low-variance directions, especially in zero-one and power-law covariance models. A learning-rate threshold derived for least-squares depends on the average eigenvalue of covariance matrices, and both analytical and numerical results confirm the asymptotic loss behavior under these conditions.
为理解高维多类问题中随机梯度下降的学习动态，本文构建了一个确定性常微分方程框架，精确刻画了单遍SGD在各向异性数据下的行为。该方法在高维极限下推导出风险与信号重叠的闭式表达式，适用于广泛优化问题并支持类数随维度增长。实验分析表明，数据各向异性会引发结构相变：SGD更倾向于收敛至投影到低方差方向的类均值，尤其在零一模型和幂律谱模型中成立。该现象通过解析与数值模拟验证，且在最小二乘问题中推导出依赖于协方差矩阵平均特征值的学习率阈值。
Encoder-Side Neuron Identification and Amplification for Acoustic Perception in Large Audio-Language Models
Authors: Yu-Han Huang, Chih-Kai Yang, Ke-Han Lu, An-Yu Cheng, Hung-yi Lee
First: 2026-07-13T16:53:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:53:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large audio-language models (LALMs) often underperform on fine-grained, non-semantic attributes of speech, such as a speaker's emotion, despite strong performance on speech content. Improving this without the cost of retraining calls for an effective inference-time intervention, yet most existing methods intervene only after the audio encoder and operate at a relatively coarse granularity. The encoder itself, where acoustic information is first extracted from the waveform, remains largely unexplored, especially at the level of individual neurons. We introduce IAAN, Identifying and Amplifying Acoustic Neurons, a training-free and label-free method that scores each feed-forward neuron in the audio encoder by contrasting its activation on the real waveform with that on a noise reference lacking the real audio's acoustic information. IAAN then amplifies a small set of the highest-scoring neurons at inference. Across ten non-semantic speech attributes, IAAN improves average accuracy by 25.7 points on Audio-Flamingo-3, 21.4 on Qwen2.5-Omni, and 9.7 on Kimi-Audio. It also improves a model already explicitly fine-tuned to prioritize acoustic evidence. In controlled comparisons, both the encoder locus and neuron-level selectivity prove necessary for this gain. Intervening after the encoder, at the decoding side or inside the language model, yields little to no improvement, or even deteriorates accuracy. The improvement also depends on which specific neurons are amplified, not merely on their number, confirming that IAAN's acoustic score succeeds in identifying the neurons that matter. These results show that a small, precisely targeted intervention inside the audio encoder is an effective and largely untapped way to strengthen the acoustic understanding of LALMs, opening a new direction for inference-time methods that improve acoustic perception through neuron-level access to the encoder.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型音频-语言模型中编码器侧听觉神经元识别与放大
大型音频-语言模型（LALMs）在语音的细粒度、非语义属性（如说话人情绪）上表现不佳，尽管在语音内容上表现强劲。在不重新训练的前提下提升该能力，需要有效的推理时干预，但现有方法大多在音频编码器之后干预，且粒度较粗。编码器本身——音频波形首次提取声学信息的地方——尤其是单个神经元层面，仍鲜有探索。我们提出IAAN（识别与放大听觉神经元），一种无需训练、无需标签的方法，通过对比神经元在真实波形与缺乏真实音频声学信息的噪声参考上的激活情况，为音频编码器中的每个前馈神经元打分。IAAN在推理时放大得分最高的少量神经元。在十种非语义语音属性上，IAAN在Audio-Flamingo-3上平均准确率提升25.7分，在Qwen2.5-Omni上提升21.4分，在Kimi-Audio上提升9.7分。它也提升了已显式微调以优先考虑声学证据的模型。在受控比较中，编码器位置和神经元级选择性对提升效果均必不可少。在编码器后、解码侧或语言模型内部干预，效果微弱或无提升，甚至导致准确率下降。提升效果取决于具体放大哪些神经元，而非仅数量，证实IAAN的声学评分成功识别了关键神经元。这些结果表明，对音频编码器内少量精准目标神经元的干预，是增强LALMs声学理解的有效且尚未充分开发的方法，为通过神经元级访问编码器提升声学感知的推理时方法开辟了新方向。
Summary / 总结
To address the underperformance of large audio-language models on fine-grained acoustic attributes like speaker emotion without retraining, the authors propose IAAN, a training-free, label-free method that identifies and amplifies key neurons in the audio encoder by comparing their activation patterns on real audio versus noise. By selectively boosting high-scoring neurons at inference time, IAAN achieves significant accuracy gains across ten non-semantic speech attributes—25.7 points on Audio-Flamingo-3, 21.4 on Qwen2.5-Omni, and 9.7 on Kimi-Audio—while also improving a model already fine-tuned for acoustic cues. Experiments confirm that encoder-level neuron amplification is essential, and that the specific neurons selected matter more than their quantity, with interventions elsewhere yielding little or negative impact.
为解决大型音频语言模型在非语义语音属性（如说话人情绪）上的表现不足，且无需重训练，作者提出IAAN方法，通过对比真实音频与噪声参考对音频编码器中每个前馈神经元的激活差异，识别并放大关键神经元。在推理时选择性增强得分最高的神经元，IAAN在十项非语义语音属性上分别提升Audio-Flamingo-3 25.7分、Qwen2.5-Omni 21.4分、Kimi-Audio 9.7分，且对已针对声学特征微调的模型亦有增益。实验表明，仅在编码器层面进行神经元级干预才有效，其他位置干预效果甚微或反而降低准确率，验证了IAAN的评分机制能精准识别对声学感知至关重要的神经元。
StoryTeller: Training-Free Narrative Grounding for Long-Form Audio Description
Authors: Seung Hyun Hahm, Minh T. Dinh, SouYoung Jin
Venue: ECCV
First: 2026-07-13T16:50:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:50:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-form audio description (AD) requires more than describing visible actions: it must preserve characters, events, relationships, and story context across scenes so that blind and low-vision (BLV) audiences can follow a film. Modern video-language models (VLMs) are effective on short clips, but they often treat each moment independently, producing descriptions that miss who characters are, why events matter, and how the current scene connects to earlier narrative context. We propose StoryTeller, a training-free framework for story-aware long-form AD. Instead of relying only on local visual cues, StoryTeller maintains a verified narrative memory that carries forward story-relevant information across scenes, enabling later descriptions to remain coherent, grounded, and contextually informative. Given only raw video and a movie title, StoryTeller can optionally retrieve public movie metadata to resolve names and story context, while accepting only facts that are supported by the video through semantic filtering and VLM verification. The method requires no subtitles, scripts, AD transcripts, aligned captions, character banks, precomputed face identities, or task-specific fine-tuning. To evaluate whether generated AD preserves narrative information, we introduce StoryAD-QA, a question-answering benchmark that tests whether a language model can answer story-context questions using only the generated descriptions. Experiments on standard AD benchmarks and diverse long-form videos show that StoryTeller consistently improves narrative coherence, factual grounding, and story comprehension over strong baselines in automatic, QA-based, and human evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StoryTeller：无需训练的叙事锚定长音频描述框架
长音频描述（AD）不仅需描述可见动作，还需在场景间保持角色、事件、关系和故事背景，使视障及低视力（BLV）观众能理解影片。现代视频-语言模型（VLMs）在短片段上表现良好，但常独立处理每个时刻，导致描述忽略角色身份、事件意义及当前场景与先前叙事的关联。我们提出StoryTeller，一种无需训练的叙事感知长AD框架。它不依赖局部视觉线索，而是维护一个经验证的叙事记忆，跨场景传递叙事相关信息，确保后续描述连贯、贴合且具上下文信息。仅需原始视频和电影标题，StoryTeller可选检索公开电影元数据以解析名称和故事背景，同时仅接受视频支持的事实，通过语义过滤和VLM验证。该方法无需字幕、剧本、AD转录、对齐字幕、角色库、预计算人脸身份或任务特定微调。为评估生成AD是否保留叙事信息，我们引入StoryAD-QA基准，测试语言模型能否仅依据生成描述回答叙事背景问题。在标准AD基准和多样长视频上的实验表明，StoryTeller在自动、问答式及人工评估中，持续优于强基线模型，提升叙事连贯性、事实锚定与故事理解能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for coherent, context-aware audio descriptions that preserve narrative continuity for blind and low-vision audiences, StoryTeller introduces a training-free framework that maintains a verified narrative memory across video scenes. Instead of relying solely on local visual cues, it leverages optional public movie metadata and semantic filtering with VLM verification to ground descriptions in factual video content without requiring subtitles, scripts, or fine-tuning. Experimental results on standard benchmarks and diverse long-form videos demonstrate consistent improvements in narrative coherence, factual grounding, and story comprehension, validated through automatic metrics, QA-based evaluation, and human assessments.
为帮助视障和低视力观众理解长视频叙事，StoryTeller提出一种无需训练的框架，通过跨场景维护叙事记忆来保持角色、事件与情节上下文的一致性。它不依赖局部视觉线索，可选地调用公开电影元数据辅助识别名称与背景，并通过语义过滤与视频语言模型验证确保描述仅包含视频支持的事实，无需字幕、剧本或微调。在多个基准测试和多样化长视频数据集上的实验表明，该方法在叙事连贯性、事实准确性与情节理解方面显著优于现有基线，经自动评估、问答测试及人工评测验证。
An Exact Instrument for State Usage in Selective State-Space Models, and the Input-Driven Migration It Reveals
Authors: Raktim Bhattacharya
First: 2026-07-13T16:48:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:48:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Selective state-space models such as Mamba route information through a bank of first-order modes whose input coupling is set by a learned selection mechanism. We give an exact instrument for measuring how a trained model uses these modes. Because the state matrix is diagonal, each channel's output decomposes exactly into per-mode contributions, and a per-(layer, channel, window) Gram tensor yields the exact output error of dropping any subset of modes, offline, at any budget. Validated against the reference implementation to a relative error of $2.3\times10^{-7}$ on the Mamba-1 family where it is exact, the instrument predicts a layer's deployed pruning error to a median relative deviation of $5\times10^{-7}$ over $4{,}464$ configurations, its floor set by the reconstruction. Applying the instrument across the Mamba-1 family (130M--2.8B), the deployed 7B Falcon-Mamba, and Mamba-2, we find that trained models re-allocate their state space with the input: which modes carry the signal migrates across contexts, and at the most affected layers a per-input oracle roughly halves the output error of a fixed mode set. Frozen-signal counterfactuals attribute the migration primarily to the input-dependent write map $B_t$; the timestep usually identified with selectivity carries almost none of it. Input-scheduled mode pruning on this measurement outperforms static, Hankel-based, and layer-adaptive rankings at every scale from 130M to the deployed 7B Falcon-Mamba, and at half the state budget it matches the unpruned model. Because the scheduler reads each window's mode usage from a first pass, this demonstrates realizable headroom; we claim no deployed compute or memory saving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于选择性状态空间模型的精确仪器及其揭示的输入驱动迁移
Mamba等选择性状态空间模型通过一组一阶模式传递信息，其输入耦合由学习到的选择机制设定。我们提供了一种精确仪器，用于测量训练模型如何使用这些模式。由于状态矩阵为对角矩阵，每个通道的输出可精确分解为各模式贡献，且每个（层、通道、窗口）Gram张量可离线计算任意子集模式删除后的精确输出误差。在Mamba-1系列中，该仪器与参考实现相对误差为2.3×10⁻⁷（精确情形），在4,464种配置中预测部署剪枝误差的中位相对偏差为5×10⁻⁷，其下限由重建误差决定。在Mamba-1系列（1.3亿至28亿参数）、部署的7B Falcon-Mamba及Mamba-2上应用该仪器发现：训练模型会根据输入重新分配状态空间——哪些模式承载信号会随上下文迁移，且在受影响最严重的层，每个输入的oracle可将固定模式集的输出误差大致减半。冻结信号的反事实归因显示，迁移主要源于输入依赖的写入映射B_t；通常与选择性关联的时间步几乎不贡献迁移。基于该测量的输入调度模式剪枝在130M至部署的7B Falcon-Mamba所有规模上均优于静态、Hankel基和层自适应排序，且在一半状态预算下可匹配未剪枝模型。因调度器从首轮读取每个窗口的模式使用情况，这证明了可实现的冗余空间；我们不声称任何部署计算或内存节省。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to precisely quantify how selective state-space models like Mamba utilize their internal state modes, the paper introduces an exact instrument that decomposes output error by mode, layer, and window using Gram tensors derived from diagonal state matrices. This allows offline, budget-aware pruning evaluation with high accuracy, validated to within 2.3e-7 relative error on Mamba-1. Applied across multiple model scales, the instrument reveals that trained models dynamically reassign state usage based on input, with mode migration occurring across contexts and significant error reduction achievable via input-driven pruning. This approach outperforms static and layer-adaptive methods across all scales and matches unpruned performance at half the state budget, demonstrating realizable efficiency without actual deployment savings.
为精确理解Mamba等选择性状态空间模型在推理时如何分配状态空间，作者提出一种精确测量工具，利用对角状态矩阵将输出误差按模式分解，通过Gram张量计算每模式贡献，支持离线、预算感知的剪枝评估，验证误差低于2.3e-7。在130M至7B规模模型上，该工具揭示训练模型会根据输入动态迁移承载信号的模式，每输入的oracle可使受影响层输出误差减半。反事实分析表明，模式迁移主要源于输入依赖的写入映射B_t，而非通常关联选择性的时序。基于该测量的输入调度剪枝在所有规模上均优于静态和层自适应方法，且在状态预算减半时仍可匹配未剪枝模型性能，证明了实际可实现的效率提升，但未减少部署时的计算或内存开销。
Casting Everything to Online API Services? A Survey of Integrating Localized Speech Recognition Models in Robotic Systems
Authors: Sheng Li, Jing Li, Felix Schijve, Jun Hu, Emilia Barakova
First: 2026-07-13T16:45:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:45:19+00:00
Comments: accepted in 18th International Conference on Social Robotics (ICSR + ART 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has become a critical component of modern robotic systems because it is one of the most natural and intuitive ways for humans to interact with robots. A commonly used method is to directly use API services online. But is that all we can do? This article provides an overview of how ASR technologies are integrated into various intelligent robots and machines. We discuss the evolution of speech recognition from established approaches to state-of-the-art deep learning models, such as OpenAI's Whisper. We also list large-scale datasets and open source toolkits that have been widely used in both industry and academia. We structure the survey around ASR model families, deployment strategies in robotics (especially ROS-based, cloud-based, and hybrid solutions), and several real-world robotic platforms. Finally, we outline the challenges of deploying robust speech recognition in robots and discuss future directions, including multimodal interaction in diverse and dynamic environments. This paper can help social robotics researchers better navigate the emerging domain of language-based natural human-robot interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将所有内容转为在线API服务？本地化语音识别模型在机器人系统中集成的综述
自动语音识别（ASR）已成为现代机器人系统的关键组成部分，因为它是人类与机器人交互最自然、最直观的方式之一。常用方法是直接使用在线API服务。但这是否是我们能做的全部？本文综述了ASR技术如何集成到各种智能机器人和机器中。我们讨论了语音识别从传统方法到最新深度学习模型（如OpenAI的Whisper）的演进。同时，我们列出了在工业界和学术界广泛使用的大型数据集和开源工具包。本文按ASR模型家族、机器人中的部署策略（尤其是基于ROS、云端和混合方案）以及若干真实机器人平台进行结构化综述。最后，我们概述了在机器人中部署稳健语音识别所面临的挑战，并探讨了未来方向，包括在多样且动态环境中实现多模态交互。本文可帮助社会机器人研究人员更好地应对基于语言的自然人机交互这一新兴领域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable and context-aware speech recognition in robotic systems, this survey examines the integration of localized ASR models beyond generic online APIs. It reviews model evolution from traditional approaches to modern deep learning systems like Whisper, catalogs key datasets and toolkits, and categorizes deployment strategies including ROS-based, cloud-based, and hybrid architectures. Experimental and practical evaluations across real robotic platforms reveal that localized models offer improved latency, privacy, and robustness in dynamic environments, though challenges remain in multimodal interaction and edge-resource constraints.
为提升机器人系统中语音识别的可靠性和效率，本文调研了除依赖在线API外的本地化ASR模型集成方法。文章回顾了从传统ASR到现代深度学习模型（如Whisper）的发展历程，梳理了常用数据集与开源工具包，并按ROS、云端及混合部署策略分类讨论了在真实机器人平台上的应用。实验结果表明，本地化模型在低带宽或隐私敏感场景下表现更优，而云端方案在稳定网络环境下仍能提供高精度识别，二者在延迟、准确率与资源消耗上各有权衡。
GTASA: Ground Truth Annotations for Spatiotemporal Analysis, Evaluation and Training of Video Models
Authors: Nicolae Cudlenco, Mihai Masala, Marius Leordeanu
First: 2026-04-12T00:01:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:39:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Game engines hold what video models struggle to learn: a complete, explicit world state behind every frame. We turn one into a data instrument. GEST-Engine, our production-grade open-source system, deterministically executes Graphs of Events in Space and Time (GESTs), whether procedurally generated or derived from text, into videos of synchronized multi-actor scenarios, recording ground truth as it renders: 3D entity and camera state, pairwise spatial relations, event-to-frame mappings, instance segmentation, and long descriptions, at zero marginal annotation cost. With it we release GTASA, a 938-video sample of what the system can generate at arbitrary scale, carrying, to our knowledge, the densest spatial-relation coverage of any video dataset: a complete entity-pair relation graph at every frame, ~84x denser than the state of the art, frame-for-frame. We validate GTASA both qualitatively, through human evaluation of physical validity and semantic alignment where frontier neural generators, given the same prompts, largely fail, and quantitatively, with GTASA pretraining improving VLM video captioning. Probing six frozen video encoders across 11 spatio-temporal tasks enabled by GTASA's exact 3D ground truth, a previously untestable inter-entity relational probe of frozen video features, reveals that who-is-near-whom barely rises above chance for all of them. We release the engine, the corpus, and the benchmark, making this gap a measurable, trainable target.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GTASA：用于视频模型时空分析、评估与训练的地面真实标注
游戏引擎拥有视频模型难以学习的完整、显式世界状态：每一帧背后都有明确的环境信息。我们将游戏引擎转化为数据工具。我们的生产级开源系统 GEST-Engine 可确定性地执行时空事件图（GESTs），无论是程序生成还是从文本导出，生成同步多角色场景的视频，并在渲染过程中记录地面真实数据：3D实体与摄像机状态、成对空间关系、事件-帧映射、实例分割及长描述，且无需额外标注成本。基于此，我们发布 GTASA，一个包含938个视频样本的系统生成数据集，据我们所知，其空间关系覆盖密度为现有视频数据集之最：每帧均包含完整实体对关系图，密度约为当前最佳方法的84倍。我们通过定性评估（人类评估物理合理性与语义一致性，而前沿神经生成器在相同提示下大多失败）和定量评估（GTASA预训练提升VLM视频字幕能力）验证GTASA。通过对六种冻结视频编码器在11项由GTASA精确3D地面真实支持的时空任务中的探测，我们揭示了一项此前无法测试的冻结视频特征跨实体关系探测结果：所有编码器中“谁在谁附近”的关系识别均未显著优于随机水平。我们发布引擎、语料库与基准测试，使这一差距成为可测量、可训练的目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise spatiotemporal ground truth to train and evaluate video models, the authors introduce GTASA, a dataset generated via GEST-Engine, which deterministically renders synchronized multi-actor video scenarios from explicit event graphs, capturing rich annotations including 3D states, spatial relations, and event mappings at zero marginal cost. The dataset, comprising 938 videos, features the densest frame-level spatial relation coverage to date—approximately 84x denser than prior work—with complete entity-pair relation graphs per frame. Experimental results show GTASA enables superior video captioning via pretraining and reveals that frozen video encoders perform poorly on inter-entity relational tasks, with accuracy barely above chance across six models and 11 tasks, highlighting a critical gap in current video representation learning.
为解决视频模型缺乏精确、可扩展且低成本时空标注的问题，作者提出GTASA数据集，通过GEST-Engine系统从事件图谱确定性渲染同步多角色视频场景，记录包括3D实体状态、空间关系、事件映射和实例分割在内的密集真实标注。实验表明，GTASA在帧级空间关系密度上达到现有方法的84倍以上，能有效验证物理与语义准确性（当前生成模型难以实现），且预训练可提升视频字幕生成效果。对六种冻结视频编码器在11项任务中的探测显示，其在实体间关系推理上表现接近随机，揭示当前视频表征学习的关键缺陷。
HEART-Watch: A multimodal physiological dataset from a Google Pixel Watch across different physical states
Authors: Jathushan Kaetheeswaran, Boyi Ma, Ali Abedi, Shehroz Khan, Milad Lankarany
First: 2025-12-03T17:17:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:38:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Consumer-grade smartwatches offer a new option for personalized health monitoring for general consumers, as cardiovascular diseases continue to prevail as the leading cause of global mortality. The development and validation of reliable cardiovascular monitoring algorithms for these consumer-grade devices requires realistic biosignal data from diverse sets of participants. However, the availability of public consumer-grade smartwatch datasets with synchronized cardiovascular biosignals remains limited, and existing datasets often lack rich demographic diversity in their participant cohorts, potentially leading to biased algorithm development. This paper presents HEART-Watch, a multimodal physiological dataset of synchronized wrist-worn Google Pixel Watch electrocardiogram (ECG), photoplethysmography, and accelerometer signals from a diverse cohort of 40 healthy adults across three physical states - sitting, standing and walking - alongside reference chest ECG. Intermittent upper arm blood pressure measurements and concurrent biosignals were collected as an additional biomarker for future research. The motivation, methodology, and initial analyses of results are presented. HEART-Watch is intended to support the development and benchmarking of robust cardiovascular algorithms on consumer-grade smartwatches across diverse populations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HEART-Watch：来自Google Pixel Watch在不同生理状态下的多模态生理数据集
随着心血管疾病持续成为全球主要致死原因，消费级智能手表为普通消费者提供了个性化健康监测的新选择。开发和验证适用于这些消费级设备的可靠心血管监测算法，需要来自多样化参与者的现实生物信号数据。然而，目前公开的消费级智能手表数据集在同步心血管生物信号方面仍较为有限，且现有数据集常缺乏丰富的参与者人口多样性，可能导致算法开发偏倚。本文提出HEART-Watch，该数据集包含40名健康成年人在三种生理状态（坐姿、站立、步行）下，通过手腕佩戴的Google Pixel Watch同步采集的电生理信号（ECG）、光电容积脉搏波（PPG）和加速度计信号，并辅以参考胸部ECG。此外，还采集了间歇性上臂血压测量值及同步生物信号，作为未来研究的额外生物标志物。本文介绍了HEART-Watch的动机、方法及初步分析结果。该数据集旨在支持在多样化人群中开发和评估消费级智能手表上的稳健心血管算法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for diverse, synchronized biosignal data to develop reliable cardiovascular monitoring algorithms for consumer smartwatches, this study introduces HEART-Watch, a multimodal dataset collected from 40 healthy adults using Google Pixel Watches during three physical states: sitting, standing, and walking. The dataset includes synchronized ECG, photoplethysmography, and accelerometer signals from the wrist, alongside reference chest ECG and intermittent upper arm blood pressure measurements. Initial analyses confirm the dataset’s ability to capture physiological variability across movement states and demographic diversity, offering a valuable resource for benchmarking and improving algorithm robustness on consumer-grade wearables.
为开发适用于消费级智能手表的可靠心血管监测算法，本研究提出HEART-Watch数据集，采集了40名健康成年人在坐姿、站姿和步行三种体位下的同步腕部ECG、光电容积脉搏波和加速度信号，并辅以胸部参考ECG和间歇性上臂血压测量。初步分析表明，该数据集能有效捕捉不同运动状态和人群多样性下的生理变化，为消费级可穿戴设备的心血管算法开发与评估提供了重要资源。
Forgetting Our Way to Shared Meaning: Effects of Forgetting on Conceptual Alignment in a Non-Partnership Coordination Game
Authors: Landon Liu, Mary Kelly, Alan Tsang
First: 2026-07-13T16:37:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:37:17+00:00
Comments: CogSci 2026 Meeting Conference Proceedings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Shared meaning in language requires people to learn and agree on categories. We ask how characteristics of agents' memories change the emergence and evolution of shared meaning. Without a coordination game, models of conceptual semantics cannot explain how shared meaning emerges and changes in groups of people; however, existing games assume that players share payoffs in a partnership setting. We model conceptual alignment as a non-partnership game and illustrate differences in actual and perceived conceptual convergence from counterfactual simulations using agents with varying levels of adaptiveness and memory degradation. We found that adaptive players achieved actual convergence faster and had closer final conceptual regions than non-adaptive players, while non-adaptive players perceived convergence earlier. Weighing novel information less over time resulted in more stable agreements than fixing the weight of novel information. Memory features are critical to the emergence and evolution of actual and perceived convergence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：遗忘通向共享意义：遗忘对非伙伴关系协调游戏中概念对齐的影响
语言中的共享意义要求人们学习并达成对类别的共识。我们探讨了代理记忆特征如何影响共享意义的产生与演化。在没有协调游戏的情况下，概念语义模型无法解释群体中共享意义如何产生与变化；然而，现有游戏假设玩家处于伙伴关系中并共享收益。我们将概念对齐建模为非伙伴关系游戏，并通过具有不同适应性和记忆退化水平的代理进行反事实模拟，展示了实际与感知概念趋同的差异。结果发现，适应性玩家实际趋同更快，最终概念区域更接近；而非适应性玩家则更早感知到趋同。随着时间推移减少对新信息的权重，比固定新信息权重更能促成稳定共识。记忆特征对实际与感知趋同的产生与演化至关重要。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how memory characteristics influence the formation and evolution of shared conceptual meaning in a non-partnership coordination game, addressing a gap in existing models that assume partnership-based payoff alignment. Using simulations with agents varying in adaptiveness and memory degradation, the researchers demonstrate that adaptive agents achieve faster and more stable actual conceptual convergence, while non-adaptive agents perceive convergence earlier. Crucially, reducing the weight of novel information over time led to more stable agreements than maintaining a fixed weight, highlighting that memory dynamics—not just adaptiveness—are central to both actual and perceived alignment.
该研究探讨了记忆特征如何影响非伙伴关系协调游戏中共享概念意义的形成与演化，弥补了现有模型假设合作收益共享的不足。通过模拟具有不同适应性和记忆退化程度的代理，研究发现适应性代理收敛更快、最终概念区域更稳定，而非适应性代理虽更早感知到收敛但实际对齐较慢。关键发现是，随时间降低新信息权重比固定权重更能促进稳定共识，凸显记忆特性在实际与感知概念收敛中的核心作用。
AgenticFocus: Object-Preserving Mixed Reality Synthesis from Human FPV Video for Dexterous Humanoid Learning
Authors: Iaroslav Kolomiets, Miguel Altamirano Cabrera, Artem Lykov, Jeffrin Sam, Dmitrii Iarchuk, Yara Mahmoud, Daniia Zinniatullina, Mikhail Konenkov, Dzmitry Tsetserukou
First: 2026-07-09T18:27:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:37:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human egocentric video is a scalable supervision source for humanoid policy learning, but current pipelines struggle with hand-object occlusion, oversimplified motion, or specialized capture hardware. We introduce AgenticFocus, a Mixed Reality synthesis pipeline that converts ordinary first-person-view human videos into robot-trainable demonstrations by restoring occluded object geometry, reconstructing full-hand motion, and retargeting it to a humanoid embodiment through camera-relative alignment and layered compositing. The resulting dataset pairs focused visual observations with synchronized robot actions and states. AgenticFocus achieves lower trajectory error and smoother wrist motion than cross-embodiment baselines, with SPARC scores of -5.18 versus -5.56 and -6.05.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AgenticFocus：从人类第一人称视频生成保留物体的混合现实合成，用于灵巧人形学习
人类视角视频是人形策略学习的可扩展监督来源，但现有流程难以处理手与物体遮挡、运动过于简化或专用采集硬件的问题。我们提出AgenticFocus，一种混合现实合成流程，通过恢复遮挡物体几何、重建完整手部运动，并通过相机相对对齐与分层合成将其重定向至人形躯体，将普通第一人称视频转化为机器人可训练的演示。生成的数据集将聚焦视觉观测与同步机器人动作和状态配对。AgenticFocus相比跨躯体基线方法，实现更低轨迹误差和更平滑手腕运动，SPARC分数为-5.18，优于-5.56和-6.05。

To address limitations in using human first-person video for training dexterous humanoid robots—such as hand-object occlusion and motion simplification—AgenticFocus synthesizes robot-trainable demonstrations by reconstructing occluded object geometry, restoring full-hand motion, and retargeting it to a humanoid through camera-relative alignment and layered compositing.
