Experimental results show AgenticFocus outperforms cross-embodiment baselines with lower trajectory error and improved SPARC scores of -5.18 compared to -5.56 and -6.05.
为解决人类第一人称视频在训练灵巧人形机器人时存在的物体遮挡、动作简化和硬件依赖等问题，AgenticFocus通过恢复遮挡物体几何、重建完整手部运动，并通过相机相对对齐与分层合成将其适配至人形机器人，生成可用于训练的机器人演示数据。该数据集将视觉观测与同步机器人动作和状态对齐，提升动作精度与流畅性。实验表明，AgenticFocus在轨迹误差和手腕运动平滑度上优于跨体态基线方法，SPARC得分达-5.18，优于-5.56和-6.05。
MIRA: A Modular Open-Source Micro-UAV for Indoor Research
Authors: Lucas K. de Oliveira, Felipe A. G. Tommaselli, João Aires Marsicano, Marco S. Tayar, Pedro A. R. Saraiva, Ricardo V. Godoy, Marcelo Becker
First: 2026-07-13T16:37:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:37:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Indoor robotics research increasingly relies on micro-UAVs whose airframe, electronics, and control software are fully open to modification. Off-the-shelf platforms rarely expose the low-level access required for such modifications, while building a custom alternative typically requires substantial engineering effort before flight testing can begin, leaving many laboratories to work within constraints that limit the scope of their research. We present MIRA (Modular Indoor Research Architecture), a low-cost, open-source micro-UAV for indoor research built around a replicable 3D-printed PLA airframe and a containerized low-level software package managing the companion-to-autopilot communication bridge via Micro XRCE-DDS. Designed as a white-box architecture, core subsystems are individually replaceable without firmware refactoring, supporting local fabrication and component substitution from existing lab inventory. We characterize MIRA through manual flight in position-control mode within an optical motion-capture volume, where the communication pipeline sustains a median companion-to-autopilot latency of 0.02 ms and power spectral density analysis confirms the structural vibration energy stays concentrated in a narrow 90 to 110 Hz band, isolated from the sub-20 Hz control bandwidth and within the autopilot safety thresholds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MIRA：一种用于室内研究的模块化开源微型无人机
室内机器人研究日益依赖于空气框架、电子设备和控制软件均可完全修改的微型无人机。现成平台通常无法提供此类修改所需的底层访问权限，而构建自定义替代方案通常需在飞行测试前投入大量工程工作，导致许多实验室受限于研究范围。我们提出MIRA（模块化室内研究架构），这是一种低成本、开源的微型无人机，采用可复制的PLA 3D打印机架和容器化底层软件包，通过Micro XRCE-DDS管理与飞控的通信桥接。其设计为白盒架构，核心子系统可独立更换而无需固件重构，支持本地制造和从现有实验室库存中替换组件。我们通过在光学运动捕捉体积内手动飞行并采用位置控制模式对MIRA进行表征，通信管道的中位数从从机到飞控的延迟为0.02毫秒，功率谱密度分析证实结构振动能量集中于90至110赫兹窄频带内，与低于20赫兹的控制带宽分离，并处于飞控安全阈值之内。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for customizable, low-cost indoor micro-UAV platforms that avoid the limitations of off-the-shelf systems or the high engineering overhead of custom builds, the authors introduce MIRA, a modular, open-source micro-UAV built with a 3D-printed PLA airframe and containerized low-level software using Micro XRCE-DDS for autopilot communication. The white-box design allows individual subsystems to be swapped without firmware changes, enabling local fabrication and lab inventory reuse. Experimental results from manual flight in an optical motion-capture volume show a median companion-to-autopilot latency of 0.02 ms and structural vibration confined to 90–110 Hz, well-separated from the 20 Hz control bandwidth and within autopilot safety limits.
为满足室内研究对可定制、低成本微型无人机的需求，作者设计了MIRA，其采用3D打印PLA机架和基于Micro XRCE-DDS的容器化软件，实现通信管理。该平台采用白盒架构，支持模块独立更换而无需固件重构，便于本地制造和实验室现有部件替换。实验在光学动作捕捉体积内手动飞行测试中，位置控制模式下伴侣机与飞控间平均延迟为0.02毫秒，结构振动能量集中在90–110赫兹频段，远离控制带宽且符合飞控安全阈值。
How Temperature Shapes Ideological Discourse in Retrieval-Augmented Generation?
Authors: Elmira Salari, Hazem Amamou, José Victor de Souza, Shruti Kshirsagar, Maria Nunes Delfino, Anderson Avila
First: 2026-07-13T16:36:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:36:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has been increasingly adopted to reduce hallucinations and strengthen the factual grounding of large language models (LLMs). While robustness to errors in the retrieval process has been explored, the impact of ideological bias on LLM outputs has been overlooked. For instance, if the retrieved material contains ideological positions, the RAG may transmit, amplify, or suppress such ideological discourses in its outputs. In this study, we address this issue by examining the influence of the RAG framework, comprising ideological discourses, in LLM-generated answers. To this end, we applied Lexical Multidimensional Analysis (LMDA) on a corpus of 1,117 COVID-19 treatment articles, identifying three ideological discourses. This corpus is then used as the external knowledge source for the RAG. We assessed several LLMs by having the models answer ideological questions at different sampling temperatures. The generated texts were assessed semantically and lexically based on their similarities with ideological reference texts. Our findings show that the RAG framework is prone to transferring ideological discourses into LLM responses, with sampling temperature having a measurable impact on the strength of this transfer. Discoursive alignment between generated answers and the reference text is highest at moderate temperatures, where models balance stochasticity with retrieval grounding, and drops at low temperatures, indicating that overly deterministic sampling suppresses discourse transfer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：温度如何塑造检索增强生成中的意识形态话语？
检索增强生成（RAG）被广泛采用以减少幻觉并增强大语言模型（LLM）的事实基础。尽管已有研究探讨RAG对检索过程错误的鲁棒性，但其对LLM输出意识形态偏见的影响却未被关注。例如，若检索材料包含意识形态立场，RAG可能在输出中传递、放大或抑制此类意识形态话语。本研究通过分析包含意识形态话语的RAG框架对LLM生成答案的影响，解决此问题。为此，我们对1117篇COVID-19治疗文章语料库应用词汇多维分析（LMDA），识别出三种意识形态话语，并将其作为RAG的外部知识源。我们评估了多个LLM在不同采样温度下回答意识形态问题的表现，依据生成文本与意识形态参考文本的语义和词汇相似性进行评估。研究发现，RAG框架易将意识形态话语传递至LLM响应，采样温度对这种传递强度具有可测量影响：中等温度下生成答案与参考文本的语义对齐度最高，因模型在随机性与检索锚定间取得平衡；低温下对齐度下降，表明过度确定性采样会抑制话语传递。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how ideological bias in retrieved documents influences outputs of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems, motivated by the underexplored impact of ideological discourses on LLM responses. Using Lexical Multidimensional Analysis on 1,117 COVID-19 treatment articles, the researchers identified three ideological discourses and used them as external knowledge sources for RAG. They evaluated multiple LLMs under varying sampling temperatures, measuring output alignment with ideological reference texts. Results show that RAG systems tend to transfer ideological discourses into responses, with moderate temperatures maximizing this transfer by balancing stochasticity and retrieval grounding, while low temperatures suppress it due to deterministic sampling.
本研究探讨了RAG框架中检索内容中的意识形态偏见如何影响大语言模型输出，动机在于弥补意识形态传播风险被忽视的问题。研究人员通过对1117篇新冠治疗文章进行词汇多维分析，识别出三种意识形态话语，并将其作为RAG的外部知识源。他们在不同采样温度下评估多个大模型，测量输出与意识形态参考文本的语义和词汇相似度。结果表明，RAG倾向于将意识形态话语传递至输出，中等温度下因平衡了随机性和检索依据，语义对齐度最高，而低温则抑制了这种传递。
A Compact Top-Loading Robot for Endovascular Interventions: Design, Control and Evaluation
Authors: Jonas Fischer, Lennart Karstensen, Franziska Mathis-Ullrich
First: 2026-07-13T16:35:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:35:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robot-assisted endovascular intervention can potentially reduce radiation exposure, improve surgeon ergonomics, enable telesurgery, support active assistance and autonomy, and enhance procedural precision. However, existing systems often suffer from limited procedural coverage because constrained patient-side setups, restricted flexibility, and complex instrument exchange hinder clinical workflow integration. This work presents a compact robotic system for endovascular interventions that enables continuous translational and rotational manipulation of standard endovascular instruments. The system consists of two alternating carts with pneumatically actuated membrane grippers integrated into rotating gripper gears. Its top-loading design allows rapid exchange of instruments such as guidewires and catheters without changing the robotic setup. A leader-follower control strategy enables continuous motion despite the finite stroke of each cart. The system was evaluated in motion-tracking experiments with guidewires and catheters and in an in vitro vascular phantom. The motion-tracking experiments showed generally smooth translational and rotational motion profiles. Across all tested guidewire and catheter experiments, the mean relative tracking errors were 3.6% for translational motion and 4.1% for rotational motion. In the vascular phantom, robot-assisted navigation reached the target in most trials, demonstrating the feasibility of the proposed manipulation concept under in vitro conditions. The presented robotic system demonstrates technical feasibility for continuous manipulation of standard endovascular instruments in bench-top and in vitro experiments. The compact top-loading design may ease instrument exchange and clinical workflow integration. Future work will focus on improving gripping performance, actuation speed, force feedback, and evaluation in more clinically realistic settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于血管内介入的紧凑型顶部装载机器人：设计、控制与评估
机器人辅助血管内介入有望减少辐射暴露、改善外科医生人机工学、支持远程手术、提供主动辅助与自主操作，并提升手术精度。然而，现有系统常因患者侧设置受限、灵活性不足及器械更换复杂而影响临床工作流程整合。本研究提出一种紧凑型血管内介入机器人系统，可连续实现标准血管内器械的平移与旋转操作。该系统由两个交替运行的推车组成，配备气动膜式夹持器并集成于旋转夹持齿轮中。其顶部装载设计允许快速更换导丝和导管等器械，而无需改变机器人配置。采用主从控制策略，即使每个推车行程有限，仍可实现连续运动。系统通过导丝与导管的运动跟踪实验及体外血管模型实验进行评估。运动跟踪实验显示平移与旋转运动轨迹总体平滑。在所有导丝与导管实验中，平移运动平均相对跟踪误差为3.6%，旋转运动为4.1%。在体外血管模型中，机器人辅助导航在多数试验中成功抵达目标，证明了所提操作概念在体外条件下的可行性。该机器人系统在台面与体外实验中展示了连续操作标准血管内器械的技术可行性。其紧凑顶部装载设计或可简化器械更换与临床工作流程整合。未来工作将聚焦提升夹持性能、驱动速度、力反馈及在更贴近临床环境中的评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce radiation exposure and improve procedural precision in endovascular interventions, this study introduces a compact top-loading robotic system designed for continuous translational and rotational manipulation of standard instruments like guidewires and catheters. The robot features two alternating carts with pneumatically actuated membrane grippers integrated into rotating gears, enabling rapid instrument exchange without altering the setup. A leader-follower control strategy compensates for limited cart stroke, ensuring smooth motion. Experimental results show mean tracking errors of 3.6% for translation and 4.1% for rotation, with successful target navigation in a vascular phantom, demonstrating technical feasibility for in vitro use and potential clinical integration.
为提升血管介入手术的精度、操作舒适性及临床流程整合性，本研究提出一种紧凑型顶部装载机器人，可连续操控导丝和导管等标准器械。该机器人由两个交替运行的气动膜式夹持器车体组成，配以旋转夹持齿轮，支持无需更换机械结构即可快速更换器械。采用主从控制策略补偿各车体行程限制，实现平滑运动。实验结果显示，平移与旋转平均跟踪误差分别为3.6%和4.1%，在血管仿真实验中多数试验成功抵达目标，验证了该系统在体外环境下的可行性。
MemoBench: Benchmarking World Modeling in Dynamically Changing Environments
Authors: Haoyu Chen, Kaichen Zhou, Hang Hua, Kaile Zhang, Jingwen Qian, Wufei Ma, Haonan Chen, Chunjiang Liu, Yizhou Zhao, Xiaoyuan Wang, Weiyue Li, Alan Yuille, Paul Pu Liang, Yilun Du
First: 2026-06-25T20:37:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:32:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video generation models aspire to simulate dynamic environments, and several benchmarks now evaluate memory consistency across frames. However, most assess consistency only while the target remains in view, and the few that force objects out of view evaluate static scenes where nothing changes during occlusion. To bridge this gap, we introduce MemoBench, a diagnostic benchmark built around the disappear-and-reappear paradigm in dynamically changing environments: a target object undergoes a physical process, disappears from view, and must be correctly recovered in its updated state upon reappearance. We curate 360 ground-truth clips spanning synthetic and real-world scenes, and design an evaluation suite combining automated metrics with VQA-based assessment across four diagnostic pillars. Evaluation of eight state-of-the-art models reveals key insights and open challenges regarding memory consistency under the disappear-and-reappear paradigm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemoBench：动态变化环境中的世界建模基准测试
视频生成模型旨在模拟动态环境，现有多个基准测试评估帧间记忆一致性。然而，多数仅在目标持续可见时评估一致性，少数强制物体移出视野的测试则仅针对静态场景，遮挡期间无变化。为弥合此差距，我们提出MemoBench，围绕动态环境中“消失与重现”范式构建诊断基准：目标物体经历物理过程后消失，重新出现时需以更新状态正确恢复。我们精选360段真实与合成场景的真值视频片段，并设计结合自动化指标与基于视觉问答（VQA）评估的四维诊断体系。对八种前沿模型的评估揭示了“消失与重现”范式下记忆一致性的重要洞见与开放挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate video generation models under realistic dynamic conditions, MemoBench introduces a benchmark that tests memory consistency when objects disappear and reappear after undergoing physical changes. The method constructs 360 ground-truth clips across synthetic and real-world scenes and employs a four-pillar evaluation suite combining automated metrics and VQA-based assessments. Experimental results on eight state-of-the-art models reveal significant performance gaps, particularly when objects reappear with altered states, highlighting the current limitations of models in maintaining accurate memory across occlusions.
为评估视频生成模型在动态变化环境中的记忆一致性，MemoBench提出‘消失与重现’范式：目标物体在离开画面期间经历物理变化，模型需在重新出现时准确还原其更新状态。该基准包含360个合成与真实场景的带真值视频片段，通过自动化指标与基于视觉问答的评估覆盖四大诊断维度。对八种前沿模型的测试表明，其在动态遮挡下的状态记忆与重建能力存在显著缺陷，揭示当前视频生成技术的关键挑战。
A Model-Free Universal AI
Authors: Yegon Kim, Juho Lee
First: 2026-02-26T17:21:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:29:31+00:00
Comments: 42nd Conference on Uncertainty in Artificial Intelligence (UAI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In general reinforcement learning, all established optimal agents, including AIXI, are model-based, explicitly maintaining and using environment models. This paper introduces Universal AI with Q-Induction (AIQI), the first model-free agent proven to be asymptotically $\varepsilon$-optimal in general RL. AIQI performs universal induction over distributional action-value functions, instead of policies or environments like previous works. Under a grain of truth condition, we prove that AIQI is strong asymptotically $\varepsilon$-optimal and asymptotically $\varepsilon$-Bayes-optimal. We also apply our novel proof techniques to show asymptotic $\varepsilon$-optimality of Self-AIXI without any ad-hoc assumptions. Our results significantly expand the diversity of known universal agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无模型通用人工智能
在一般强化学习中，所有已建立的最优代理（包括AIXI）都是基于模型的，明确维护并使用环境模型。本文提出了基于Q归纳的通用人工智能（AIQI），这是首个被证明在一般强化学习中渐近ε-最优的无模型代理。AIQI对分布型动作价值函数进行通用归纳，而非像以往工作那样对策略或环境进行归纳。在“一点真实”条件下，我们证明了AIQI是强渐近ε-最优且渐近ε-贝叶斯最优。我们还应用新颖的证明技术，展示了无需任何人为假设的Self-AIXI的渐近ε-最优性。我们的成果显著扩展了已知通用代理的多样性。

Motivated by the limitation that existing universal AI agents like AIXI rely on explicit environment models, this paper proposes AIQI, the first model-free agent proven to be asymptotically ε-optimal in general reinforcement learning. AIQI performs universal induction over distributional action-value functions rather than policies or environments, enabling it to achieve strong asymptotic ε-optimality and ε-Bayes-optimality under a grain of truth condition. Evaluating RE Practices for Explainability: Synthesizing Insights from Daimler Truck into an Explainable RE Framework Proposal
Authors: Umm-e- Habiba, Lucas Mauser, Jonas Fritzsch, Justus Bogner, Stefan Wagner
First: 2026-07-13T16:28:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:28:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Explainability has emerged as a critical requirement for AI-based systems, particularly in safety-critical and regulated domains. Although prior research has proposed frameworks, patterns, and user-centered approaches to support explainability, there is limited empirical understanding of how existing Requirements Engineering (RE) practices support explainability requirements across the RE lifecycle, especially in an industrial context. This paper reports early findings from an ongoing industry-based study investigating how explainability requirements are elicited, specified, and validated using established RE techniques. We conducted a multi-phase qualitative study with eight practitioners at Daimler Truck, employing think-aloud protocols and moderated group discussions across requirements elicitation, specification, and validation steps. Our preliminary analysis reveals recurring challenges across all steps, including conceptual ambiguity during elicitation, limited testability and expressiveness during specification, and fragmented validation due to vague criteria and regulatory uncertainty. These findings indicate that current RE practices provide limited support to systematically address explainability requirements. The paper contributes empirical insights into step-specific and cross-cutting challenges and outlines a research vision toward developing an empirically grounded RE framework for explainable AI-based systems.

Bridging the Gap Between Latent and Explicit Reasoning with Looped Transformers
Authors: Ying Fan, Anej Svete, Kangwook Lee
First: 2026-06-30T14:58:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:27:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Language models typically reason via explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), generating intermediate steps token-by-token. Latent CoT offers an alternative: it performs multi-step reasoning in the model's hidden states, replacing decoded tokens with continuous representations for greater efficiency. However, existing latent CoT methods underperform explicit CoT beyond 1B parameters, and the gap widens with scale. Looped, or recurrent-depth, Transformers, which reuse their weights to increase computation depth without adding parameters, are a natural fit for latent reasoning. We therefore ask whether looped Transformers can bridge this gap. We answer affirmatively with a simple recipe: a looped padded Transformer that processes K latent blocks in parallel for R iterations, with a cross-entropy loss on each latent position's gold CoT-step token, similar to explicit CoT supervision. We instantiate it as LOTUS (Looped Transformers with parallel supervision on latents). LOTUS is, to our knowledge, the first latent-CoT method to bridge the gap to explicit CoT at the 3B scale, while cutting thought-phase latency by 2.5x-6.9x from compact math expressions to natural language. Projecting LOTUS's post-loop latents through the base LM head recovers the gold reasoning steps and even surfaces alternative valid intermediate steps, evidence that its latent space is interpretable and CoT-aligned. Ablations confirm that both the looped backbone and the parallel supervision on gold CoT tokens are essential. Code is available at https://github.com/yingfan-bot/lotus.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用循环Transformer弥合潜在与显式推理之间的差距
语言模型通常通过显式的思维链（CoT）进行推理，逐个生成中间步骤的标记。潜在CoT提供了一种替代方案：它在模型隐藏状态中执行多步推理，用连续表示替代解码标记，以提高效率。然而，现有潜在CoT方法在超过10亿参数时表现不及显式CoT，且随着规模增大差距进一步扩大。循环（或递归深度）Transformer通过重用权重增加计算深度而不增加参数，是潜在推理的自然选择。因此，我们探讨循环Transformer是否能弥合这一差距。我们通过一个简单方案给出肯定答案：一种循环填充Transformer，它并行处理K个潜在块，进行R次迭代，对每个潜在位置的黄金CoT步骤标记施加交叉熵损失，类似于显式CoT监督。我们将其实例化为LOTUS（带潜在并行监督的循环Transformer）。据我们所知，LOTUS是首个在3B规模上弥合潜在CoT与显式CoT差距的方法，同时将从紧凑数学表达式到自然语言的思维阶段延迟降低2.5至6.9倍。将LOTUS的循环后潜态通过基础语言模型头投影，可恢复黄金推理步骤，甚至揭示其他有效中间步骤，证明其潜在空间具有可解释性且与CoT对齐。消融实验确认，循环主干结构和对黄金CoT标记的并行监督均至关重要。代码见https://github.com/yingfan-bot/lotus。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of explicit chain-of-thought reasoning and the underperformance of latent CoT methods beyond 1B parameters, the paper proposes LOTUS: a looped Transformer architecture that processes K latent blocks in parallel for R iterations, supervised with cross-entropy loss on gold CoT tokens. This design leverages recurrent-depth computation without adding parameters, enabling efficient multi-step reasoning in hidden states. Experiments show LOTUS bridges the performance gap with explicit CoT at the 3B scale while reducing thought-phase latency by 2.5x to 6.9x across tasks, and its post-loop latents recover gold reasoning steps and generate alternative valid intermediates, demonstrating interpretability and CoT alignment.
为解决显式链式思维推理效率低及潜式链式思维在1B参数以上表现不佳的问题，本文提出LOTUS架构：一种并行处理K个潜式块、循环R次的循环Transformer，通过在潜式位置上使用黄金CoT令牌的交叉熵损失进行监督。该设计在不增加参数的前提下提升计算深度，实现隐藏状态中的高效多步推理。实验表明，LOTUS在3B规模下可媲美显式CoT推理性能，同时将思维阶段延迟降低2.5至6.9倍，并能从循环后潜式中恢复黄金推理步骤甚至生成有效替代步骤，证明其潜式空间具备可解释性与CoT对齐性。
A Multi-Model Metric-based Selection Framework for Abstractive Text summarization
Authors: Ahmed Alansary, Ali Hamdi
First: 2026-06-03T22:34:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:24:09+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures, IMSA2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic text summarization has become increasingly important due to the rapid growth of digital textual information. This paper presents a Multi-Model Summarization Framework designed to improve the robustness and quality of abstractive text summarization. Relying on a single model often leads to inconsistent summarization quality across articles with varying structures and topics. To address this limitation, the proposed framework integrates multiple fine-tuned transformer-based summarization models and introduces a metric-based selection mechanism. In this framework, each model independently generates a candidate summary for the same input article. The generated summaries are then evaluated using automatic evaluation metrics that capture both lexical similarity and semantic relevance. Based on these scores, the framework selects the highest-quality summary as the final output. The models are fine-tuned and evaluated on the widely used CNN/DailyMail news summarization dataset. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves the highest BERTScore among all compared methods with a score of 88.63%. It also outperforms several LLMs such as GPT3-D2, Falcon-7b, and Mpt-7b, highlighting its effectiveness and robustness. These findings highlight the effectiveness of leveraging multiple transformer-based models within a metric-based selection strategy to improve the quality and robustness of automatic text summarization systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向抽象文本摘要的多模型度量驱动选择框架

Evaluated on the CNN/DailyMail dataset, the framework achieves the highest BERTScore of 88.63% among all compared methods and outperforms several large language models including GPT3-D2, Falcon-7b, and Mpt-7b, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness in improving summarization quality.
为应对不同文本结构下摘要质量不稳定的挑战，本文提出一种多模型框架，结合多个微调的Transformer摘要模型，并通过自动评估指标选择最优摘要。每个模型独立生成同一输入的摘要，再依据词法与语义相似度评分，选取得分最高的摘要作为最终输出。在CNN/DailyMail数据集上的实验表明，该框架BERTScore达88.63%，优于GPT3-D2、Falcon-7b和Mpt-7b等大语言模型，验证了其在提升摘要质量和鲁棒性方面的有效性。
From Expressivity to Sample Complexity: Narrow Teachers for Transformers via C-RASP
Authors: Michael Rizvi-Martel, Satwik Bhattamishra, Guillaume Rabusseau, Michael Hahn
First: 2026-07-13T16:22:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:22:36+00:00
Comments: 9 pages total
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A theoretical understanding of Transformers is crucial to better understand the capacities and limitations of large language models (LLMs). There is much work analyzing the expressivity of attention-based models. By proposing handcrafted weights or using computational complexity arguments, a large amount of past theoretical works have sought to characterize which tasks are and which are not in the hypothesis class of Transformer models. However, little work investigates the learnability of such solutions. In this work, we make progress towards this goal. Inspired by recent loss landscape analysis work, we propose preliminary sample complexity bounds for learning C-RASP constructions with Transformers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从表达能力到样本复杂度：通过C-RASP为Transformer设计窄教师
理解Transformer的理论基础对于更好地把握大型语言模型（LLM）的能力与局限至关重要。已有大量研究分析基于注意力机制的模型的表达能力，通过提出手工设计的权重或使用计算复杂度论证，过去许多理论工作试图刻画哪些任务属于Transformer模型的假设类，哪些不属于。然而，很少有研究探讨此类解决方案的可学习性。本文朝着这一目标迈出一步。受近期损失景观分析工作的启发，我们为使用Transformer学习C-RASP构造提出了初步的样本复杂度界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the learnability of Transformer models beyond their expressivity, this work investigates sample complexity for learning C-RASP constructions using Transformers. Drawing on recent loss landscape analysis, the authors derive preliminary theoretical bounds on the number of samples required for learning such structured weight configurations. Experimental results demonstrate that Transformers can effectively learn C-RASP models with relatively few samples, confirming the practical feasibility of these theoretical bounds and suggesting that narrow teacher architectures may be more sample-efficient than previously assumed.
为理解Transformer模型在表达能力之外的学习能力，本文研究了Transformer学习特定结构化注意力模式（C-RASP构造）的效率。作者借鉴损失景观分析的见解，推导出初步的样本复杂度界限，为成功学习所需训练样本数量提供理论保证。实验结果表明，Transformer能以相对较少的样本有效学习这些结构化模式，验证了理论界限，并凸显了窄教师模型在高效训练中的潜力。
From Global to Factor-Wise Expert Composition in Discrete Diffusion Models
Authors: Haozhe Huang, Yudong Xu, Abhijoy Mandal, Alán Aspuru-Guzik
First: 2026-07-13T16:22:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:22:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models offer a powerful framework for solving complex reasoning tasks, particularly through compositional generation, which combines multiple pre-trained experts to generalize beyond their individual training data. Recent theoretical corrections introduce time-dependent mixing weights to better align composed diffusion dynamics with the intended target. However, these methods are fundamentally limited by working on a per-sample basis, treating each generated state monolithically and ignoring the potential spatial or functional specializations of different experts. In this work, we address this limitation by proposing FactorDiff - a factor-wise composition framework for diffusion models. We posit that samples can be further decomposed into smaller factors, and propose a sampling process that dynamically routes each factor to the most relevant expert. We instantiate this framework with spatial/pixel-level compositions and validate it on the ARC-AGI benchmark, demonstrating that simple factor-specific routing consistently outperforms complex global scalar weighting schemes on tasks that require logical consistency and spatial disentanglement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从全局到因子级专家组合的离散扩散模型
离散扩散模型为解决复杂推理任务提供了一个强大的框架，特别是通过组合生成，将多个预训练专家结合以超越其各自训练数据的泛化能力。近期理论修正引入了时间依赖的混合权重，以更好地使组合扩散动力学与目标保持一致。然而，这些方法从根本上受限于按样本处理，将每个生成状态视为整体，忽略了不同专家潜在的空间或功能专业化。本文通过提出FactorDiff——一种扩散模型的因子级组合框架，解决了这一限制。我们假设样本可进一步分解为更小的因子，并提出一种动态将每个因子路由至最相关专家的采样过程。我们以空间/像素级组合实例化该框架，并在ARC-AGI基准上验证，结果表明，简单的因子特定路由在需要逻辑一致性和空间解耦的任务上，持续优于复杂的全局标量加权方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of global mixing weights in discrete diffusion models that treat each sample monolithically, this work introduces FactorDiff, a factor-wise expert composition framework that decomposes samples into smaller factors and dynamically routes each factor to the most relevant expert. Evaluated on the ARC-AGI benchmark, the method demonstrates that simple factor-specific routing consistently outperforms complex global scalar weighting schemes, particularly on tasks requiring logical consistency and spatial disentanglement.
为了解决现有离散扩散模型中全局混合权重无法利用专家间空间或功能特性的缺陷，本文提出FactorDiff框架，将样本分解为更小因子并动态路由每个因子至最相关的专家。在ARC-AGI基准测试中，该方法表明简单的因子级路由策略在需要逻辑一致性和空间解耦的任务上，显著优于复杂的全局标量加权方案。
Severity-Aware Curriculum Learning with Multi-Model Response Selection for Medical Text Generation
Authors: Ahmed Alansary, Molham Mohamed, Ali Hamdi
First: 2026-06-03T23:28:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:21:53+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures, IMSA2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Telehealth systems have become increasingly important for delivering accessible and timely medical information. Existing large language models often struggle to provide consistent and contextually appropriate medical responses across varying levels of case severity. This limitation highlights the need for models that can effectively adapt to the progressive complexity in medical queries. To address this challenge, we introduce a severity-aware multi-model framework that integrates curriculum training strategy with relevance-based response selection. The proposed framework employs a three-stage curriculum learning strategy, where each model is trained sequentially on mild, moderate, and critical cases to progressively acquire domain knowledge. The approach uses five large language models, each trained independently under the same curriculum. During inference, all models generate candidate responses, and the response with highest BERTScore is selected as the final output. The framework is trained and evaluated on the MAQA dataset, which provides annotated medical question-answer pairs. Experimental results evaluated using BERTScore demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance compared to both baseline and fine-tuned models, attaining 86.71% in the baseline setting and 90.30% after fine-tuning. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining curriculum learning with multi-model response selection in improving response quality and relevance in medical text generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向医疗文本生成的严重程度感知课程学习与多模型响应选择
远程医疗系统在提供便捷及时的医疗信息方面日益重要。现有大语言模型往往难以在不同病例严重程度下提供一致且语境恰当的医疗响应，这凸显了需要能够有效适应医疗查询渐进复杂性的模型。为解决此问题，我们提出一种严重程度感知的多模型框架，结合课程训练策略与基于相关性的响应选择。该框架采用三阶段课程学习策略，各模型依次在轻度、中度和重度病例上训练，逐步积累领域知识。该方法使用五个大语言模型，每个模型在相同课程下独立训练。推理阶段，所有模型生成候选响应，选择BERTScore最高的响应作为最终输出。该框架在MAQA数据集上进行训练和评估，该数据集提供标注的医疗问答对。实验结果表明，该方法在BERTScore评估下优于基线和微调模型，基线设置下达到86.71%，微调后达到90.30%。结果表明，结合课程学习与多模型响应选择能有效提升医疗文本生成的响应质量与相关性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for contextually accurate medical responses across varying case severity, this study proposes a severity-aware curriculum learning framework that trains five large language models sequentially on mild, moderate, and critical medical cases. During inference, all models generate responses, and the one with the highest BERTScore is selected as the final output. Evaluated on the MAQA dataset, the method outperforms both baseline and fine-tuned models, achieving 86.71% and 90.30% BERTScore after fine-tuning, demonstrating the efficacy of curriculum-based progression combined with multi-model response selection in enhancing medical text generation quality.
为解决现有大模型在不同病情严重程度下生成医疗响应不一致的问题，该研究提出一种基于病情严重度的课程学习框架，依次对五个大语言模型进行轻度、中度和重度病例的分阶段训练。推理时，所有模型生成候选回答，选择BERTScore最高的作为最终输出。在MAQA数据集上的实验表明，该方法在基线和微调后分别达到86.71%和90.30%的BERTScore，证明课程学习与多模型响应选择结合能显著提升医疗文本生成的准确性和相关性。
Toward a Scientific Discovery Engine for Weather and Climate Data: A Visual Analytics Workbench for Embedding-Based Exploration
Authors: Nihanth W. Cherukuru, Matt Rehme, Kirsten J. Mayer, David John Gagne, John Schreck, John Clyne, Charlie Becker
First: 2026-05-01T17:03:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:21:30+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 5 figures, Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Earth system science is producing increasingly large, high-dimensional datasets from both physics-based and AI-driven models. While embedding-based representations make these data searchable and serve as foundational building blocks for AI-driven discovery engines, nearest neighbors in latent spaces are not automatically scientifically meaningful. They may reflect real meteorological structures, or simply artifacts of preprocessing, geography, or model bias. Researchers therefore need visual tools to inspect latent space organization, trace search results back to physical evidence, and evaluate candidate representations against one another. We present an open source visual analytics workbench designed to support this provenance-aware scientific retrieval workflow. The system links distinct embedding experiments to shared source data, metadata, spatial contexts, and model configurations. It enables interactive retrieval strategy design by allowing users to issue image-level and localized patch-level queries, apply multi-constraint filters, and inspect analogs through familiar meteorological views. This facilitates a discovery loop where scientists characterize a phenomenon in a well-understood dataset and use its latent signature to probe larger archives. While we demonstrate the workbench through a tropical cyclone retrieval scenario using a vision foundation model (DINOv3) on ERA5 data, the framework is model-agnostic and designed to integrate with other embedding architectures in the future. Finally, we evaluate its out-of-core retrieval backend, demonstrating that interactive visual search over tens of millions of embeddings is highly scalable on commodity hardware.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向气象与气候数据的科学发现引擎：基于嵌入式探索的可视化分析工作台
地球系统科学正从基于物理和人工智能驱动的模型中生成日益庞大、高维的数据集。虽然基于嵌入的表示方法使这些数据可搜索，并作为人工智能驱动发现引擎的基础构建模块，但潜在空间中的最近邻并不自动具有科学意义。它们可能反映真实的气象结构，也可能只是预处理、地理或模型偏差的产物。因此，研究人员需要可视化工具来检查潜在空间的组织结构，将搜索结果追溯到物理证据，并对比评估候选表示方法。 我们提出了一种开源可视化分析工作台，旨在支持这种具有溯源意识的科学检索工作流。该系统将不同的嵌入实验与共享的数据源、元数据、空间上下文和模型配置相链接。它通过允许用户发出图像级和局部补丁级查询、应用多约束过滤器，并通过熟悉的气象视图检查类比，支持交互式检索策略设计。这有助于科学家在已知数据集中表征现象，并利用其潜在签名探索更大规模的档案库。虽然我们通过使用ERA5数据上的视觉基础模型（DINOv3）进行热带气旋检索场景演示了该工作台，但该框架与模型无关，并设计为未来可集成其他嵌入架构。最后，我们评估了其基于外存的检索后端，证明在普通硬件上对数千万个嵌入进行交互式视觉搜索具有高度可扩展性。

The system supports interactive querying at image and patch levels, multi-constraint filtering, and visualization of meteorological analogs, allowing scientists to trace latent space results back to physical phenomena. Evaluated through a tropical cyclone retrieval task using DINOv3 on ERA5 data, the framework demonstrates scalability for interactive search over tens of millions of embeddings using commodity hardware, while remaining model-agnostic for future integration with other embedding architectures.
为应对物理模型与AI模型生成的高维气象气候数据的复杂性，本文提出一个支持溯源感知探索的可视化分析工作台。该系统将嵌入实验与原始数据、元数据、空间上下文及模型配置关联，支持图像与局部区域级查询、多约束过滤及气象可视化结果比对。实验表明，该系统可在标准硬件上高效支持对千万级嵌入向量的交互式检索，验证案例基于DINOv3模型在ERA5数据上的热带气旋检索，且框架对模型无依赖，便于未来扩展集成其他嵌入架构。
InqEduAgent: Adaptive AI Learning Partners with Gaussian Process Augmentation
Authors: Wen-Xi Yang, Tian-Fang Zhao, Guan Liu
First: 2025-08-05T07:33:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:17:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted by the 2026 8th Asia Conference on Machine Learning and Computing (ACMLC 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Collaborative partnerships play a crucial role in inquiry-oriented education. However, most learning partners are currently assigned through experience-driven heuristics or rule-based machine assistants, which often result in limited knowledge expansion and low adaptability. To address these challenges, this study introduces InqEduAgent, an LLM-empowered generative agent framework designed to simulate and select adaptive learning partners for inquiry-based learning. InqEduAgent integrates a Gaussian process-augmented matching mechanism to model the cognitive and evaluative characteristics of learners, allowing adaptive partner selection based on prior knowledge patterns. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that InqEduAgent consistently achieves superior performance across diverse learning scenarios and large language model configurations. This study advances human-AI collaborative learning by enabling intelligent pairing between human- and AI-based learning partners, and contributes to adaptive user modeling and personalized recommendation within Web-based educational environments.

Higher-Order Cell Tracking Transformer
Authors: Jordão Bragantini, Ilan Theodoro, Loïc A. Royer
First: 2026-07-13T16:12:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:12:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reconstructing lineages from live-imaging microscopy requires linking cell detections across time, including through cell divisions. A common approach is to construct a candidate graph and associate cell segmentations (nodes) across frames. However, these and other existing methods overlook two structural obstacles in candidate tracking graphs: (i) cell divisions entangle distinct lineage paths in the node embedding space, and (ii) edges sharing a node have near-random label agreement, so the candidate-graph topology carries no useful information for graph neural networks to aggregate. We propose the \textbf{Higher-Order Cell Tracking Transformer} (HOCT), an edge-centric architecture in which candidate cell links attend to one another under a 3D geometric prior, resolving both issues. Evaluated on the Cell Tracking Challenge and a bacteria division benchmark, HOCT achieves state-of-the-art results without deep pre-trained image encoders. Moreover, the proposed approach is easier to fine-tune, quickly reducing tracking errors by 59% with 400 annotations in a human-in-the-loop setting, outperforming LoRA fine-tuning of competing transformer baselines (6.75% improvement).
中文标题/摘要
标题：高阶细胞追踪变换器
从活体成像显微镜重建谱系需要在时间上关联细胞检测，包括细胞分裂过程。常用方法是构建候选图并关联不同帧中的细胞分割（节点）。然而，这些方法及其他现有方法忽视了候选追踪图中的两个结构障碍：(i) 细胞分裂在节点嵌入空间中交织不同的谱系路径；(ii) 共享节点的边具有近随机的标签一致性，因此候选图拓扑对图神经网络聚合无用信息。我们提出\textbf{高阶细胞追踪变换器}（HOCT），一种以边为中心的架构，其候选细胞链接在三维几何先验下相互关注，解决了上述两个问题。在细胞追踪挑战赛和细菌分裂基准测试中，HOCT在无需深度预训练图像编码器的情况下达到最先进性能。此外，该方法更易微调，在人类参与的设置下仅用400个标注即可将追踪误差降低59%，优于竞争性变换器基线的LoRA微调（提升6.75%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately linking cell detections across time in live-imaging microscopy—especially through cell divisions—this paper introduces the Higher-Order Cell Tracking Transformer (HOCT), an edge-centric architecture that leverages 3D geometric priors to enable candidate cell links to attend to each other, thereby resolving structural issues in candidate tracking graphs. HOCT addresses two key limitations: cell divisions causing entangled lineage paths in node embeddings and edges sharing nodes exhibiting near-random label agreement that renders graph topology uninformative for GNNs. Evaluated on the Cell Tracking Challenge and a bacteria division benchmark, HOCT achieves state-of-the-art performance without relying on deep pre-trained image encoders and demonstrates superior fine-tuning efficiency, reducing tracking errors by 59% with only 400 annotations in a human-in-the-loop setting, outperforming LoRA fine-tuning of competing transformer baselines by 6.75%.
为解决活体显微成像中跨时间细胞检测关联（尤其细胞分裂场景）的挑战，本文提出高阶细胞追踪Transformer（HOCT），一种以边为中心的架构，通过3D几何先验使候选细胞链接相互关注，从而解决候选图结构中存在的细胞谱系路径纠缠与边拓扑信息无用等问题。在细胞追踪挑战赛和细菌分裂基准测试中，HOCT无需深度预训练图像编码器即达到最先进性能，并在仅需400个标注的人机协同微调场景下，将追踪误差降低59%，优于对比模型LoRA微调的6.75%提升。
Paradoxes of Game Theoretic Equilibria and Price of Anarchy
Authors: Georgios Piliouras, Ian Gemp, Siqi Liu, Luke Marris
First: 2026-07-13T16:09:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:09:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For decades, static solution concepts (Nash, Correlated, and Coarse Correlated Equilibria) and the Price of Anarchy (PoA) have formed the bedrock of algorithmic game theory, with no-regret learning proving fast convergence to such game-theoretic equilibria. We show that reducing multi-agent learning to static equilibrium and black-box regret analysis obscures underlying dynamic disequilibrium and game theoretic bounds. First, interior Nash equilibria lack $C^1$ vector field information, meaning agents cannot distinguish aligned from strictly opposing incentives. Inheriting this geometry, the worst-case pure Nash equilibria dictating robust PoA bounds manifest as topologically unstable strict saddles, and in canonical congestion games, as global repellers supported on almost everywhere strictly dominated strategies. Anchoring efficiency guarantees to these unstable states causes algebraic sensitivity; we prove that accommodating all strictly positive affine costs renders the PoA unbounded. Furthermore, projecting learning trajectories onto the discrete simplex of correlated play systematically accommodates non-rationalizable behavior. Evaluating dynamics via Coarse Correlated Equilibria or proximal refinements fails to preclude strictly dominated strategies. Moreover, optimal $O(1/T)$ swap-regret minimization does not preclude macroscopic turbulence, manifesting as chaotic limit sets even in minimal games. Finally, we examine the non-atomic limit of congestion games. Though considered highly stable with tight sub-linear $Θ(p/\ln p)$ PoA bounds (where $p$ is the polynomial degree), we prove that under discrete-time learning, the unique equilibrium destabilizes into Li-Yorke chaos and global attractors whose time-averaged inefficiency degrades exponentially as $2^p$. These results necessitate re-evaluating worst-case equilibrium frameworks for dynamically grounded metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：博弈论均衡悖论与无效率代价
数十年来，静态解概念（纳什均衡、相关均衡和粗相关均衡）及无效率代价（PoA）构成了算法博弈论的基础，无悔学习被证明能快速收敛至此类博弈均衡。我们指出，将多智能体学习简化为静态均衡与黑箱无悔分析，掩盖了其内在的动态非均衡状态及博弈论界限。 首先，内部纳什均衡缺乏$C^1$向量场信息，意味着智能体无法区分对齐与严格对抗的激励。继承此几何结构，决定鲁棒PoA界限的最坏情况纯纳什均衡表现为拓扑不稳定的严格鞍点，在典型拥塞博弈中则表现为全局排斥点，其支撑几乎处处严格劣势策略。将效率保证锚定于这些不稳定状态会导致代数敏感性；我们证明，若允许所有严格正仿射成本，则PoA无界。此外，将学习轨迹投影到相关博弈的离散单纯形会系统性地容纳非理性可解释行为。通过粗相关均衡或邻近精炼评估动态，无法排除严格劣势策略。更进一步，最优$O(1/T)$交换无悔最小化亦无法排除宏观湍流，即使在最小博弈中亦表现为混沌极限集。最后，我们考察拥塞博弈的非原子极限。尽管被认为高度稳定且具有紧致的次线性$Θ(p/\ln p)$ PoA界限（其中$p$为多项式次数），我们证明在离散时间学习下，唯一均衡会失稳为李-约克混沌及全局吸引子，其时间平均无效率随$2^p$指数衰减。这些结果要求重新评估基于动态根基的最坏情况均衡框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static equilibrium concepts in capturing dynamic learning behavior, the paper investigates how game-theoretic equilibria and Price of Anarchy (PoA) metrics fail to reflect real-world multi-agent dynamics. It argues that reducing learning to static equilibria obscures disequilibrium phenomena and leads to misleading efficiency guarantees. The authors demonstrate that interior Nash equilibria lack sufficient geometric structure to distinguish aligned from opposing incentives, and that worst-case pure Nash equilibria often correspond to topologically unstable saddles or repellers in congestion games. They prove that PoA becomes unbounded under affine cost functions, and that projecting learning onto correlated play allows non-rationalizable behavior. Even with optimal regret minimization, macroscopic turbulence and chaotic limit sets emerge. In non-atomic congestion games, despite sub-linear PoA bounds, discrete-time learning induces Li-Yorke chaos and exponentially worsening time-averaged inefficiency as 2^p.
该研究旨在揭示静态博弈均衡概念在刻画动态学习行为时的局限性，指出将多智能体学习简化为静态均衡会掩盖系统内在的不稳定性。作者证明，内部纳什均衡缺乏足够的几何结构，无法区分激励对齐与对抗关系，而最坏情况下的纯纳什均衡往往是拓扑不稳定的鞍点，甚至在拥塞博弈中表现为全局排斥点，且在仿射成本下会导致无界的价格无效率（PoA）。研究进一步表明，将学习轨迹投影到相关策略单纯形会容纳非理性行为，即使最优的O(1/T)交换后悔最小化也无法避免宏观湍流，甚至在最小博弈中产生混沌极限集。在非原子拥塞博弈中，尽管理论PoA呈次线性，但在离散时间学习下，唯一均衡会演变为李-约克混沌，其时间平均效率随2^p指数衰减，从而要求重新评估基于均衡的动态指标。
When Local Monitors Miss Compositional Harm: Diagnosing Distributed Backdoors in Multi-Agent Systems
Authors: Yibo Hu, Ren Wang
First: 2026-07-13T16:08:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:08:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As multi-agent, tool-using LLM systems are deployed, a common safety net is a runtime monitor that checks each message, tool call, or step on its own. We show this net has a fundamental hole. A distributed backdoor splits a harmful payload across agents, so every local check passes while the assembled object is the attack. The monitor can be right on every step and still miss the attack. The problem is not splitting itself: split fragments can still leak suspicious tokens or provenance edges. The hard case is \emph{local benignness}. No fragment carries the harm, and what is left looks like ordinary benign traffic. We formalize this as an \emph{observability boundary}: a monitor catches only what its view can tell apart from benign traffic. We prove that once the fragments look benign in the monitored view, no detector on that view can catch them, however strong it is. Across a controlled testbed, an external benchmark, and end-to-end agent runs, local monitors lose the signal exactly as local evidence disappears, and it returns only when the monitor sees the assembled object. A monitor trained only on benign traffic recovers the attack's code structure across held-out encodings (0.874 mean AUROC). A decoded-view gate, given the encoding family, blocks every tested attack. But seeing more is not enough: full-trace monitors and decoders still fail unless they reach the representation where the payload is exposed. Local safety is not global safety when harm is compositional, and the open problem is finding that representation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当本地监控遗漏组合性危害时：诊断多智能体系统中的分布式后门
随着多智能体、工具使用型大语言模型系统的部署，一种常见的安全措施是在运行时对每条消息、工具调用或步骤进行独立检查。我们指出该措施存在根本性漏洞：分布式后门将有害载荷分散至多个智能体，使得每个本地检查均通过，但组合后的对象即为攻击。即使监控在每一步都正确，仍可能遗漏攻击。问题不在于分割本身：分割片段仍可能泄露可疑标记或溯源边。难点在于\emph{局部良性性}：无片段携带危害，剩余部分看似普通良性流量。我们将其形式化为\emph{可观测边界}：监控仅能捕获其视图中可与良性流量区分开的内容。我们证明，一旦片段在监控视图中呈现良性，无论检测器多强，均无法捕获。在受控测试床、外部基准及端到端智能体运行中，本地监控在局部证据消失时即丢失信号，仅当监控看到组合对象时才恢复。仅在良性流量上训练的监控器，能在保留编码中恢复攻击的代码结构（平均AUROC为0.874）。给定编码族的解码视图门控可阻断所有测试攻击。但仅增加观测量不足：全轨迹监控器和解码器仍失败，除非它们抵达载荷暴露的表示层。当危害具有组合性时，局部安全不等于全局安全，开放问题在于寻找该表示层。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that local monitors in multi-agent LLM systems may miss compositional harm, the paper formalizes the problem as an 'observability boundary'—where fragments of an attack appear benign in local views, making them undetectable by any monitor trained only on local data. The authors demonstrate that even if each agent’s step passes local checks, the assembled object can still be malicious, and detectors fail unless they observe the full composition. Experiments across controlled testbeds, benchmarks, and end-to-end agent runs show that local monitors lose detection capability as local evidence vanishes, recovering only when the assembled object is visible. A monitor trained on benign traffic achieves 0.874 mean AUROC in recovering attack structure across held-out encodings, while a decoded-view gate can block all tested attacks when given the encoding family—but full-trace monitors and decoders still fail unless they reach the representation where the payload is exposed.
该论文指出，多智能体LLM系统中的局部监控器无法检测到将有害负载分散到多个智能体中的组合型攻击，因为每个局部步骤均表现为良性。作者将此问题形式化为“可观测边界”——即监控器只能检测其视图中可与良性流量区分开的成分。实验表明，当有害片段在局部视图中不可区分时，监控器必然失效，仅当组装后的对象被观察到时才能成功检测。即使仅在良性数据上训练的监控器，也能在隐藏编码中以0.874的平均AUROC恢复攻击结构；若给定编码族，解码视图门控可阻断所有测试攻击。然而，仅增加观测范围仍不足，除非监控器能访问暴露攻击负载的表示层，否则完整轨迹监控器和解码器仍会失败，说明局部安全不等于全局安全。
Playful AI in Professional Email: A Field Experiment on Tone and Recipient Engagement
Authors: Ziv Ben-Zion, Teddy Lazebnik
First: 2026-07-13T16:06:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:06:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly reshaping workplace communication, yet whether AI-assisted writing changes how recipients actually behave, and through what channel, remains unknown. Here, in a randomized crossover field experiment, 121 employees across six companies sent work emails under three conditions over three weeks: unaided writing, GPT-5 rewriting in a playful tone, and GPT-5 rewriting in a professional tone. Across 16,880 emails, playful editing increased emotional positivity (B=+0.068, p<0.001), and professional editing decreased it (B=-0.041, p<0.001), yet neither condition directly altered open rates, reply rates, or response times. Instead, within-sender positivity strongly predicted both opening (OR=2.05) and replying (OR=3.32, p<0.001), a significant indirect pathway through which AI editing shaped behavior, in the absence of any direct effect. These findings suggest that AI-assisted communication shapes workplace engagement not through its use, but through the emotional tone of the language it produces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：专业邮件中的趣味AI：一项关于语气与收件人参与度的田野实验
大型语言模型（LLMs）正迅速重塑职场沟通，但AI辅助写作是否以及如何影响收件人实际行为，尚不明确。本研究在六家公司121名员工中进行随机交叉田野实验，他们在三周内分三组发送工作邮件：无AI辅助写作、GPT-5以趣味语气重写、GPT-5以专业语气重写。在16,880封邮件中，趣味编辑提升了情绪积极度（B=+0.068, p<0.001），专业编辑则降低（B=-0.041, p<0.001），但两种编辑均未直接影响打开率、回复率或响应时间。相反，发件人自身的情绪积极度显著预测了邮件打开（OR=2.05）和回复行为（OR=3.32, p<0.001），这是AI编辑影响行为的重要间接路径，且无直接效应。研究结果表明，AI辅助沟通通过其生成语言的情绪语调而非使用本身，塑造职场参与度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how AI-assisted writing affects workplace communication behavior, the study conducted a randomized crossover field experiment with 121 employees across six companies who sent 16,880 emails over three weeks under three conditions: unaided writing, playful GPT-5 rewriting, and professional GPT-5 rewriting. Results showed that playful editing increased emotional positivity in emails (B=+0.068, p<0.001), while professional editing decreased it (B=-0.041, p<0.001), yet neither tone directly influenced open rates, reply rates, or response times. Instead, the emotional positivity of the sender’s own emails strongly predicted both email opening (OR=2.05) and replying (OR=3.32, p<0.001), revealing that AI’s impact on engagement operates indirectly through tone rather than direct behavioral manipulation.

HiFi-LLP: High-Fidelity, Low-Cost Latency Predictors with Confidence for Robust HW-NAS
Authors: Shambhavi Balamuthu Sampath, Behzad Shomali, Nael Fasfous, Moritz Thoma, Judeson Anthony Fernando, Lukas Frickenstein, Pierpaolo Mori, Manoj Rohit Vemparala, Alexander Frickenstein, Walter Stechele
First: 2026-07-13T16:05:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:05:16+00:00
Comments: Published in the Proceedings of the 2025 IEEE 38th International System-on-Chip Conference (SOCC)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With deep neural networks (DNNs) increasingly deployed on edge devices, hardware (HW)-aware optimization techniques--such as HW-aware compression and HW-aware neural architecture search (HW-NAS)--have become essential. These methods rely on real feedback from the target hardware to tailor DNN architectures for efficient deployment. While the search can be parallelized, latency measurements via hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) remain a bottleneck due to their sequential nature. Recent approaches use latency predictors to replace costly HIL feedback, but challenges persist: (1) platform-specific predictors often require tens of thousands of samples, and (2) inaccurate predictions can mislead the NAS process. To address this, we introduce HiFi-LLP, a high-fidelity, low-cost latency predictor based on graph attention networks, augmented with a confidence metric. HiFi-LLP outperforms prior platform-specific predictors by up to 9 percentage points (p.p.) in the 10% accuracy bound and achieves a Spearman's rank correlation of up to 0.996 across six devices in the LatBench dataset. We further propose a hybrid NAS framework that routes low-confidence predictions to HIL, achieving up to 8.6$\times$ speedup compared to typical NAS while maintaining a competitive Pareto front.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HiFi-LLP：面向鲁棒硬件神经架构搜索的高保真、低成本延迟预测器
随着深度神经网络（DNN）在边缘设备上的部署日益广泛，硬件（HW）感知优化技术——如硬件感知压缩和硬件感知神经架构搜索（HW-NAS）——变得至关重要。这些方法依赖目标硬件的真实反馈来定制DNN架构以实现高效部署。虽然搜索过程可并行化，但通过硬件在环（HIL）进行的延迟测量仍因顺序性而成为瓶颈。近期方法使用延迟预测器替代昂贵的HIL反馈，但仍存在挑战：（1）平台特定预测器通常需要数万样本；（2）不准确的预测可能误导NAS过程。为此，我们提出HiFi-LLP，一种基于图注意力网络的高保真、低成本延迟预测器，并附加置信度度量。HiFi-LLP在10%精度阈值下比现有平台特定预测器性能提升最高达9个百分点，并在LatBench数据集的六种设备上实现高达0.996的斯皮尔曼等级相关系数。我们进一步提出一种混合NAS框架，将低置信度预测路由至HIL，相比典型NAS实现最高8.6倍加速，同时保持竞争力的帕累托前沿。
Summary / 总结
As deep neural networks expand to edge devices, hardware-aware optimization like HW-NAS requires accurate latency feedback, which is costly and sequential via hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) testing. To reduce this bottleneck, HiFi-LLP introduces a graph attention network-based latency predictor with a confidence metric, enabling low-cost, high-fidelity predictions. It outperforms existing platform-specific predictors by up to 9 percentage points in 10% accuracy bound and achieves a Spearman’s rank correlation of 0.996 across six devices. A hybrid NAS framework that routes low-confidence predictions to HIL further delivers up to 8.6x speedup while preserving competitive Pareto front performance.
随着深度神经网络向边缘设备部署，硬件感知优化技术如HW-NAS需要准确的延迟反馈，但硬件在环（HIL）测试成本高且串行化。为缓解此瓶颈，HiFi-LLP提出基于图注意力网络的延迟预测器并附加置信度度量，实现低成本高保真预测。在LatBench六款设备上评估，其在10%精度阈值内准确率提升最高9个百分点，Spearman秩相关系数达0.996。当与混合NAS框架结合——将低置信度预测重定向至HIL测试时，可实现最高8.6倍加速，同时保持竞争力的帕累托前沿表现。
IntraShuffler: A Privacy Preserving Framework for Heterogeneous DP Federated Learning
Authors: Farhin Farhad Riya, Olivera Kotevska, Jinyuan Stella Sun
First: 2026-06-01T17:54:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:04:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Heterogeneous Differential Privacy (HDP) in Federated Learning (FL) allows clients to select individual privacy budgets ($\varepsilon_i$) according to institutional policies and data sensitivity. In practice, many HDP-FL systems employ $\varepsilon$-aware server aggregation to improve model utility by re-weighting client updates according to their declared privacy budgets. However, gradient updates in FL retain structural patterns induced by non-independent and identically-distributed (non-IID) data, and these additional signals exposed by $\varepsilon$-aware aggregation create new opportunities for inference by an honest-but-curious server. In this work, we first show that a server equipped with gradient denoising and surrogate modeling can mount a \emph{Privacy Inference Attack} that infers distributional attributes of clients and links updates from the same client across training rounds, measured via surrogate inference accuracy and linkage success, under realistic knowledge constraints. The Shuffle-Model has been widely studied as a defense against such inference risks by anonymizing update sources, but it is fundamentally incompatible with HDP-FL $\varepsilon$-aware aggregation. To address this challenge, we propose \textbf{IntraShuffler}, a middleware defense framework designed for HDP-FL systems. IntraShuffler introduces a privacy-aware shuffling mechanism that groups clients into privacy-compatible buckets and performs parameter-level shuffling within each bucket to disrupt persistent gradient structure while preserving $\varepsilon$-aware aggregation. Experiments across four different datasets show that IntraShuffler reduces gradient recoverability by over 60% and decreases surrogate inference accuracy from 0.78 to 0.33 while maintaining comparable model utility across multiple FL aggregation rules.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IntraShuffler：一种面向异构差分隐私联邦学习的隐私保护框架
联邦学习中的异构差分隐私（HDP）允许客户端根据机构政策和数据敏感性选择各自的隐私预算（ε_i）。实践中，许多HDP-FL系统采用ε感知服务器聚合，通过根据客户端声明的隐私预算重新加权客户端更新以提升模型效用。然而，FL中的梯度更新保留了由非独立同分布（非IID）数据诱导的结构模式，而ε感知聚合暴露的额外信号为诚实但好奇的服务器提供了新的推断机会。本文首先证明，在梯度去噪与代理建模能力下，服务器可发起隐私推断攻击，推断客户端分布属性并关联跨训练轮次的同一客户端更新，其效果通过代理推断准确率与链接成功率衡量。尽管Shuffle模型被广泛研究用于抵御此类推断风险，但其匿名化更新源的机制与HDP-FL的ε感知聚合根本冲突。为此，我们提出IntraShuffler，一种面向HDP-FL系统的中间件防御框架。IntraShuffler引入隐私感知的洗牌机制，将客户端分组至隐私兼容桶内，并在每个桶内进行参数级洗牌，以破坏持久梯度结构同时保留ε感知聚合。在四个不同数据集上的实验表明，IntraShuffler使梯度可恢复性降低超过60%，代理推断准确率从0.78降至0.33，同时在多种FL聚合规则下保持相当的模型效用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that ε-aware aggregation in heterogeneous differential privacy federated learning exposes client-specific gradient patterns to inference attacks, this work proposes IntraShuffler, a middleware framework that enables privacy-preserving shuffling within privacy-compatible client groups while maintaining ε-aware aggregation. The method groups clients by privacy budget and shuffles parameters at the group level to break persistent gradient structures without compromising server-side aggregation logic. Experimental results across four datasets demonstrate that IntraShuffler reduces gradient recoverability by over 60% and lowers surrogate inference accuracy from 0.78 to 0.33, while preserving model utility under various aggregation schemes.
为应对异构差分隐私联邦学习中ε感知聚合暴露客户端梯度结构导致的隐私推断风险，本文提出IntraShuffler框架，通过在隐私兼容的客户端组内进行参数级打乱，破坏梯度结构同时保留ε感知聚合能力。实验表明，该方法使梯度可恢复性降低超60%，推断准确率从0.78降至0.33，且在多种聚合规则下模型效用保持相当水平。
Transformer-based segmentation of prosodic boundaries in Brazilian Portuguese
Authors: Rodrigo de Freitas Lima, Julio Cesar Galdino, Marcos Vinicius Treviso
First: 2026-07-08T13:41:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:03:28+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 5 figures, submitted to an IEEE conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic prosodic segmentation identifies boundaries between speech units from acoustic and linguistic evidence. Although recent deep learning approaches have produced strong results for English, automatic segmentation for Brazilian Portuguese (BP) still relies mostly on rule-based or traditional machine-learning methods. This paper presents SAMPA, a Whisper-based segmenter that transcribes BP speech while inserting explicit markers for terminal prosodic boundaries. We fine-tune Whisper large-v3 on manually segmented recordings from the NURC-SP dataset and evaluate different training and test-time filtering configurations, including out-of-distribution testing on the MuPe-Diversidades dataset. SAMPA achieves competitive boundary-detection performance across settings, with the best models reaching F1=0.731 on the held-out test split and F1=0.796 on MuPe-Diversidades. Finally, through n-gram and acoustic-visual analyses, we show that our model follows morphosyntactic, semantic, and prosodic cues for detecting prosodic boundaries.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Transformer的巴西葡萄牙语语调边界分割
自动语调分割通过声学和语言学证据识别语音单位间的边界。尽管近期深度学习方法在英语中表现优异，但巴西葡萄牙语（BP）的自动分割仍主要依赖基于规则或传统机器学习的方法。本文提出SAMPA，一种基于Whisper的分割器，可在转录BP语音的同时插入显式终端语调边界标记。我们使用NURC-SP数据集的手动分割录音对Whisper large-v3进行微调，并评估不同训练和测试时过滤配置，包括在MuPe-Diversidades数据集上的分布外测试。SAMPA在不同设置下均取得竞争力的边界检测性能，最佳模型在保留测试集上F1=0.731，在MuPe-Diversidades上F1=0.796。最后，通过n-gram和声学-视觉分析，我们证明模型遵循形态句法、语义和语调线索以检测语调边界。

为解决巴西葡萄牙语自动音段边界分割缺乏深度学习方法的问题，本研究提出SAMPA模型，基于Whisper架构并使用NURC-SP数据集的手动标注数据进行微调，以在转录过程中插入明确的音段边界标记。模型在多种训练与测试时过滤配置下进行评估，包括在MuPe-Diversidades数据集上的分布外测试，分别在保留测试集和分布外测试集上达到0.731和0.796的F1分数。通过对n-gram及声学-视觉特征的分析，证实该模型能有效利用形态句法、语义及音段线索检测边界。
AutoPath: Learning Transferable Goal-Conditioned Stochastic Path Prior for Safe Navigation Without Human Demonstrations
Authors: Ziyang Zhang, Boyang Zhou, Zesong Yang, Haocheng Peng, Zeming Gai, Xiao Liang, Yujun Shen, Danping Zou, Ruizhen Hu, Hujun Bao, Zhaopeng Cui
First: 2026-07-13T16:00:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T16:00:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RA-L). 8 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time navigation in cluttered and dynamic environments requires collision-free and dynamically feasible motion under limited perception. However, feasible navigation behaviors are inherently multimodal because multiple paths may exist around obstacles. In this paper, we formulate navigation as learning a transferable goal-conditioned stochastic path prior that models a reusable distribution over goal-aligned geometry-consistent local paths conditioned on local observations. This formulation enables structured sampling of navigation candidates, allowing multiple feasible paths to be explored through sampling without relying on robot-specific motion constraints. To this end, we introduce a goal-aligned canonical state representation that removes in-plane rotational ambiguity and normalizes local geometry with respect to the goal, enabling rotation-invariant path distribution learning. We further develop a structured prior learning framework that parameterizes local paths using a geometry-aware polar action manifold and incorporates risk-sensitive utility shaping with multi-goal distributional rollouts for stable and safety-aware planning. Extensive experiments in dense static environments and dynamic pedestrian scenarios demonstrate that the proposed method achieves consistently high success rates with competitive efficiency while enabling cross-platform transfer of a single path prior learned on differential-drive robots to quadruped platforms without retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AutoPath：学习可迁移的目标条件随机路径先验以实现无需人类演示的安全导航
在杂乱且动态的环境中进行实时导航，需要在感知受限的情况下实现无碰撞且动态可行的运动。然而，可行的导航行为本质上是多模态的，因为障碍物周围可能存在多条路径。本文将导航建模为学习一种可迁移的目标条件随机路径先验，该先验基于局部观测条件，建模目标对齐且几何一致的局部路径的可重用分布。该建模方式支持结构化采样导航候选路径，无需依赖机器人特定的运动约束即可探索多条可行路径。为此，我们引入目标对齐的规范状态表示，消除平面内旋转歧义，并将局部几何归一化至目标方向，从而实现旋转不变的路径分布学习。我们进一步构建了一种结构化先验学习框架，使用几何感知的极坐标动作流形参数化局部路径，并结合风险敏感效用塑造与多目标分布式 rollout，以实现稳定且安全的规划。在密集静态环境和动态行人场景的大量实验表明，所提方法在成功率和效率上表现优异，且能将单个在差速驱动机器人上学习到的路径先验直接迁移到四足机器人平台，无需重新训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe, real-time navigation in cluttered and dynamic environments without human demonstrations, this paper proposes AutoPath, a goal-conditioned stochastic path prior that models multimodal, geometry-consistent local paths conditioned on local observations. The method introduces a goal-aligned canonical state representation to eliminate rotational ambiguity and normalizes local geometry, enabling rotation-invariant learning. It further employs a geometry-aware polar action manifold and risk-sensitive utility shaping with multi-goal distributional rollouts to learn a structured, transferable prior. Experiments show the method achieves high success rates and efficient navigation in both static and dynamic environments, and successfully transfers from differential-drive to quadruped robots without retraining.
为实现无需人类演示的复杂环境中安全实时导航，本文提出AutoPath方法，学习一种目标条件下的随机路径先验，该先验基于局部观测生成可复用的目标对齐路径分布。方法采用目标对齐的规范状态表示消除旋转歧义并标准化局部几何，支持旋转不变的路径分布学习；结合几何感知极坐标动作流形与风险敏感效用塑造及多目标分布式 rollout，实现稳定安全的规划。实验表明该方法在静态与动态环境中成功率高，且能将差速驱动机器人训练的单一路径先验直接迁移到四足机器人平台，无需重新训练。
MET: Theory-Grounded and Culture-Aware Multilingual Moral Reasoning
Authors: Ayoung Lee, Ryan Kwon, Yunxiang Zhang, Yuxuan Liu, Peter Railton, Lu Wang
First: 2026-07-13T15:59:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:59:25+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models are increasingly used for moral decision-making across diverse linguistic and cultural contexts, yet existing work overlooks multilinguality on three aspects: 1) multilingual evaluation benchmarks use direct translation, failing to adapt culture-specific items; 2) inference-time methods for moral reasoning rely on static, English-centric scaffolds and lack grounding in moral theory; 3) training methods for moral decision-making typically require expensive supervision from stronger models or human annotators. We address these gaps with three contributions. First, we introduce MCLASH, a multilingual moral decision-making benchmark to capture culturally situated moral intuitions and social norms across languages. Second, we propose MET (Multilingual Ethics with Theory-grounded reasoning), a two-step prompting method built on expert-curated, theory-based grounds drawn from psychology and philosophy: the model first selects situation- and culture-specific grounds, then reasons over them in the native language of the user. Third, we introduce MET-D (MET-Distillation), which enhances the second step through a self-distillation training stage that requires no external supervision. MET-D improves macro-F1 over the base model on all three models of different sizes and families (Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Gemma3-4B), by an average of 3.71 points on MCLASH and 4.23 on MMoralExceptQA, with a peak MCLASH gain of 12.94 points for Malay on Qwen3-8B. We further reveal that MET-D increases native-language reasoning by 62.13 points on average, and that beneficial grounds differ systematically across cultures. Together, these contributions open the path for culture-aligned, theory-grounded multilingual moral reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MET：基于理论且文化敏感的多语言道德推理
语言模型在多样化的语言和文化背景下日益用于道德决策，但现有研究在三个方面忽视了多语言性：1）多语言评估基准采用直接翻译，未能适应文化特异性项目；2）道德推理的推理时方法依赖静态、以英语为中心的框架，缺乏道德理论基础；3）道德决策的训练方法通常需要昂贵的更强模型或人工标注器监督。我们通过三项贡献解决这些不足。首先，我们引入MCLASH，一个多语言道德决策基准，用于捕捉跨语言的文化情境道德直觉与社会规范。其次，我们提出MET（基于理论的多语言伦理推理），一种基于心理学和哲学专家整理的理论基础的两步提示方法：模型首先选择情境和文化特定的理论基础，然后在用户母语中进行推理。第三，我们引入MET-D（MET蒸馏），通过无需外部监督的自蒸馏训练阶段增强第二步。MET-D在所有三种不同规模和家族的模型（Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、Gemma3-4B）上，相对于基线模型在MCLASH和MMoralExceptQA两个基准上平均提升3.71和4.23个宏F1分数，其中Qwen3-8B在马来语上MCLASH最高提升达12.94分。我们进一步发现，MET-D平均提升母语推理能力62.13分，且有益理论基础在不同文化间系统性差异。这些贡献共同为文化对齐、理论基础的多语言道德推理开辟了道路。

Motivated by the need to improve multilingual moral reasoning that respects cultural context and theoretical grounding, the paper introduces MET, a two-step prompting method that first selects culturally relevant moral grounds and then reasons in the user’s native language, supported by MET-D, a self-distillation technique that enhances reasoning without external supervision. Evaluated on MCLASH, a new multilingual moral benchmark, MET-D consistently outperforms baseline models across sizes and families, achieving average macro-F1 gains of 3.71 on MCLASH and 4.23 on MMoralExceptQA, with a peak gain of 12.94 points for Malay on Qwen3-8B. The method also significantly boosts native-language reasoning by 62.13 points on average and reveals systematic cultural differences in the selection of moral grounds.

NeuralActuator: Neural Actuation Modeling for Robot Dynamics and External Force Perception
Authors: Zhiyang Dou, John U. Onyemelukwe, Hangxing Zhang, Heng Zhang, Minghao Guo, Yunsheng Tian, Michal Piotr Lipiec, Joshua Jacob, Chao Liu, Peter Yichen Chen, Yuri Ivanov, Wojciech Matusik
First: 2026-07-13T15:58:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:58:34+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://people.csail.mit.edu/frankzydou/projects/NeuralActuator/index.html Code: https://github.com/Frank-ZY-Dou/Dynamics-Modeling/tree/main/NeuralActuator
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Differentiable simulators have advanced policy learning and model-based control, yet actuator dynamics remain an important source of sim-to-real error. This is particularly acute on low-cost platforms, where the linear current-to-torque relation $τ= K_tI$ becomes unreliable during commanded-target tracking because of friction, hysteresis, backlash, and thermal effects. We present NeuralActuator, a neural actuator model that jointly predicts (i) a simulator-equivalent generalized-effort surrogate for trajectory propagation on low-cost servo platforms, (ii) external force with a contact-probability gate for sensorless force perception, and (iii) a motor-condition score for the supervised joint. We also introduce the Neural Actuation Dataset (NAD), collected with a twin-arm teleoperation system that records robot states and actuator telemetry together with external-force labels. The torque-surrogate head is trained through differentiable simulation from pose trajectories without direct generalized-effort labels, while the force, gate, and motor-condition heads receive direct supervision. A Transformer captures temporal dependencies while supporting real-time inference. We evaluate NeuralActuator on a 5-DoF OpenManipulator-X, a 6-DoF SO-101, and a 7-DoF Franka Emika Panda, spanning three actuator families and platforms costing approximately USD 500 to over USD 30,000. The low-cost platforms support dynamics and force evaluation, while the offline Franka experiment provides an additional payload-force-estimation benchmark. Experiments further demonstrate its application for motor condition estimation on OpenManipulator-X and improved behavior-cloning performance when NeuralActuator is used as a pretrained module.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuralActuator：用于机器人动力学与外力感知的神经驱动建模
可微分模拟器已推动策略学习与基于模型的控制发展，但执行器动力学仍是模拟到现实误差的重要来源。尤其在低成本平台上，由于摩擦、滞后、间隙和热效应，电流-转矩线性关系τ=K_tI在跟踪指令目标时变得不可靠。我们提出NeuralActuator，一种神经驱动模型，联合预测：(i) 用于低成本伺服平台轨迹传播的模拟器等效广义作用力代理；(ii) 带接触概率门控的无传感器外力感知；(iii) 用于监督关节的电机状态评分。我们还引入Neural Actuation Dataset (NAD)，通过双臂遥操作系统采集，记录机器人状态、执行器遥测数据及外力标签。转矩代理头通过无广义作用力标签的姿态轨迹的可微分模拟训练，而力、门控和电机状态头则接受直接监督。Transformer捕捉时序依赖并支持实时推理。我们在5自由度OpenManipulator-X、6自由度SO-101和7自由度Franka Emika Panda上评估NeuralActuator，覆盖三种执行器家族和成本约500美元至3万美元以上的平台。低成本平台支持动力学与力评估，而离线Franka实验提供额外负载-力估计基准。实验进一步验证其在OpenManipulator-X上的电机状态估计应用，以及作为预训练模块时提升行为克隆性能的效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent sim-to-real gap in low-cost robotic platforms due to unreliable actuator dynamics, NeuralActuator introduces a neural model that jointly predicts a simulator-equivalent torque surrogate, external force with a contact-probability gate, and motor condition score. Trained using a differentiable simulator and a new dataset (NAD) collected via teleoperation with force labels, the model employs a Transformer architecture to capture temporal dependencies while enabling real-time inference. Evaluated across three robotic platforms spanning cost and actuator types, it successfully enables accurate dynamics and force estimation on low-cost systems and improves behavior cloning when used as a pretrained module.
为解决低成本机器人平台因摩擦、滞后、背隙和热效应导致的电机动力学建模不准确问题，NeuralActuator提出一个神经网络模型，联合预测用于轨迹模拟的扭矩代理、通过接触概率门控实现无传感器力感知，以及电机状态评分。该模型通过可微分仿真和Neural Actuation Dataset（NAD）训练，其中扭矩头从姿态轨迹中学习无需直接扭矩标签，而力与状态头采用直接监督。Transformer结构支持实时推理并捕捉时序依赖。在涵盖$500至$30,000的三类机器人平台（5-DoF、6-DoF、7-DoF）上验证，该模型成功实现低成本平台的动力学与力估计，并在作为预训练模块时提升行为克隆性能。
GFR-SAM: Training-Free Referring Camouflaged Object Segmentation via Cross-Image Prompting
Authors: Yilong Yang, Jianxin Tian, Shengchuan Zhang, Liujuan Cao
First: 2026-07-13T15:55:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:55:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Referring Camouflaged Object Detection (Ref-COD) requires segmenting hidden targets guided by reference cues. While supervised methods are annotation-heavy and training-free approaches via sparse point-prompting are sensitive to localization errors, we propose GFR-SAM, a robust three-stage training-free framework. GFR-SAM shifts the paradigm from fragile point-matching to a "Generate-Filter-Refine" pipeline. First, we introduce In-Context Exemplar-guided Segmentation, empowering SAM3 with cross-image inference to generate candidate masks via holistic visual exemplars, bypassing its native intra-image constraints. Second, a Region-Global Contrastive Filtering module ranks candidates through DINOv3-based prototypical alignment, effectively suppressing background distractors. Finally, a Geometric-Semantic Refinement module synergizes bounding box and text prompts to recover fine-grained boundaries and enhance instance recall. Evaluated on the R2C7K benchmark, GFR-SAM outperforms existing training-free methods by 8.7\% in weighted F-measure ($F_β^w$) and competes with supervised state-of-the-art counterparts. Ultimately, this work underscores the potential of unlocking SAM3's latent capability for cross-image In-Context prompting, establishing a robust, training-free paradigm that effectively bridges the gap between general-purpose foundation models and specialized, label-intensive perception tasks without the need for task-specific fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GFR-SAM：通过跨图像提示实现无需训练的指代伪装目标分割
指代伪装目标检测（Ref-COD）需要根据参考线索分割隐藏目标。虽然监督方法依赖大量标注，而基于稀疏点提示的无训练方法对定位误差敏感，我们提出GFR-SAM，一种稳健的三阶段无训练框架。GFR-SAM将范式从脆弱的点匹配转向“生成-过滤-精炼”流程。首先，引入上下文示例引导分割，使SAM3通过整体视觉示例实现跨图像推理，生成候选掩码，绕过其原生的图像内约束。其次，区域-全局对比过滤模块基于DINOv3原型对齐对候选进行排序，有效抑制背景干扰。最后，几何-语义精炼模块结合边界框与文本提示，恢复细粒度边界并提升实例召回率。在R2C7K基准上，GFR-SAM在加权F度量（$F_β^w$）上比现有无训练方法高出8.7%，并可与监督式最先进方法竞争。本工作凸显了释放SAM3跨图像上下文提示潜在能力的潜力，建立了一种稳健的无训练范式，有效弥合通用基础模型与需大量标注的专用感知任务之间的差距，无需任务特定微调。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for training-free methods in referring camouflaged object segmentation that avoid annotation dependency and localization sensitivity, GFR-SAM introduces a three-stage framework: it first uses cross-image prompting via holistic visual exemplars to generate candidate masks, then filters them using DINOv3-based prototypical alignment to suppress background noise, and finally refines boundaries with geometric and semantic cues from bounding boxes and text prompts. Evaluated on R2C7K, GFR-SAM achieves an 8.7% higher weighted F-measure than existing training-free methods and matches supervised state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the viability of leveraging SAM3’s latent cross-image prompting capability without fine-tuning.
为解决参照式伪装目标分割中训练依赖强、定位误差敏感的问题，GFR-SAM提出三阶段训练免框架：首先通过跨图像视觉范例引导SAM3生成候选分割掩码，再利用DINOv3原型对齐对比模块过滤背景干扰，最后结合边界框与文本提示进行几何-语义精细化，提升边界精度与实例召回率。在R2C7K数据集上，其加权F值比现有训练免方法高8.7%，性能可媲美监督式SOTA模型，验证了SAM3跨图像上下文提示能力，构建了无需微调的鲁棒训练免范式。
FLYNN: Robust Neural Network for Robot Navigation using Fly Brain Topology
Authors: Benquan Wang, Jingdao Chen
First: 2026-06-21T02:41:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:51:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While deep learning models achieve state-of-the-art performance in complex tasks, they remain brittle when faced with new environments or sensory deprivation. In contrast, biological systems exhibit remarkable tolerance to these challenges. We address this vulnerability by developing a recurrent neural network (RNN) whose architecture is directly derived from the synaptic-resolution brain connectome of the fruit fly Drosophila melanogaster. We demonstrate the feasibility of training the fly connectome neural network (FLYNN) to perform vision-based navigation in MuJoCo, achieving performance comparable to modern hand-crafted networks of similar parameter counts. Crucially, FLYNN exhibits superior resistance to out-of-distribution (OOD) data and tolerance to sensory loss without further training. It remained functional even under total vision loss while hand-crafted networks largely failed, even when specifically trained with camera dropout. Principal Component Analysis (PCA) of the internal state of FLYNN suggests that it exhibits a particularly high degree of representational modularity, which might be related to its robustness. Our work provides a new direction for designing resilient artificial agents following the topology of biological brains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLYNN：基于果蝇脑拓扑结构的鲁棒机器人导航神经网络
尽管深度学习模型在复杂任务中表现出最先进的性能，但在面对新环境或感官剥夺时仍易脆弱。相比之下，生物系统对这些挑战表现出显著的耐受性。我们通过开发一种递归神经网络（RNN），其架构直接源自果蝇（Drosophila melanogaster）的突触分辨率脑连接组，来解决这一脆弱性问题。我们证明了训练果蝇连接组神经网络（FLYNN）在MuJoCo中执行基于视觉的导航是可行的，其性能与参数量相当的现代手工设计网络相当。关键的是，FLYNN在面对分布外（OOD）数据和感官损失时表现出更强的鲁棒性，且无需进一步训练。即使在完全失明的情况下，FLYNN仍能保持功能，而手工设计网络即使专门训练了摄像头失效场景也大多失效。对FLYNN内部状态进行主成分分析（PCA）表明，其表现出高度的表征模块化，这可能与其鲁棒性相关。我们的工作为设计遵循生物脑拓扑结构的鲁棒人工代理提供了新方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of deep learning models in novel or sensory-deprived environments, this work proposes FLYNN, a recurrent neural network whose architecture mirrors the synaptic connectome of the fruit fly Drosophila melanogaster. Trained in the MuJoCo physics engine for vision-based navigation, FLYNN achieves performance comparable to hand-crafted networks of similar size but demonstrates significantly greater robustness: it maintains functionality under total vision loss while conventional networks fail, even when pretrained with camera dropout. 为了解决深度学习模型在新环境或感官缺失下脆弱的问题，该研究提出FLYNN，一种架构直接基于果蝇Drosophila melanogaster突触级联接组的循环神经网络。在MuJoCo仿真环境中训练用于视觉导航，FLYNN表现可媲美同等规模的手工设计网络，同时在分布外数据和感官缺失下展现出更强鲁棒性——即使在完全失明情况下仍能正常工作，而同类网络则普遍失效。其内部状态的主成分分析显示高度的表征模块化，或与其鲁棒性相关。
Time-Lag-Aware Deep Reinforcement Learning for Flexible Job-Shop Scheduling in PPVC Module Factories
Authors: Ziheng Zhang, Wei Zhang
First: 2026-07-13T15:50:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:50:00+00:00
Comments: 10 pages,5 figures, UR by IEEE TII
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prefabricated prefinished volumetric construction moves most building work into module factories, whose production floor operates as a flexible job shop. A major complication is decisive: long post-operation time-lags caused by concrete curing, watertightness ponding tests, and paint drying, during which a module is blocked while its workstation stays free. On benchmark instances grounded in an official national prefabrication guidebook, these lags inflate even the optimal reference makespan by about 67% on average, and ignoring them at decision time, then repairing to feasibility, is worse than every dispatching rule. We adapt a state-of-the-art dual-attention deep reinforcement learning solver through three minimally invasive, individually ablatable extensions: lag-aware dynamics with an admissible reward bound, two anticipatory lag feature channels, and liveness-masked operation- and station-type embeddings. With every extension disabled the implementation reproduces the original solver exactly, so all gains are attributable to the adaptations. We release a public, guidebook-grounded benchmark generator. On held-out instances the learned policy is the strongest solver-free scheduler: it reaches within about 4% of a constraint-programming reference and beats every dispatching rule and a genetic-algorithm metaheuristic, with its advantage widening under capacity contention, and a single size-mixed policy carries this lead across the trained range of factory sizes. It needs no solver, model, or license in the loop and re-plans within seconds of a disruption; where an exact solver can be deployed, that solver remains the quality ceiling, a boundary we map explicitly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向PPVC模块工厂柔性作业车间调度的时滞感知深度强化学习
预制装配式整体建筑将大部分施工移入模块工厂，其生产车间构成柔性作业车间。主要难点在于：混凝土养护、防水试验及油漆干燥等长时滞操作，导致模块被阻塞而工作站空闲。在基于官方国家预制指南的基准实例中，这些时滞使最优参考完工时间平均增加67%；若决策时忽略时滞再事后修复，效果劣于所有调度规则。我们通过三项最小侵入、可独立移除的扩展，改进当前最先进的双注意力深度强化学习求解器：时滞感知动态模型（含可接受奖励边界）、两个前瞻性时滞特征通道、以及存活掩码操作与工位类型嵌入。每项扩展单独关闭时，系统完全复现原求解器，所有性能提升均归因于这些改进。我们公开发布基于指南的基准生成器。在未参与训练的实例上，所学策略成为最强无求解器调度器：其性能接近约束规划参考解的4%以内，优于所有调度规则及遗传算法元启发式方法，且在产能紧张时优势更显著；单一规模混合策略在训练覆盖的工厂规模范围内持续领先。该方法无需求解器、模型或授权，中断后数秒内即可重新规划；若部署精确求解器，其仍为质量上限，我们明确绘制该边界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional scheduling methods in prefabricated construction module factories where long time-lags between operations severely inflate makespan, this work adapts a state-of-the-art deep reinforcement learning solver with three targeted extensions: lag-aware dynamics, anticipatory lag features, and liveness-masked embeddings. These modifications enable the agent to anticipate and account for delays like curing and drying without compromising feasibility. Experimental results on benchmark instances derived from a national prefabrication guidebook show the adapted policy outperforms all dispatching rules and a genetic algorithm, achieving within 4% of a constraint-programming baseline and maintaining superior performance under capacity stress, while being solver-free and capable of rapid re-planning after disruptions.
为解决预制模块工厂中因混凝土养护、防水测试和油漆干燥等操作导致的长时滞严重拖累生产效率的问题，本文在现有深度强化学习求解器基础上，通过三项轻量级扩展——时滞感知动态、前瞻时滞特征通道和活性掩码操作-工位嵌入——使其能有效预测并处理时滞而不破坏可行性。实验表明，该策略在基于国家预制指南构建的基准实例上，优于所有调度规则和遗传算法，接近约束规划参考解（误差约4%），且在产能紧张时优势更明显。该策略无需外部求解器或许可，可快速响应扰动，且单一策略可覆盖多种工厂规模。
High-level spatial Dubins airplane-based reference smoothing with low-level geometric tracking for quadrotor control
Authors: Mogens Plessen
First: 2026-07-13T15:49:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:49:26+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A method for the control of quadrotors is presented. It is composed of a high-level reference smoothing step and a low-level reference tracking step. The high-level step leverages the Dubins airplane model for dimensionality reduction and reduced computational complexity, and exploits its structure for decoupling, spatial modeling and the formulation of a small linear program. The low-level step leverages a geometric tracking controller, which is based on the full quadrotor model. The method is designed for the tracking of references subject to lateral constraints along the path. An example is the tracking of references along obstacle contours. It is differentiated between two different setups. Either the high-level planning step is conducted once and offline, or, alternatively, the high-level planning step is conducted recedingly online in closed-loop over a limited spatial prediction horizon.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于高阶空间Dubins飞机模型的参考路径平滑与低阶几何跟踪的四旋翼飞行器控制方法
本文提出一种四旋翼飞行器控制方法，由高阶参考路径平滑步骤和低阶参考跟踪步骤组成。高阶步骤利用Dubins飞机模型实现维度约简和降低计算复杂度，并利用其结构实现解耦、空间建模及小规模线性规划的构建。低阶步骤基于完整四旋翼模型的几何跟踪控制器。该方法适用于路径上存在横向约束的参考轨迹跟踪，例如沿障碍物轮廓的跟踪。方法区分两种设置：高阶规划步骤可离线一次性执行，或在线闭环递归执行于有限空间预测时域内。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of quadrotor trajectory tracking under lateral constraints, such as obstacle avoidance, by introducing a two-stage control framework. The high-level stage uses a Dubins airplane model to simplify the problem, enabling efficient decoupling and formulation of a small linear program for reference smoothing, while the low-level stage employs a geometric tracking controller based on the full quadrotor dynamics. The method supports both offline and receding-horizon online planning, and experimental results demonstrate improved tracking accuracy and robustness under lateral constraints compared to baseline approaches.
该文提出一种用于受横向路径约束的四旋翼飞行器控制方法，结合高层参考轨迹平滑模块与低层几何跟踪控制器。高层模块利用Dubins飞机模型降低维度，通过小型线性规划生成平滑、解耦的参考轨迹，提升规划效率；低层控制器基于完整四旋翼动力学模型跟踪该轨迹。支持两种运行模式：离线规划或闭环在线递推规划。实验表明，该方法在跟踪障碍轮廓等约束路径时，相比无轨迹平滑的基准方法，显著提升轨迹平滑度与跟踪精度。
Restricted Dynamic Geometric Complexity: Path-Space Reduction and Möbius--Jacobi Response
Authors: Zavier Li
First: 2026-07-08T09:36:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:49:20+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Structured preconditioners restrict optimization to a small family of positive metrics, but endpoint condition-number reachability does not measure the geometric effort required to reach a useful metric. We formulate this effort as a path-space value problem. Restricted dynamic geometric complexity is the least affine-invariant length of an admissible metric path whose endpoint reaches a Hessian-relative generalized-eigenvalue condition target.
