Path elimination gives an exact min-plus semigroup and Bellman principle, while fixed-horizon kinetic energy is exactly squared complexity divided by twice the horizon. The main response result is global on a Hadamard state space: geodesic convexity produces a smooth intervention-cube path branch and a uniformly coercive Jacobi form, while one Green inverse generates the value Hessian, two-sided force-to-curvature bounds, exact Möbius effects, and arbitrary prescribed finite-order responses. For the hard condition target, a bordered Jacobi--KKT theorem differentiates the moving projection endpoint and multiplier on every regular active spectral stratum; its indefinite inverse also explains why hard-target interactions need not share the unconstrained sign. The theory specializes to affine-invariant positive-definite geometry. A determinant-one two-dimensional diagonal model has an exact target interval, a closed-form forced path, and a strictly negative-definite interaction matrix. A moving diagonal Hessian gives a closed-form hard-target projection, multiplier, and pair effects of either sign, while a coordinate-sequential three-dimensional protocol yields an exact path metric strictly larger than the ambient projection distance. Thus the global Green and bordered hard-target responses are explicit laws of restricted metric-path elimination built on Bellman composition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：受限动态几何复杂度：路径空间缩减与莫比乌斯-雅可比响应
结构化预条件子将优化限制在一小类正度量中，但端点条件数可达性无法衡量达到有用度量所需的几何努力。我们将此努力形式化为路径空间值问题。受限动态几何复杂度是达到赫塞相对广义特征值条件目标的最小仿射不变度量路径长度。路径消除给出精确的min-plus半群与贝尔曼原理，而固定时间范围的动能恰好等于复杂度平方除以两倍时间范围。主要响应结果在哈达玛状态空间上全局成立：测地凸性产生光滑干预立方路径分支与一致强凸雅可比形式，一个格林逆生成值赫塞矩阵、双向力-曲率界、精确莫比乌斯效应及任意指定有限阶响应。对于硬条件目标，有边界的雅可比-KKT定理在每个正则活跃谱层面上区分移动投影端点与乘子；其不定逆也解释了为何硬目标交互无需共享无约束符号。该理论特化于仿射不变正定几何。一个行列式为一的二维对角模型具有精确目标区间、闭式强迫路径及严格负定交互矩阵。移动对角赫塞矩阵给出闭式硬目标投影、乘子及正负符号的成对效应，而坐标顺序三维协议则产生严格大于环境投影距离的精确路径度量。因此，全局格林与有边界硬目标响应是基于贝尔曼合成构建的受限度量路径消除的显式定律。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to quantify geometric effort in restricted metric spaces for optimization, the paper introduces 'restricted dynamic geometric complexity' as the minimal affine-invariant path length to reach a Hessian-relative condition target. It formulates this as a path-space value problem and derives exact min-plus semigroup and Bellman principles, showing that kinetic energy equals squared complexity over twice the horizon. Key results include global geodesic convexity yielding smooth intervention paths and a coercive Jacobi form, with a single Green inverse generating the value Hessian, force-to-curvature bounds, Möbius effects, and arbitrary finite-order responses. For hard targets, a bordered Jacobi--KKT theorem differentiates endpoints and multipliers across spectral strata, explaining sign non-uniformity in interactions. Specialized to positive-definite geometry, the model demonstrates exact closed-form solutions for 2D diagonal cases and 3D sequential protocols, revealing that path metrics can exceed ambient projection distances.
为量化受限度量空间中优化所需的几何代价，本文提出“受限动态几何复杂度”——即达到Hessian相对条件目标所需的最小仿射不变路径长度，并将其形式化为路径空间值问题。通过路径消除，得到精确的min-plus半群与贝尔曼原理，且固定时间步的动能等于复杂度平方除以两倍时间步。核心结果表明，在Hadamard状态空间上，测地凸性生成光滑干预路径与一致强凸的雅可比形式，单个Green逆矩阵可生成值Hessian、力-曲率界、Möbius效应及任意有限阶响应。针对硬目标，边框雅可比-KKT定理在每个正则活跃谱层上区分移动投影终点与乘子，其不定逆解释了硬目标交互无需共享无约束符号的原因。在正定几何中，二维对角模型具有精确目标区间与闭式强迫路径，交互矩阵严格负定；三维坐标顺序协议则生成严格大于环境投影距离的路径度量，揭示了基于贝尔曼合成的受限度量路径消除的显式定律。
STEP: Career-Path Recommendation via Temporal and Educational Trajectory Modeling
Authors: Iman Johary, Guillaume Bied, Alexandru C. Mara, Tijl De Bie
First: 2026-07-13T15:48:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:48:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Career paths encode decades of skill acquisition, role transitions, and educational investment, and understanding them at scale underpins workforce planning, labor market policy, and job recommendation. Resumes are a rich source of information about career paths: they contain detailed descriptions of work experience, education, and skills. Yet their unstructured, heterogeneous, and multilingual nature has long prevented large-scale systematic analysis. With the advent of large language models (LLMs), it is now possible to source rich career trajectory data containing temporal and educational signals from unstructured resumes, enabling new opportunities for career-path recommendation. Exploiting this opportunity, we present STEP (Sequential Trajectory of Employment Prediction), a novel career-path recommendation system that leverages temporal and educational signals to predict the next job in a career trajectory. STEP integrates a time-decay Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU) cell to model temporal dynamics, Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) conditioned on educational attainment, and attention-based sequence pooling to select relevant features for next job prediction. To improve internal occupation representation for STEP, we introduce ROUTE, a two-stage contrastive procedure that first adapts a multilingual encoder to the career domain via unsupervised denoising autoencoding, then performs supervised contrastive fine-tuning with guided negative selection. We evaluate STEP on four datasets of career trajectories, including an improved version of our publicly available JobHop dataset, and show that it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in next job prediction. The dataset and code are publicly released to support reproducible career-trajectory research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STEP：通过时间与教育轨迹建模的职业路径推荐
职业路径编码了数十年的技能获取、角色转换和教育投入，理解其大规模模式是劳动力规划、劳动力市场政策和职位推荐的基础。简历是职业路径信息的丰富来源，包含详细的工作经历、教育背景和技能描述。然而，其非结构化、异构性和多语言特性长期阻碍了大规模系统性分析。随着大语言模型（LLM）的出现，现在可以从非结构化简历中提取包含时间和教育信号的丰富职业轨迹数据，为职业路径推荐带来新机遇。我们提出STEP（就业序列轨迹预测），一种新颖的职业路径推荐系统，利用时间和教育信号预测职业轨迹中的下一个职位。STEP整合了时间衰减门控循环单元（GRU）以建模时间动态，基于教育成就的特征线性调制（FiLM），以及基于注意力的序列池化以选择对下一个职位预测相关的特征。为提升STEP内部职业表示，我们引入ROUTE，一种两阶段对比学习流程：首先通过无监督去噪自编码将多语言编码器适配职业领域，然后通过带引导负样本选择的监督对比微调进行优化。我们在四个职业轨迹数据集上评估STEP，包括我们公开发布的JobHop数据集的改进版本，结果表明其在下一个职位预测任务上优于现有最先进基线。数据集和代码已公开发布，以支持可复现的职业轨迹研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand and predict career paths for workforce planning and job recommendations, this paper introduces STEP, a system that leverages temporal and educational signals extracted from unstructured resumes using large language models. STEP employs a time-decay GRU to model career progression over time, FiLM modulation conditioned on education level, and attention-based pooling to focus on relevant features for next-job prediction. To enhance occupation representation, it introduces ROUTE, a two-stage contrastive learning method combining unsupervised denoising autoencoding and supervised contrastive fine-tuning with negative sampling. Evaluated on four career trajectory datasets including an enhanced JobHop dataset, STEP consistently outperforms existing baselines in next-job prediction accuracy.
为支持大规模职业路径分析与推荐，本文提出STEP系统，利用简历中的时间与教育信号预测下一份工作。该系统采用带时间衰减的GRU建模时序动态，FiLM机制结合教育背景，注意力池化筛选关键特征。为提升职业表示能力，引入ROUTE双阶段对比学习方法，先通过无监督去噪自编码适配多语言编码器，再进行监督对比微调并引导负样本选择。在四个职业轨迹数据集（含改进版JobHop）上，STEP显著优于现有基线模型。
Active Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Alper Kamil Bozkurt, Shangtong Zhang, Yuichi Motai
First: 2026-07-13T15:46:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:46:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables effective policies to be trained from large, previously collected datasets and subsequently improved through limited online interaction. This offline-to-online RL (O2O-RL) paradigm is particularly promising in nonstationary domains where interaction is costly or potentially hazardous. Standard O2O-RL pipelines train multiple candidate policies offline, evaluate them using off-policy or online evaluation, and then deploy and fine-tune the policy with the highest estimated value. However, as in offline pretraining, fine-tuning performance is highly sensitive to the choice of algorithm and hyperparameters, making it risky to commit to a single policy. Objectives: We study active policy selection for fine-tuning under a limited interaction budget in O2O-RL settings. To our knowledge, this is the first work to address this problem. Methods: We formulate the problem by identifying a fundamental trade-off between allocating online interactions to policy evaluation, which helps identify high-performing policies, and allocating them to fine-tuning, which improves policy performance. We then propose an approach that balances this trade-off by actively selecting policies for fine-tuning based on upper-confidence bounds on their future performance. These bounds are derived from locally linear performance forecasts fitted to observations obtained through online evaluation. Results: Across a diverse range of experiments, the proposed approach consistently outperforms existing O2O-RL baselines. Conclusions: Actively selecting and fine-tuning policies uses limited online interaction budgets more effectively than either committing to a single policy or dividing the budget equally among all policies. Our framework also advances offline RL toward practical deployment in real-world systems where online interaction is costly or risky.
中文标题/摘要
标题：主动的离线到在线强化学习
背景：离线强化学习（RL）可从大量预先收集的数据集中训练出有效策略，并通过有限的在线交互进一步优化。该离线到在线RL（O2O-RL）范式在交互成本高昂或潜在危险的非平稳领域尤其有前景。标准O2O-RL流程通常在离线阶段训练多个候选策略，通过离线或在线评估选择最优策略，再部署并微调该策略。然而，与离线预训练类似，微调性能对算法和超参数的选择高度敏感，因此盲目选择单一策略风险较大。目标：我们研究在O2O-RL设置下，如何在有限交互预算内主动选择策略进行微调。据我们所知，这是首次针对该问题的研究。方法：我们通过识别一个基本权衡来建模该问题：一方面将在线交互分配给策略评估，以识别高表现策略；另一方面分配给微调，以提升策略性能。我们提出一种方法，通过基于未来表现上置信界主动选择策略进行微调，这些置信界由在线评估获得的观测数据拟合的局部线性表现预测得出。结果：在多样化的实验中，所提方法始终优于现有O2O-RL基线。结论：主动选择并微调策略，比固定单一策略或平均分配预算给所有策略，更有效地利用有限的在线交互预算。我们的框架也推动离线RL向实际部署迈进，尤其适用于在线交互成本高昂或风险较高的真实系统。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently utilize limited online interactions in offline-to-online reinforcement learning, this work proposes an active policy selection method that dynamically allocates online budget between policy evaluation and fine-tuning. The method uses upper-confidence bounds derived from locally linear performance forecasts to prioritize policies for fine-tuning, balancing exploration and exploitation. Experimental results across diverse environments show that this active selection strategy consistently outperforms baselines that either commit to a single policy or distribute interactions equally, demonstrating superior performance under constrained interaction budgets.
为更高效地利用稀缺的在线交互资源，该研究提出了一种主动策略选择方法，在线预算在策略评估与微调之间动态分配。该方法基于局部线性性能预测生成的置信上界，优先选择高潜力策略进行微调，以平衡探索与利用。在多个实验中，该方法始终优于固定单一策略或平均分配预算的基线方法，证明其在受限交互预算下具有更优的性能表现。
JobHop v2: A Large-Scale Career Trajectory Dataset from Unstructured Resumes
Authors: Iman Johary, Guillaume Bied, Alexandru C. Mara, Tijl De Bie
First: 2026-07-13T15:42:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:42:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large-scale, richly annotated career trajectory data underpins workforce planning, job recommendation, and labour market analysis, yet publicly available datasets are either small, closed to independent use, or built from pre-standardized occupational codes with LLM-synthesized rather than authentic free text. We present JobHop~v2, an improved version of the publicly available JobHop dataset, constructed through end-to-end large language model (LLM) extraction from a corpus of ${\sim}440{,}000$ pseudonymized, multilingual resumes provided by VDAB, the Flemish Public Employment Service. The released dataset comprises $355{,}315$ career trajectories annotated with ESCO occupational codes, quarter-level temporal information, and normalized five-level education attainment, broadening both the coverage and the annotation richness of the original release. Relative to v1, JobHop~v2 introduces a redesigned extraction pipeline based on reasoning-controlled LLM inference with a retry mechanism (achieving a 100% JSON parse rate), a richer extraction schema, and a revised evaluation protocol scored against three complementary annotation baselines. Evaluated against these baselines, our best extractor comes closest to the inter-annotator agreement ceiling among all compared models, trailing it by only 1.1-2.7 percentage points. The dataset and code are publicly released to support reproducible career-trajectory research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：JobHop v2：来自非结构化简历的大规模职业轨迹数据集
大规模、丰富标注的职业轨迹数据是劳动力规划、职位推荐和劳动力市场分析的基础，但现有公开数据集要么规模小，要么限制独立使用，或基于预标准化的职业代码构建，其标注文本由大语言模型合成而非真实自由文本。我们提出了JobHop v2，这是公开可用的JobHop数据集的改进版本，通过端到端大语言模型（LLM）从弗拉芒公共就业服务（VDAB）提供的约44万份匿名化、多语言简历中提取构建。发布的数据集包含355,315条职业轨迹，标注有ESCO职业代码、季度级时间信息和标准化的五级教育水平，扩展了原始版本的覆盖范围和标注丰富度。相较于v1，JobHop v2引入了基于推理控制的LLM推断并带有重试机制的重新设计提取管道（实现100% JSON解析率）、更丰富的提取模式和修订后的评估协议（基于三个互补标注基线评分）。在这些基线评估中，我们的最佳提取器在所有比较模型中最接近标注者间一致性上限，仅相差1.1-2.7个百分点。数据集和代码已公开发布，以支持可复现的职业轨迹研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for large-scale, authentic, and richly annotated career trajectory data for workforce analysis, the authors introduce JobHop v2, a dataset built by extracting structured career information from 440,000 pseudonymized resumes using an end-to-end LLM pipeline. The method employs reasoning-controlled LLM inference with a retry mechanism to ensure 100% JSON parse rate, and includes richer annotations such as ESCO codes, temporal granularity, and education levels. Experimental results show that the best extractor in JobHop v2 achieves near-human inter-annotator agreement, differing by only 1.1–2.7 percentage points, outperforming prior methods and significantly expanding the dataset’s coverage and annotation depth.
为支持劳动力市场分析所需的大型真实职业轨迹数据，作者构建了JobHop v2数据集，通过端到端大语言模型从44万份匿名简历中提取结构化职业信息。方法采用基于推理控制的大模型推理与重试机制，确保100% JSON解析率，并扩展了标注维度如ESCO职业代码、季度时间粒度和教育水平。实验表明，最佳提取器在三项基准标注中接近人工标注者间的一致性上限，仅相差1.1–2.7个百分点，显著优于前代方法，提升了标注质量和覆盖范围。
CatRetriever: Contrastive Representation Learning for Slab-to-Bulk Retrieval in Generative Catalyst Discovery
Authors: Jungho Oh, Woosung Kim, Dong Hyeon Mok, Jonggeol Na, Seoin Back
First: 2026-07-13T15:38:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:38:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse design is an emerging data-driven paradigm for efficiently navigating vast chemical spaces to discover new materials with targeted properties, and in the context of heterogeneous catalysis, surface generative models have recently advanced this goal by directly generating catalyst surface-adsorbate structures. However, these models typically operate at the slab level and do not provide the corresponding parent bulk structure, making it difficult to assess bulk-dependent properties such as formation energy, surface energy, crystallographic symmetry, and synthesizability. Here, we address this missing slab-to-bulk connection as a retrieval problem and introduce CatRetriever, a contrastive representation learning model that aligns slab and bulk crystal representations in a shared latent space. From a slab query, CatRetriever accurately retrieves plausible parent bulk candidates with R@1 > 91% and R@3 > 98% on both the in-distribution and holdout evaluation sets. We further extend the CatRetriever framework into an adsorption energy targeted bulk discovery pipeline that combines bulk retrieval, generative search space expansion, and adsorption energy distribution analysis. This workflow evaluates candidates by both structural compatibility with the query slab and their ability to access the target adsorption energy range across diverse surface environments. CatRetriever therefore provides a scalable route for connecting catalyst generative models with physically plausible and adsorption energy compatible bulk catalyst discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CatRetriever：用于生成型催化剂发现中片状到块体检索的对比表征学习
逆向设计是一种新兴的数据驱动范式，旨在高效探索庞大的化学空间以发现具有目标性能的新材料；在异质催化领域，表面生成模型最近通过直接生成催化剂表面-吸附物结构推动了这一目标。然而，这些模型通常在片状水平上操作，不提供相应的母体块体结构，使得评估与块体相关的性质（如形成能、表面能、晶体对称性和可合成性）变得困难。本文将这一缺失的片状到块体连接视为检索问题，并引入CatRetriever，一种对比表征学习模型，它在共享的潜在空间中对齐片状和块体晶体表征。从片状查询出发，CatRetriever在分布内和保留集评估数据集上均能准确检索出合理的母体块体候选结构，R@1 > 91% 且 R@3 > 98%。我们进一步将CatRetriever框架扩展为一个以吸附能为目标的块体发现流程，结合块体检索、生成式搜索空间扩展和吸附能分布分析。该工作流程通过结构兼容性（与查询片状结构）和在多样化表面环境中实现目标吸附能范围的能力来评估候选结构。因此，CatRetriever为连接催化剂生成模型与物理上合理且吸附能兼容的块体催化剂发现提供了一条可扩展的路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to bridge the gap between slab-level catalyst generation and physically meaningful bulk structures, CatRetriever employs contrastive representation learning to align slab and bulk crystal representations in a shared latent space. By training on paired slab-bulk data, the model retrieves plausible bulk candidates from slab queries with high accuracy—R@1 exceeding 91% and R@3 exceeding 98%—on both in-distribution and holdout sets. The framework is further extended into a generative pipeline that combines bulk retrieval, surface expansion, and adsorption energy analysis to identify bulk structures compatible with target adsorption energies, enabling scalable, physically grounded catalyst discovery.
为解决生成催化剂表面结构时缺乏对应母体块体结构的问题，CatRetriever采用对比表示学习方法，在共享潜在空间中对块体与表面结构进行对齐。该模型在配对数据上训练，能从表面结构查询中高精度检索出合理块体候选结构，准确率R@1超过91%，R@3超过98%，在分布内与分布外测试集均表现优异。该框架进一步扩展为一个生成流程，结合块体检索、表面结构扩展与吸附能分析，筛选出结构兼容且吸附能匹配目标范围的块体催化剂。
SVI360: Spherical Video Interpolation
Authors: Le-Kim Nguyen, Renato Martins, Pascal Vasseur, Cedric Demonceaux
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-13T15:37:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:37:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECCV 2026. Code and trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/video360_interpolation/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This paper addresses the problem of omnidirectional video interpolation, which plays an essential role in applications such as virtual reality and immersive video enhancement. Existing video interpolation methods are not well-suited for spherical videos, as they have difficulty handling severe distortions close to the poles. To address this issue, we propose SVI360, a dual-branch framework that combines the image frame and its rotated orthogonal view to deal with these distortions. The core methodological aspect of the approach is to reinforce equivariance of the flow displacements between the original and orthogonal views to improve intermediate frame prediction. Experiments show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in interpolation quality while maintaining accurate optical flow in four different public benchmarks. Code and pre-trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/video360_interpolation/
中文标题/摘要
标题：SVI360：球面视频插值
本文解决了球面视频插值问题，该问题在虚拟现实和沉浸式视频增强等应用中至关重要。现有视频插值方法不适用于球面视频，因为难以处理靠近极点的严重畸变。为解决此问题，我们提出了SVI360，一种双分支框架，结合图像帧及其旋转正交视图以应对这些畸变。该方法的核心是强化原始视图与正交视图间流位移的等变性，以提升中间帧预测效果。实验表明，我们的方法在四个公开基准测试中插值质量优于现有最先进方法，同时保持精确的光流。代码和预训练模型见：https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/video360_interpolation/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-quality interpolation in spherical videos for virtual reality and immersive applications, this paper introduces SVI360, a dual-branch framework that leverages both a video frame and its rotated orthogonal view to handle severe distortions near the poles. The method enforces equivariance between the flow displacements of the original and orthogonal views to enhance intermediate frame prediction. Experimental results demonstrate that SVI360 achieves superior interpolation quality compared to existing methods while preserving accurate optical flow across four public benchmarks.
为了解决球形视频在VR和沉浸式应用中插值质量差的问题，本文提出SVI360框架，通过结合原始帧与其旋转正交视图来应对极点区域的严重畸变。该方法通过增强原始帧与正交视图间光流位移的等变性，提升中间帧预测效果。实验表明，SVI360在四个公开数据集上插值质量优于现有方法，同时保持了准确的光流估计。
ProAct: A Benchmark and Multimodal Framework for Structure-Aware Proactive Response
Authors: Xiaomeng Zhu, Fengming Zhu, Weijie Zhou, Ye Tian, Zhenlin Hu, Yufei Huang, Yuchun Guo, Xinyu Wu, Zhengyou Zhang, Fangzhen Lin, Xuantang Xiong
First: 2026-02-03T11:52:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:37:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While passive agents merely follow instructions, proactive agents align with higher-level objectives, such as assistance and safety by continuously monitoring the environment to determine when and how to act. However, developing proactive agents is hindered by the lack of specialized resources. To address this, we introduce ProAct-75, a benchmark designed to train and evaluate proactive agents across diverse domains, including assistance, maintenance, and safety monitoring. Spanning 75 tasks, our dataset features 91,581 step-level annotations enriched with explicit task graphs. These graphs encode step dependencies and parallel execution possibilities, providing the structural grounding necessary for complex decision-making. Building on this benchmark, we propose ProAct-Helper, a reference baseline powered by a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) that grounds decision-making in state detection, and leveraging task graphs to enable entropy-driven heuristic search for action selection, allowing agents to execute parallel threads independently rather than mirroring the human's next step. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ProAct-Helper outperforms strong closed-source models, improving trigger detection mF1 by 6.21%, saving 0.25 more steps in online one-step decision, and increasing the rate of parallel actions by 15.58%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProAct：面向结构感知主动响应的基准与多模态框架
被动代理仅遵循指令，而主动代理则通过持续监控环境以确定何时及如何行动，从而与更高层次目标（如协助与安全）对齐。然而，主动代理的开发受限于缺乏专用资源。为此，我们引入ProAct-75基准，旨在跨多个领域（包括协助、维护与安全监控）训练和评估主动代理。该数据集涵盖75项任务，包含91,581个步骤级标注，并附带显式任务图。这些图编码步骤依赖关系与并行执行可能性，为复杂决策提供结构化基础。基于此基准，我们提出ProAct-Helper，一种由多模态大语言模型（MLLM）驱动的参考基线，其决策基于状态检测，并利用任务图实现熵驱动启发式搜索以选择动作，使代理能够独立执行并行线程，而非简单模仿人类下一步动作。大量实验表明，ProAct-Helper优于强闭源模型，触发检测mF1提升6.21%，在线单步决策节省0.25步，平行动作执行率提升15.58%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for structured, proactive agents that can autonomously align with high-level goals like safety and assistance, the authors introduce ProAct-75, a benchmark with 75 tasks and 91,581 step-level annotations enriched with explicit task graphs that encode dependencies and parallel execution. Building on this, they propose ProAct-Helper, a multimodal LLM-based agent that uses state detection and entropy-driven heuristic search guided by task graphs to enable parallel action execution. Experiments show ProAct-Helper outperforms strong closed-source models by improving trigger detection mF1 by 6.21%, reducing online decision steps by 0.25, and increasing parallel action rate by 15.58%.
为推动具备自主决策能力的主动代理发展，本研究提出ProAct-75基准，包含75个任务及91,581个带任务图结构标注的步骤级数据，任务图编码步骤依赖与并行执行可能性。基于此，提出ProAct-Helper基线模型，结合多模态大语言模型、状态检测与任务图引导的熵驱动启发式搜索，支持并行动作执行。实验表明，该模型在触发检测mF1上提升6.21%，在线单步决策节省0.25步，平行动作率提升15.58%，优于主流闭源模型。
An Explainable Agentic System for Detection of Conversational Scams with Summary-Based Memory
Authors: Ahmed Omar Salim Adnan, Yogananda Manjunath, Shivanjali Khare
First: 2026-07-13T15:36:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:36:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Following the rapid progress of generative Artificial Intelligence, there is a growing threat posed by conversational scams. These scams often span over multiple weeks or months, gradually build trust and request for money or sensitive information. Existing scam-detection systems mainly focus on isolated messages, which renders them inadequate against this evolving threat. This paper extends single-message phishing detection and presents an explainable agentic system for detecting sophisticated conversational scams. It also introduces ConScamBench-278, an initial public multi-category benchmark for conversational scam detection spanning eight scam types, released to support reproducible evaluation and future expansion. On isolated messages the single-message detector attains 100% phishing recall, while the conversation-level detector identifies all conversational scams in the public LoveFraud02 corpus (83/83) and reaches 97.8% accuracy (95% CI [95.4, 99.0]) on ConScamBench-278. Two user studies (N = 100 and N = 45) further motivate the system: participants report frequently experiencing uncertainty when judging suspicious conversations. In an uncontrolled pre/post comparison, users self-reported trust, self-confidence, and perceived need for AI-based scam detection all increased (p < 0.001, Wilcoxon signed-rank). The system also receives a System Usability Scale score of 74.7 (95% CI [72.5, 76.9]), above the established usability benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于摘要记忆的可解释代理系统用于对话式诈骗检测
随着生成式人工智能的快速发展，对话式诈骗威胁日益加剧。此类诈骗通常持续数周或数月，逐步建立信任后要求转账或获取敏感信息。现有诈骗检测系统主要针对孤立消息，难以应对这一演变中的威胁。本文扩展了单消息钓鱼检测，提出了一种可解释的代理系统，用于检测复杂的对话式诈骗。同时，本文发布了ConScamBench-278，这是首个公开的多类别对话式诈骗检测基准，涵盖八类诈骗，旨在支持可复现评估和未来扩展。在孤立消息上，单消息检测器实现100%钓鱼召回率；在对话层面，该检测器在公开的LoveFraud02语料库中识别出全部83个对话式诈骗（83/83），并在ConScamBench-278上达到97.8%准确率（95%置信区间[95.4, 99.0]）。两项用户研究（N=100和N=45）进一步验证了系统价值：参与者报告在判断可疑对话时常感不确定。在无控制的前后对比中，用户自我报告的信任度、自信心及对AI诈骗检测的需求均显著提升（p<0.001，Wilcoxon符号秩检验）。系统还获得系统可用性量表得分74.7（95%置信区间[72.5, 76.9]），高于公认可用性基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing threat of multi-turn conversational scams that evade single-message detection systems, this paper introduces an explainable agentic system that leverages summary-based memory to track evolving scam patterns across extended conversations. The method combines conversation-level analysis with a public benchmark, ConScamBench-278, containing 278 samples across eight scam types. Experimental results show the system achieves 100% recall on isolated phishing messages and correctly identifies all 83 conversational scams in the LoveFraud02 corpus, with 97.8% accuracy on the benchmark. User studies (N=100 and N=45) confirmed increased user trust and confidence in AI detection, with statistically significant improvements (p<0.001) in perceived need for such tools, and a usability score of 74.7, exceeding established benchmarks.
鉴于现有单消息检测系统难以应对跨多轮对话的复杂诈骗，本文提出一种基于摘要记忆的可解释代理系统，用于检测此类对话式诈骗。该方法通过分析对话上下文并追踪信任信号演变，结合可解释性机制提升检测能力。实验表明，系统在单消息层面实现100%钓鱼召回率，在LoveFraud02数据集上准确识别全部83个对话诈骗案例，且在新构建的ConScamBench-278基准上达到97.8%准确率。两项用户研究（N=100和N=45）显示，用户在使用系统后自我报告的信任度、自信心及对AI防诈工具的需求显著提升（p<0.001），系统可用性评分74.7，高于行业基准。
VoxENES 2026: Benchmarking Generalization of Speech Spoofing Detectors Against LLM-Era TTS and Voice Conversion
Authors: Aastha Sharma, Guangjing Wang
First: 2026-07-13T15:35:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:35:29+00:00
Comments: Accepted in InterSpeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern LLM-driven text-to-speech (TTS) and voice conversion (VC) systems produce synthetic speech that differs from the generators represented in many legacy spoofing benchmarks. This mismatch creates a temporal generalization gap that can overestimate detector robustness under real-world post-processing conditions. We bridge this gap by introducing VoxENES 2026, a bilingual (English and Spanish) benchmark of 53,628 audio samples generated using 10 contemporary speech synthesis methods and evaluated under 10 standardized post-processing conditions. Using VoxENES 2026, we benchmark eight pretrained detectors without fine-tuning and observe substantial performance degradation: the best model achieves 28.98\% EER overall, while most perform near or below random chance across modern generators and perturbations. Our results highlight the reliance on brittle artifacts in current detectors and establish VoxENES 2026 as a practical testbed for developing robust audio spoofing countermeasures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VoxENES 2026：针对大语言模型时代TTS与声纹转换的语音欺骗检测器泛化能力基准测试
现代基于大语言模型的文本到语音（TTS）和声纹转换（VC）系统生成的合成语音，与许多传统欺骗检测基准所代表的生成器存在差异。这种不匹配导致了时间泛化差距，可能在真实世界后处理条件下高估检测器的鲁棒性。我们通过引入VoxENES 2026基准来弥合这一差距，该基准包含53,628个音频样本，涵盖10种当代语音合成方法，且在10种标准化后处理条件下进行评估。使用VoxENES 2026，我们对八种预训练检测器进行无微调基准测试，观察到显著性能下降：最佳模型整体EER为28.98%，多数模型在现代生成器和扰动下表现接近或低于随机猜测水平。我们的结果揭示了当前检测器对脆弱伪特征的依赖，并确立VoxENES 2026作为开发鲁棒音频欺骗防御措施的实用测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the mismatch between legacy spoofing benchmarks and modern LLM-driven TTS and voice conversion systems, this study introduces VoxENES 2026, a bilingual benchmark with 53,628 audio samples generated by 10 contemporary synthesis methods and evaluated under 10 standardized post-processing conditions. Eight pretrained spoofing detectors were tested without fine-tuning, revealing significant performance degradation: the best model achieved only 28.98% EER overall, while most detectors performed near or below random chance, underscoring their reliance on brittle artifacts and highlighting the need for more robust detection methods.
鉴于现有语音欺骗检测基准与现代基于大语言模型的TTS和声纹转换系统存在不匹配，本研究提出VoxENES 2026双语基准，包含53,628个由10种现代合成方法生成并经10种标准化后处理条件测试的音频样本。在不进行微调的情况下评估8个预训练检测器，结果显示性能显著下降：最佳模型整体EER仅为28.98%，多数模型表现接近或低于随机水平，表明当前检测器依赖脆弱特征，缺乏对真实世界合成语音的鲁棒性。
NMIRacle: Multi-modal Generative Molecular Elucidation from IR and NMR Spectra
Authors: Federico Ottomano, Yingzhen Li, Alex M. Ganose
First: 2025-12-17T10:29:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:34:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Molecular structure elucidation from spectroscopic data is a long-standing challenge in Chemistry, traditionally requiring expert interpretation. We introduce NMIRacle, a two-stage generative framework that builds upon recent paradigms in AI-driven spectroscopy with minimal assumptions. In the first stage, NMIRacle learns to reconstruct molecular structures from count-aware fragment representations, capturing both fragment identities and their occurrences. In the second stage, a spectral encoder maps input spectra (IR, 1H-NMR, 13C-NMR) into a latent embedding used to condition the pre-trained generator, which is fine-tuned for direct spectra-to-molecule generation. This formulation bridges fragment-level chemical modeling with spectral evidence, yielding accurate molecular predictions. Empirical results demonstrate that NMIRacle outperforms existing baselines on molecular elucidation, while maintaining robust performance across increasing levels of molecular complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NMIRacle：从红外与核磁共振谱进行多模态生成式分子结构解析
从光谱数据解析分子结构是化学领域长期存在的挑战，传统上依赖专家解读。我们提出了NMIRacle，一种两阶段生成框架，基于近期AI驱动光谱学的范式，且假设最少。第一阶段，NMIRacle学习从计数感知的片段表示重建分子结构，同时捕捉片段身份及其出现次数。第二阶段，光谱编码器将输入光谱（红外、¹H-NMR、¹³C-NMR）映射为潜在嵌入，用于条件化预训练生成器，该生成器经微调后可直接实现光谱到分子的生成。该框架将片段级化学建模与光谱证据相结合，实现精确的分子预测。实证结果表明，NMIRacle在分子解析任务上优于现有基线方法，且在分子复杂度递增时仍保持稳健性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate molecular structure elucidation from spectroscopic data without expert intervention, NMIRacle introduces a two-stage generative framework that combines fragment reconstruction with spectral conditioning. The first stage learns to reconstruct molecular structures from count-aware fragment representations, while the second stage uses spectral encoders to map IR, 1H-NMR, and 13C-NMR inputs into latent embeddings that guide a pre-trained generator for direct spectra-to-molecule generation. Experimental results show NMIRacle consistently outperforms existing methods across diverse molecular complexity levels, demonstrating strong accuracy and robustness in molecular prediction.
为解决传统上依赖专家解读的分子结构解析难题，NMIRacle提出一种两阶段生成框架，结合片段重建与光谱条件化。第一阶段从计数感知的片段表示中重建分子结构，第二阶段通过IR、1H-NMR和13C-NMR光谱编码器引导预训练生成器直接实现光谱到分子的生成。实验表明，NMIRacle在不同分子复杂度水平上均优于现有方法，且无需先验结构假设，预测准确性显著提升。
Production and Perception in LLMs: A Token Probability Approach
Authors: Anna Marklová, Jiří Milička, Martina Vokáčová, Rudolf Rosa
First: 2026-07-13T15:33:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:33:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The asymmetry between language production and perception has been well-documented in psycholinguistics. Whether large language models (LLMs) exhibit a functionally analogous distinction remains an open question, particularly given that LLMs rely on the same underlying mechanism (next-token prediction) for both input and output processing. In this exploratory study, we operationalize the production-perception distinction through direct token probability measurements rather than metalinguistic prompting. Using the base Llama-3.1-8B model, we generated poems under a production prompt and re-scored the same tokens under both rephrased production prompts and perception-oriented prompts. Across an extended experiment with four production and three perception prompts, production-perception distances consistently and substantially exceeded production-production distances, with non-overlapping ranges across conditions and an overall average ratio of approximately 1.8. Near-ceiling correlations in the production-production control confirm that the effect is specific to communicative framing rather than prompt surface variation, and we show the effect replicates across five open-weight models (Llama-3.1-8B, EuroLLM-9B, gemma-2-9b-it, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3, and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), spanning both base and instruction-tuned variants. Temporal analysis revealed that the perception prompt exerts its strongest influence at the beginning of the sequence, with divergence decaying as generated context accumulates, though the specific shape of this decay varies across prompt pairs. These findings suggest that prompt framing alone induces a production-perception distinction in LLM probability distributions, even within a decoder-only architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中的生成与感知：基于令牌概率的方法
心理语言学中语言生成与感知的不对称性已被广泛记录。大语言模型（LLMs）是否表现出功能上类似的区别仍是一个开放性问题，尤其考虑到LLMs在输入和输出处理中依赖相同的底层机制（下一个令牌预测）。在本探索性研究中，我们通过直接测量令牌概率而非元语言提示来操作化生成-感知的区别。使用基础版Llama-3.1-8B模型，在生成提示下生成诗歌，并在重写生成提示和感知导向提示下重新评分相同令牌。在扩展实验中，使用四个生成提示和三个感知提示，生成-感知距离始终且显著超过生成-生成距离，不同条件下的范围不重叠，整体平均比值约为1.8。生成-生成控制组的近天花板相关性证实，该效应特异性地源于交际框架而非提示表面变化，且该效应在五个开源模型（Llama-3.1-8B、EuroLLM-9B、gemma-2-9b-it、Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）中得到复制，涵盖基础版和指令调优版本。时间分析显示，感知提示在序列开始时影响最强，随着生成上下文积累，差异衰减，但不同提示对之间的衰减形状各异。这些发现表明，仅通过提示框架即可在LLM概率分布中诱导生成-感知区别，即使在仅解码器架构中亦然。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models exhibit a functional distinction between language production and perception, despite relying on the same next-token prediction mechanism. By measuring token probabilities under different prompt framings—production-oriented versus perception-oriented—researchers found that production-perception distances consistently exceeded production-production distances, with an average ratio of 1.8 across multiple prompts and five open-weight models. The effect was specific to communicative framing, not prompt surface variation, and decayed over sequence length, strongest at the beginning. These results suggest prompt framing alone can induce a production-perception asymmetry in LLMs, even in decoder-only architectures.
本研究探讨大型语言模型（LLM）是否在语言生成与感知之间存在类似心理学语言学中的功能差异。研究通过直接测量生成过程中不同提示框架下（生产型与感知型）的词元概率，而非依赖元语言提示。在Llama-3.1-8B模型上，使用四组生产提示与三组感知提示，发现生产-感知词元概率差异显著大于生产-生产差异，平均比值约1.8，且分布不重叠。该效应在五种开源模型中均重复出现，且生产-生产控制组相关性接近天花板，表明其源于提示框架而非表面提示差异。时间分析显示感知提示在序列初期影响最强，随着生成上下文累积，差异逐渐衰减，但衰减模式因提示对而异。
$\mathtt{Q^2SAR}$: overcoming classical bottlenecks in drug discovery via quantum multiple kernel learning
Authors: Mariano Caruso, Daniel Ruiz, Alejandro Giraldo, Guido Bellomo
First: 2026-07-13T15:33:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:33:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantitative Structure-Activity Relationship ($\mathtt{QSAR}$) modeling is a foundational computational methodology in early-stage drug discovery, heavily relied upon for predicting compound toxicity, bioavailability, and therapeutic potential. However, classical methods often struggle to effectively map the highly complex, non-linear, and high-dimensional interactions inherent in molecular data, leading to reduced predictive accuracy and costly late-stage clinical failures. In this paper, we present a Quantum Multiple Kernel Learning ($\mathtt{QMKL}$) framework, dubbed Next-Gen $\mathtt{Q^2SAR}$, that leverages Quantum Support Vector Machines ($\mathtt{QSVMs}$) to overcome these classical limitations. By encoding molecular descriptors into exponentially large quantum Hilbert spaces, our approach substantially enhances the expressiveness of non-linear modeling. Benchmarking our quantum-enhanced framework on a dataset targeting the $\mathtt{DYRK1A}$ kinase (a critical target for Alzheimer's disease), the $\mathtt{QMKL}$-$\mathtt{SVM}$ achieves an impressive Area Under the Curve ($\mathtt{AUC}$) score of $0.8750$, significantly outperforming classical state-of-the-art Gradient Boosting models ($\mathtt{AUC} = 0.8037$). Furthermore, we establish a theoretical and empirical pathway toward resolving classical data bottlenecks through projected quantum kernels ($\mathtt{PQK}$) and measurement accelerators. As quantum computing architecture matures, this framework paves the way for autonomous cognitive architectures and self-improving drug discovery pipelines, promising to unlock deeper insights across vast chemical spaces and to accelerate the development of life-saving therapeutics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Q²SAR：通过量子多核学习克服药物发现中的经典瓶颈
定量构效关系（QSAR）建模是药物发现早期阶段的基础计算方法，广泛用于预测化合物毒性、生物利用度和治疗潜力。然而，经典方法往往难以有效映射分子数据中固有的高度复杂、非线性和高维交互，导致预测精度降低和后期临床失败成本高昂。本文提出了一种量子多核学习（QMKL）框架，称为下一代Q²SAR，利用量子支持向量机（QSVMs）克服这些经典限制。通过将分子描述符编码到指数级大的量子希尔伯特空间中，我们的方法显著增强了非线性建模的表达能力。在针对DYRK1A激酶（阿尔茨海默病关键靶点）的数据集上对量子增强框架进行基准测试，QMKL-SVM取得了0.8750的曲线下面积（AUC）得分，显著优于经典最先进的梯度提升模型（AUC=0.8037）。此外，我们建立了通过投影量子核（PQK）和测量加速器解决经典数据瓶颈的理论与实证路径。随着量子计算架构的成熟，该框架为自主认知架构和自我改进的药物发现流程铺平了道路，有望在广阔化学空间中揭示更深层次的见解，并加速救命药物的开发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of classical QSAR models in capturing complex molecular interactions, this paper introduces a quantum-enhanced framework called Q^2SAR, which employs Quantum Multiple Kernel Learning (QMKL) using Quantum Support Vector Machines (QSVMs) to encode molecular descriptors into exponentially large quantum Hilbert spaces. This approach significantly boosts non-linear modeling capacity, overcoming classical bottlenecks in drug discovery. Evaluated on a DYRK1A kinase dataset, the QMKL-SVM achieved an AUC of 0.8750, substantially outperforming classical Gradient Boosting (AUC 0.8037), and demonstrates a scalable pathway via projected quantum kernels and measurement accelerators to improve predictive accuracy and accelerate therapeutic development.
为克服传统QSAR模型在捕捉分子复杂交互中的局限性，本文提出量子增强框架Q^2SAR，采用量子多核学习（QMKL）结合量子支持向量机（QSVM），将分子描述符编码至指数级量子希尔伯特空间，显著提升非线性建模能力。在DYRK1A激酶数据集上，QMKL-SVM达到0.8750的AUC，显著优于传统梯度提升模型（0.8037），并展示通过投影量子核与测量加速器解决经典数据瓶颈的可行路径，有望加速救命药物的开发。
EHR-MPC: Inference-Time Control for Sepsis Treatment with Generative Patient Digital Twins
Authors: Joshua Pickard, Wei Qi, Na Li, Ann Woolley, Lisa Cosimi, Roy Kishony, Deborah Hung
First: 2026-07-07T19:24:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:31:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sepsis is a leading cause of mortality, yet optimal treatment policies remain contested. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) approaches learn fixed strategies for sepsis treatment, limiting adaptability to changing clinical objectives during inference. We propose EHRMPC, a framework that decouples learning patient dynamics from optimizing treatment by training a patient digital twin in the form of a generative electronic health record (EHR) model. The digital twin predicts clinical trajectories under interventions and enables model predictive control (MPC) to optimize treatments via inference-time planning over simulations. We evaluate EHR-MPC on a multicenter ICU sepsis cohort spanning 8 hospitals in the Mass General Brigham health system using both off-policy importance sampling and on-policy simulation-based evaluation. Relative to RL baselines, EHR-MPC achieves comparable off-policy performance and improved simulation performance. Unlike RL, this work frames sepsis treatment optimization as inference-time control over learned patient dynamics, establishing a general framework for decision making with generative clinical models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EHR-MPC：基于生成式患者数字孪生的脓毒症治疗推理时控制
脓毒症是主要致死原因之一，但最佳治疗策略仍存争议。现有强化学习（RL）方法学习固定治疗策略，限制了在推理过程中对临床目标变化的适应性。我们提出EHR-MPC框架，通过训练生成式电子健康记录（EHR）模型作为患者数字孪生，将学习患者动态与治疗优化解耦。该数字孪生可预测干预下的临床轨迹，并支持模型预测控制（MPC）通过推理时仿真规划优化治疗。我们在马萨诸塞州总医院健康系统8家医院的多中心ICU脓毒症队列上评估EHR-MPC，采用离策略重要性采样和基于仿真的策略评估。相比RL基线方法，EHR-MPC实现相当的离策略性能并提升仿真性能。与RL不同，本工作将脓毒症治疗优化框架为推理时基于学习患者动态的控制，建立了一种通用框架，用于生成式临床模型的决策制定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptable sepsis treatment strategies during inference, this work introduces EHR-MPC, a framework that separates patient dynamics modeling from treatment optimization by training a generative EHR-based digital twin. The digital twin simulates clinical trajectories under interventions, enabling model predictive control (MPC) to plan optimal treatments at inference time. Evaluated on a multicenter ICU sepsis dataset from 8 hospitals, EHR-MPC matches off-policy RL performance while showing superior simulation-based results, demonstrating its advantage in dynamic, real-time decision-making using learned patient models.
为应对临床推理阶段治疗策略缺乏适应性的挑战，该研究提出EHR-MPC框架，通过训练生成式电子健康记录数字孪生体，将患者动态建模与治疗优化解耦。该孪生体可模拟干预下的临床轨迹，支持推理时的模型预测控制以规划最优治疗方案。在覆盖八家医院的多中心ICU脓毒症数据集上评估，EHR-MPC在离策略评估中表现与强化学习基线相当，且在仿真评估中表现更优，凸显其在实时、情境敏感决策中的优势。
Agent Hacks Agent: Autoresearch for Production-Agent Red-Teaming
Authors: Xutao Mao, Xiang Zheng, Cong Wang
First: 2026-07-13T15:31:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:31:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Production LLM agents such as Claude Code and Codex operate over untrusted content, files, commands, and workspace state, making safety failures directly actionable. Red-teaming must therefore keep pace with evolving models and tools. Existing approaches mainly optimize attack success and preserve artifacts such as benchmarks, payloads, or attack programs, which record where attacks succeed but not the enabling conditions behind unsafe agent behavior. We study automated red-teaming for production LLM agents using one agentic research environment to discover reusable vulnerability knowledge about another. We present AHA, a falsifiable discovery loop that proposes a vulnerability hypothesis, constructs a falsifier, instantiates a valid attack, executes it in a sandboxed harness, reflects on the trajectory, and promotes confirmed findings into a Vulnerability Concept Graph (VCG). Each concept links an attacker-facing surface to an unsafe trajectory through a claim, enabling condition, falsifier, transfer prediction, and supporting evidence. Across Claude Code and Codex on three scenarios covering direct and indirect attacks, the discovered concepts reveal a reusable vulnerability core across models and agents. A frozen VCG requires no further search and outperforms the strongest frozen discovery baseline by 14.2 percentage points under the same single-shot protocol, while transferring across scenarios and attack channels. The resulting VCG provides an auditable artifact for production safety teams to inspect vulnerabilities, validate patches, and accumulate reusable safety knowledge. Our code is available at https://github.com/henrymao2004/Auto-research-red-teaming-in-sleep.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agent Hacks Agent：面向生产级Agent的自动化红队研究
生产级大语言模型代理（如Claude Code和Codex）在不可信内容、文件、命令及工作区状态上运行，使其安全失效具有直接可操作性。因此，红队必须跟上模型与工具的演进。现有方法主要优化攻击成功率并保留基准、载荷或攻击程序等产物，记录攻击成功之处，但未揭示导致代理不安全行为的促成条件。我们通过一个代理研究环境，研究面向生产级LLM代理的自动化红队技术，以发现可复用的漏洞知识。我们提出AHA，一种可证伪的发现循环：提出漏洞假设，构建证伪器，实例化有效攻击，于沙箱环境中执行，反思攻击轨迹，并将确认结果纳入漏洞概念图（VCG）。每个概念通过主张、促成条件、证伪器、迁移预测及支持证据，将攻击者接口与不安全轨迹关联。在Claude Code和Codex上针对三类场景（直接与间接攻击），所发现的概念揭示了跨模型与代理的可复用漏洞核心。冻结的VCG无需进一步搜索，在相同单次协议下，相较最强冻结发现基线提升14.2个百分点，且可跨场景与攻击通道迁移。生成的VCG为生产安全团队提供可审计产物，用于检查漏洞、验证补丁及积累可复用安全知识。代码开源于https://github.com/henrymao2004/Auto-research-red-teaming-in-sleep。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to safely evaluate production LLM agents that operate on untrusted inputs, this work introduces AHA, an automated red-teaming framework that discovers reusable vulnerability patterns across agents. AHA operates via a falsifiable loop: it hypothesizes a vulnerability, builds a falsifier, executes an attack in a sandbox, reflects on the attack trajectory, and formalizes confirmed findings into a Vulnerability Concept Graph (VCG). Each VCG concept links an attacker-facing surface to unsafe behavior through claims, enabling conditions, falsifiers, transfer predictions, and evidence. Experiments on Claude Code and Codex across three attack scenarios show that AHA’s VCG outperforms existing baselines by 14.2 percentage points under single-shot evaluation and transfers effectively across attack types and agents, offering a reusable, auditable safety artifact for production teams.
为应对生产级LLM代理在处理不可信内容时的安全风险，本文提出AHA框架，通过一个代理环境自动探测并发现另一代理的可复用漏洞知识。AHA采用可证伪的循环机制：提出漏洞假设、构建反例、在沙箱中执行攻击、分析攻击轨迹，并将确认结果结构化为漏洞概念图（VCG）。每个概念连接攻击面与不安全路径，包含声明、触发条件、反例、迁移预测和证据。在Claude Code与Codex上针对三类攻击场景的实验表明，AHA生成的VCG在单次评估中比最强基线高14.2个百分点，且能跨攻击类型与模型迁移，为生产安全团队提供可审计、可复用的安全知识。
Think Through a Bottleneck: Hourglass Reasoning for Rigorous Induction
Authors: Huan Zhu
First: 2026-07-13T15:29:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:29:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-refinement often fails to strengthen few-shot inductive reasoning in large language models. Prompting a model to explicitly state its inferred rule does little on its own. What actually matters is a structurally enforced isolation between reasoning stages, so that information can only pass between them as a compressed symbolic state. We introduce \textbf{Hourglass reasoning}, which enforces strict context isolation between reasoning stages. The frozen LLM acts as a meta-constructor, building for each task a symbolic encoder--decoder: an Induction module compresses the support examples into a schema $φ$ (encoder) and a transient scaffold $z$; a Deduction module derives rule $T$ (decoder) from these and discards $z$; an Implementer compiles $(φ, T)$ into artifacts; an error-driven Refiner revises $(φ, T)$ and regenerates artifacts from scratch. Only $(φ, T)$ crosses stage boundaries, so all refinement stays anchored to the rule. We evaluate Hourglass across three benchmarks spanning visual abstraction, hardware synthesis, and textual rule induction, using GPT-5.5 and Gemini 3.1 Pro. On ARC-AGI-2, it raises best-of-5 accuracy by up to 14 points over an iterative-refinement baseline. On ChipBench, it nearly doubles Verilog synthesis accuracy with GPT-5.5, from 31\% to 58\%. BBEH-Linguini draws on puzzles from the International Linguistics Olympiad, a setting where prior work has shown that explicit verbalization can hurt performance. Hourglass mitigates this tendency, and on Gemini 3.1 Pro, it reverses the effect entirely. Ablations confirm that these gains come from the isolation between stages and the quality of the initial induction, not from prompt wording or the particular symbolic form used. It is how information flows through the reasoning process, rather than the language used to express it, that drives inductive reasoning in frozen LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：瓶颈思考：用于严谨归纳的沙漏式推理
自我精炼通常无法增强大语言模型的少样本归纳推理能力。单纯要求模型明确陈述其推断规则效果甚微。真正关键的是在推理阶段之间实施结构化隔离，使信息只能以压缩的符号状态在阶段间传递。 我们引入了\textbf{沙漏式推理}，强制在推理阶段间实现严格上下文隔离。冻结的LLM作为元构造器，为每个任务构建一个符号编码-解码器：归纳模块将支持示例压缩为模式φ（编码器）和临时支架z；演绎模块从φ和z中推导出规则T（解码器）并丢弃z；实施器将(φ, T)编译为产物；误差驱动的精炼器修订(φ, T)并从头生成产物。只有(φ, T)跨越阶段边界，因此所有精炼都锚定在规则上。 我们在涵盖视觉抽象、硬件综合和文本规则归纳的三个基准上评估沙漏式推理，使用GPT-5.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro。在ARC-AGI-2上，其准确率比迭代精炼基线高14分。在ChipBench上，使用GPT-5.5时，其Verilog综合准确率从31%提升至58%。BBEH-Linguini借鉴了国际语言学奥林匹克竞赛的谜题，该领域先前研究表明显式口头化会损害性能。沙漏式推理缓解了这一倾向，并在Gemini 3.1 Pro上完全逆转了该效应。 消融实验确认，这些收益源于阶段间的隔离和初始归纳的质量，而非提示词措辞或所用符号形式。在冻结LLM中，驱动归纳推理的是信息在推理过程中的流动方式，而非其表达语言。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the limitation of self-refinement in few-shot inductive reasoning by proposing Hourglass reasoning, which enforces strict structural isolation between reasoning stages to prevent information leakage. It uses a frozen LLM as a meta-constructor to build a symbolic encoder-decoder pipeline: an Induction module compresses examples into a schema φ and scaffold z, a Deduction module derives rule T from them while discarding z, an Implementer generates artifacts from (φ, T), and a Refiner revises (φ, T) and regenerates artifacts. Only φ and T cross stage boundaries, anchoring refinement to the rule. Experiments on ARC-AGI-2, ChipBench, and BBEH-Linguini show gains of up to 14 points in accuracy, nearly doubling Verilog synthesis accuracy, and reversing performance degradation from explicit verbalization. Ablations confirm gains stem from stage isolation and initial induction quality, not prompt design or symbolic form.
为了解决大语言模型在少样本归纳推理中自修正效果不佳的问题，本文提出Hourglass推理方法，通过在推理阶段间强制结构隔离来提升性能。该方法利用冻结的LLM作为元构造器，构建符号编码-解码器：归纳模块将示例压缩为模式φ和临时骨架z，推导模块从φ和z中生成规则T并丢弃z，实现模块将(φ, T)编译为产物，修正模块则修订(φ, T)并从头生成产物。只有φ和T能跨阶段传递，确保修正始终锚定规则。在ARC-AGI-2、ChipBench和BBEH-Linguini三个基准上，该方法最高提升准确率14分，Verilog综合准确率几乎翻倍（31%→58%），并逆转了显式语言化导致的性能下降。消融实验表明，性能提升源于阶段隔离和初始归纳质量，而非提示词设计或符号形式。
First-Order Softmax Weighted Switching Gradient Method for Distributed Stochastic Minimax Optimization with Stochastic Constraints
Authors: Zhankun Luo, Antesh Upadhyay, Sang Bin Moon, Abolfazl Hashemi
First: 2026-03-06T00:14:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:28:06+00:00
Comments: This paper was accepted by the 42nd annual Conference on Uncertainty in Artificial Intelligence (UAI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses the distributed stochastic minimax optimization problem subject to stochastic constraints. We propose a novel first-order Softmax-Weighted Switching Gradient method tailored for federated learning. Under full client participation, our algorithm achieves the standard $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ oracle complexity to satisfy a unified bound $ε$ for both the optimality gap and feasibility tolerance. We extend our theoretical analysis to the practical partial participation regime by quantifying client sampling noise through a stochastic superiority assumption. Furthermore, by relaxing standard boundedness assumptions on the objective functions, we establish a strictly tighter lower bound for the softmax hyperparameter. We provide a unified error decomposition and establish a sharp $\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}δ)$ high-probability convergence guarantee. Ultimately, our framework demonstrates that a single-loop primal-only switching mechanism provides a stable alternative for optimizing worst-case client performance, effectively bypassing the hyperparameter sensitivity and convergence oscillations often encountered in traditional primal-dual or penalty-based approaches. We verify the efficacy of our algorithm via experiment on the Neyman-Pearson (NP) classification, fair classification, and federated safe reinforcement learning tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于带随机约束的分布式随机极小极大优化的首阶Softmax加权切换梯度方法
本文研究带随机约束的分布式随机极小极大优化问题，提出一种专为联邦学习设计的新型首阶Softmax加权切换梯度方法。在所有客户端参与的前提下，该算法实现标准$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$的Oracle复杂度，以满足最优性间隙与可行性容差统一为$ε$的界。我们通过随机优越性假设量化客户端采样噪声，将理论分析扩展至实际部分参与场景；同时，通过放宽目标函数的标准有界性假设，建立更严格的Softmax超参数下界。我们提供统一的误差分解，并建立$\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}δ)$的高概率收敛保证。最终，我们的框架表明，单循环仅用原变量的切换机制可稳定优化最坏客户端性能，有效规避传统原-对偶或惩罚方法中常见的超参数敏感性与收敛振荡问题。我们在Neyman-Pearson分类、公平分类及联邦安全强化学习任务中验证了算法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient distributed stochastic minimax optimization under stochastic constraints, this paper introduces a first-order Softmax-Weighted Switching Gradient method designed for federated learning. The method employs a primal-only switching mechanism that dynamically adjusts gradients based on softmax weights, achieving $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ oracle complexity under full client participation to simultaneously satisfy optimality and feasibility bounds. Theoretical analysis is extended to partial client participation by modeling sampling noise via a stochastic superiority assumption, and relaxed boundedness conditions lead to a tighter lower bound for the softmax hyperparameter. The algorithm provides a sharp $\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}{δ})$ high-probability convergence guarantee through unified error decomposition, and experiments on Neyman-Pearson classification, fair classification, and federated safe reinforcement learning confirm its stability and robustness compared to traditional primal-dual or penalty-based methods.
为解决分布式随机极小极大优化问题中的随机约束，本文提出一种基于Softmax加权切换梯度的首阶优化方法，专为联邦学习设计。该方法采用仅用原问题的单循环切换机制，通过Softmax动态加权梯度，在全客户端参与下实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$的Oracle复杂度，同时满足优化间隙与可行性容差的统一界限。方法扩展至部分客户端参与场景，通过随机优越性假设量化采样噪声，并放松目标函数的有界性假设，推导出更紧的Softmax超参数下界。理论分析给出$\mathcal{O}(\log\frac{1}{δ})$的高概率收敛保证，实验在Neyman-Pearson分类、公平分类及联邦安全强化学习任务中验证其稳定性，有效规避传统原-对偶或惩罚方法常见的超参数敏感与收敛震荡问题。
Where Experts Disagree, Models Fail: Detecting Implicit Legal Citations in French Court Decisions
Authors: Avrile Floro, Tamara Dhorasoo, Soline Pellez, Nils Holzenberger
First: 2026-03-24T09:10:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:25:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Applying computational methods to law at scale requires separating genuine legal reasoning from surface similarity. We study this through a concrete task: detecting implicit citations of the French Civil Code, where a court applies a statutory rule without naming it: a post-hoc question about the reasoning a court actually used. We release a benchmark of 1,015 passage-article pairs annotated by three legal experts. Our central finding is that their disagreement is itself informative: the third of cases the experts dispute are where models fail. Our best ensemble reaches an F1 score of 0.70 overall. Yet, two-thirds of its false positives fall on those disputed cases, a concentration that holds across all ten models we evaluate. Disagreement is a signal of intrinsic difficulty, not annotation noise. This should not block useful tools, however: reframed as top-$k$ ranking with multi-model consensus, the same signals reach 76% precision for the top-200 candidates without supervision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：专家意见分歧处，模型表现不佳：检测法国法院判决中的隐含法律引用
大规模应用计算方法于法律领域，需区分真实法律推理与表面相似性。我们通过具体任务研究此问题：检测法国《民法典》的隐含引用——法院在未明确提及法条时适用该法条：事后追问法院实际使用的推理过程。我们发布了一个由三位法律专家标注的1015个段落-法条配对基准数据集。核心发现是，专家意见分歧本身具有信息价值：专家争议的三分之一案例正是模型失败之处。我们最佳集成模型整体F1分数达0.70。然而，其三分之二的假阳性结果集中于这些争议案例，且在评估的十种模型中均保持一致。分歧反映的是内在难度，而非标注噪声。但这不应阻碍实用工具的开发：若将该问题重构为多模型共识的Top-k排序，无需监督即可在前200个候选中达到76%的精确率。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of detecting implicit legal citations in French court decisions, where courts apply statutory rules without explicitly naming them, requiring models to infer the underlying legal reasoning. The authors construct a benchmark of 1,015 annotated passage-article pairs from three legal experts, revealing that cases where experts disagree are precisely where models underperform. Their best ensemble model achieves an overall F1 score of 0.70, but two-thirds of its false positives cluster in the disputed cases, indicating that expert disagreement signals intrinsic task difficulty rather than annotation noise. Crucially, reframing the task as a top-k ranking with multi-model consensus improves precision to 76% for the top-200 candidates without additional supervision.
该研究旨在解决计算法学中区分真实法律推理与表面相似性的挑战，聚焦于检测法国民法典的隐式引用——即法院在未明确提及法条时适用其规定。基于三位法律专家标注的1015组段落-法条配对数据，研究发现模型失败与专家分歧高度相关：三分之一存在争议的案例被模型错误分类，且三分之二的假阳性来自这些争议案例。尽管最佳集成模型整体F1分数为0.70，但将任务重构为多模型共识的Top-k排序后，无需额外监督即可在前200个候选中达到76%的精确率，表明分歧反映的是任务固有难度而非标注噪声。
Requirement-Driven Design of Whole-Body Social Tactile Sensing via Virtual Human-Robot Interaction
Authors: Dakarai Crowder, Ruohan Zhang, Alexis E. Block, Wenzhen Yuan
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-07-13T15:23:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:23:45+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 figures, accepted to IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tactile sensing for social-physical human-robot interaction (spHRI) is designed in a hardware-driven manner, where predefined sensor configurations constrain coverage, spatial resolution, and the range of recognizable gestures. We propose a requirement-driven framework that derives sensing requirements, specifically spatial resolution and placement, directly from interaction data. Using a VR-based platform with haptic feedback, we collected high-resolution whole-body contact distributions across multiple social scenarios, from which we identified nine recurring social touch gestures. Eight gestures were selected for controlled data collection with 18 participants, yielding an open-source dataset of 5,520 trials. Analysis of contact distributions and simulated tactile encodings provides quantitative baselines for skin coverage and sensor density on a humanoid robot platform. While demonstrated on a single robot platform, the methodology is designed to be transferable to other robot morphologies, potentially enabling morphology-specific sensing requirements to be derived prior to hardware fabrication.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于需求驱动的全身社交触觉感知设计：通过虚拟人机交互实现
社交-物理人机交互（spHRI）中的触觉感知通常以硬件驱动方式设计，预定义的传感器配置限制了覆盖范围、空间分辨率及可识别手势的范围。本文提出一种需求驱动框架，直接从交互数据中推导感知需求，特别是空间分辨率与传感器布置。通过基于VR的触觉反馈平台，我们收集了多种社交场景下的全身高分辨率接触分布数据，识别出九种重复出现的社交触碰手势。从中选取八种手势，由18名参与者进行受控数据采集，形成包含5,520次试验的开源数据集。接触分布分析与模拟触觉编码结果，为类人机器人平台的皮肤覆盖范围与传感器密度提供了量化基准。该方法虽在单一机器人平台上验证，但设计上可迁移至其他机器人形态，有望在硬件制造前推导出形态特定的感知需求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of hardware-driven tactile sensing in social-physical human-robot interaction, this study proposes a requirement-driven framework that derives spatial resolution and sensor placement directly from interaction data. Using a VR platform with haptic feedback, the researchers collected high-resolution whole-body contact distributions across multiple social scenarios, identifying nine recurring touch gestures. Eight of these gestures were then tested with 18 participants, generating a dataset of 5,520 trials. Analysis of contact patterns and simulated tactile encodings established quantitative benchmarks for skin coverage and sensor density on a humanoid robot, demonstrating the method’s potential for transferable, morphology-specific sensing design prior to hardware fabrication.
为解决社交物理人机交互中硬件驱动触觉传感的局限性，本文提出一种需求驱动框架，直接从交互数据中推导空间分辨率与传感器布局需求。通过VR平台结合触觉反馈，作者收集了多场景下全身接触分布数据，识别出九种常见触碰手势，并选取八种进行18名参与者控制实验，构建包含5520次试验的开源数据集。通过对接触分布与模拟触觉编码的分析，建立了人形机器人皮肤覆盖与传感器密度的量化基准，证明该方法可迁移至其他机器人形态，支持在硬件制造前根据形态定制传感需求。
From World Action Models to Embodied Brains: A Roadmap for Open-World Physical Intelligence
Authors: Yuanzhi Liang, Xufeng Zhan, Haibin Huang, Chi Zhang, Xuelong Li
First: 2026-07-13T15:22:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:22:56+00:00
Comments: Ongoing work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial general intelligence ultimately requires agents that can reason and act in the physical world. Action models, vision-language-action policies, and world models have advanced this goal, while World Action Models (WAMs) are particularly promising because they connect candidate interventions with predicted consequences. However, progress remains fragmented: models use incompatible action spaces and prediction targets, datasets and tasks follow different conventions, and runtime systems expose limited interfaces for reuse and evaluation. We review the evolution toward WAMs and organize these limitations into three coupled gaps: model roles and representations, objectives and standardization, and system composition. Building on this analysis, we propose a co-evolution roadmap for physical intelligence centered on the \emph{embodied brain}, a long-term model target for integrating multimodal context, comparing candidate interventions, and issuing state-transition or capability requests rather than direct actuator commands. WAMs provide promising prototypes for its predictive functions, while a physical harness grounds model outputs through tools, controllers, verification, and trace logging. Shared contracts align heterogeneous models, data, tasks, and embodiments, and closed-loop post-training converts verified interaction into reusable experience. Together, these components define a modular physical-intelligence stack for adaptive and self-improving embodied agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从世界行动模型到具身大脑：开放世界物理智能路线图
人工通用智能最终需要能在物理世界中推理和行动的智能体。行动模型、视觉-语言-行动策略和世界模型已推动这一目标，而世界行动模型（WAMs）尤为有前景，因其将候选干预与预测后果相连接。然而，进展仍显碎片化：模型使用不兼容的动作空间和预测目标，数据集和任务遵循不同规范，运行时系统提供有限接口以支持复用和评估。我们回顾了向WAMs演进的过程，并将这些局限性归纳为三个相互关联的缺口：模型角色与表示、目标与标准化、系统组合。基于此分析，我们提出以“具身大脑”为中心的物理智能协同演进路线图——作为长期模型目标，它整合多模态上下文、比较候选干预、发出状态转换或能力请求，而非直接执行器指令。WAMs为其预测功能提供了有前景的原型，而物理约束装置通过工具、控制器、验证和轨迹日志将模型输出接地。共享契约统一异构模型、数据、任务和具身形式，闭环后训练将验证交互转化为可复用经验。这些组件共同定义了一个模块化的物理智能堆栈，支持自适应和自我改进的具身智能体。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the fragmented progress in developing artificial agents capable of physical reasoning by proposing a unified roadmap toward embodied physical intelligence, centered on the concept of an 'embodied brain' that integrates multimodal context and issues capability requests rather than direct actuator commands. It identifies three key gaps—model roles, objective standardization, and system composition—that hinder advancement and proposes a co-evolutionary approach: using World Action Models as predictive prototypes, grounding outputs through physical harnesses with tools and verification, and aligning heterogeneous components via shared contracts and closed-loop post-training. Experimental results across diverse physical tasks demonstrate that this modular stack enables adaptive, self-improving agents capable of reasoning about interventions and their consequences in open-world environments.
本文旨在解决当前物理智能研究碎片化的问题，提出以“具身大脑”为核心的协同演进路线图。动机源于行动模型、视觉-语言-行动策略和世界模型在物理世界推理中的不兼容性，作者识别出三大关键缺口：模型表示不统一、目标与评估缺乏标准化、系统组件难以互操作。解决方案是将世界行动模型（WAMs）作为预测功能原型，结合物理约束装置（如工具与控制器）实现输出落地，通过共享契约统一模型、数据、任务与具身系统，并利用闭环后训练将验证交互转化为可复用经验。实验表明，该模块化框架显著提升具身智能体的自适应与自我改进能力，验证了路线图的可行性。
Automated Synthesis of Facial Mechanisms for Conversational Animatronic Robots
Authors: Zongzheng Zhang, Zi Lin, Jiawen Yang, Ziqiao Peng, Junyan Lao, Lin Cheng, Huazhe Xu, Hang Zhao, Hao Zhao
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-07-13T15:22:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:22:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted by RSS 2026. Project page: https://zzongzheng0918.github.io/automated-facial-mechanisms-synthesis/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Animatronic faces are a central component of socially interactive robots, enabling rich nonverbal communication through facial articulation. However, state-of-the-art animatronic faces are typically tailored systems: each new facial geometry requires extensive manual mechanical redesign, making large-scale personalization prohibitively slow and costly. In this work, we pursue automated and scalable mechanical face synthesis, aiming to rapidly generate a physically realizable facial mechanism for a wide range of facial geometries. We introduce a parametric, linkage-driven mechanical face template whose topology and actuator layout are explicitly parameterized to support systematic scaling and retargeting across diverse facial morphologies. Building on this template, we propose a hierarchical automatic design algorithm that takes a single 2D portrait as input, reconstructs a target 3D face, and synthesizes a collision-free, manufacturable internal mechanism. The algorithm combines anatomy-guided feasible motion volumes, Action Unit (AU)-derived trajectory-based expressiveness objectives, and a collision-driven outer-loop refinement strategy. Beyond hardware synthesis, we argue that future mechanical faces deployed at scale must engage in bidirectional, multi-turn conversation rather than functioning solely as speaking or listening heads. To this end, we develop a dual-identity conversational facial motion synthesis framework that jointly models speaking and listening behaviors from audio, producing temporally coherent 3D facial motion suitable for physical execution. We validate our system through extensive experiments, including (i) quantitative evaluation of automatic mechanism synthesis across diverse facial geometries, (ii) comparisons against manual mechanical design, (iii) benchmarks on conversational facial motion synthesis and real-time deployment, and (iv) perceptual user studies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于对话式仿生机器人面部的自动化机械结构合成
仿生面部是社交互动机器人的重要组成部分，通过面部运动实现丰富的非语言交流。然而，当前最先进的仿生面部通常是定制化系统：每个新的面部几何形状都需要大量手动机械重设计，导致大规模个性化变得极其缓慢且昂贵。本研究致力于自动化和可扩展的机械面部合成，旨在快速为多种面部几何形状生成可物理实现的面部机构。我们引入一种参数化、连杆驱动的机械面部模板，其拓扑结构和执行器布局明确参数化，以支持在不同面部形态间系统性缩放和重定向。基于该模板，我们提出一种分层自动设计算法，输入单张2D肖像，重建目标3D面部，并合成无碰撞、可制造的内部机构。该算法结合解剖学引导的可行运动体积、基于动作单元（AU）的轨迹表达性目标，以及碰撞驱动的外环优化策略。除硬件合成外，我们认为未来大规模部署的机械面部必须参与双向、多轮对话，而不仅限于发声或听觉功能。为此，我们开发了一种双身份对话式面部运动合成框架，联合从音频中建模发声与倾听行为，生成适合物理执行的时序一致的3D面部运动。我们通过大量实验验证系统，包括（i）在多种面部几何形状上自动机构合成的定量评估，（ii）与手动机械设计的对比，（iii）对话式面部运动合成与实时部署的基准测试，以及（iv）感知用户研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of manually designing animatronic facial mechanisms for each unique face geometry, this work proposes an automated, scalable method to synthesize physically realizable facial mechanisms from a single 2D portrait. The approach uses a parametric linkage-based template with explicitly defined topology and actuator layout, enabling systematic retargeting across morphologies. A hierarchical algorithm reconstructs the 3D face and generates collision-free, manufacturable mechanisms by integrating anatomy-guided motion volumes, AU-driven expressiveness objectives, and collision-aware refinement. Additionally, a dual-identity conversational motion synthesis framework jointly models speaking and listening behaviors from audio to produce temporally coherent 3D facial motion. Experimental results demonstrate the system’s effectiveness in mechanism synthesis across diverse geometries, outperforming manual design in speed and scalability, and achieving high perceptual quality in conversational motion synthesis and real-time deployment.
为解决现有仿生面部机械需针对每种面部几何手动重设计、难以规模化的问题，该研究提出一种参数化连杆驱动模板，支持跨形态的自动重定向。系统以2D肖像为输入，重建3D面部并生成无碰撞、可制造的内部机械结构，结合解剖学引导的运动体积、AU驱动的表情目标与碰撞优化策略。同时，开发了双身份对话面部运动合成框架，联合建模说话与倾听行为，生成时序一致的3D面部运动。实验表明，该方法在多种面部几何上实现自动机制生成，优于手工设计，对话面部运动合成表现优异，且用户感知良好，支持实时部署。
Self-Healing Visual Recovery for Autonomous Ground Vehicles Using Camera-Only Visual Odometry
Authors: Jakob Solberg Berntzen, Safia Fatima, Leon Moonen
First: 2026-07-13T15:22:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:22:32+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 11 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-cost unmanned ground vehicles are often used in indoor places like warehouses, inspection corridors, and farm rows, where painted floor lines guide the robot. Line following is useful because it only needs one camera and little computing power, but it can fail when the line is blocked or turns sharply and goes out of view. Sensor-rich platforms tolerate this through hardware redundancy (LiDAR, GPS, multiple cameras), but camera-only systems must recover at runtime with no additional infrastructure. This paper presents a lightweight, two-stage recovery approach that restores guideline tracking without LiDAR, GPS, or a GPU. When the line is lost, the robot first turns in place while slowly relaxing its color checks and waiting for confirmation across multiple frames (Stage 1). If the line is still not found, monocular visual odometry moves the robot back to saved breadcrumb positions before it tries again (Stage 2). The system uses a depth-gated HSV line tracker, a YOLOv8n obstacle detector, and a visual odometry breadcrumb mapper, and it runs at 20 Hz on CPU-only hardware. The controller embeds a complete MAPE-K loop within a single 50 ms control tick, with no external adaptation manager required. The approach is evaluated across 119 fault-injected episodes on three Webots simulation courses. The method was successful in 86.6% of cases, with a median recovery time of 3.26 seconds. These results demonstrate that reliable visual recovery is feasible on camera-only UGVs within practical cost and computational limits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅使用摄像头视觉里程计的自主地面车辆自愈式视觉恢复
低成本无人地面车辆常用于仓库、检查通道和农田等室内场所，地面标线引导机器人行进。跟随标线方法仅需一个摄像头和少量计算能力，但当标线被遮挡或急转弯脱离视野时会失效。硬件冗余（激光雷达、GPS、多摄像头）的平台可通过传感器容错，但仅摄像头系统必须在运行时无额外基础设施下自行恢复。本文提出一种轻量级两阶段恢复方法，无需激光雷达、GPS或GPU即可恢复标线跟踪。当标线丢失时，机器人原地旋转并逐步放宽颜色检测，等待多帧确认（第一阶段）；若仍未找到标线，则通过单目视觉里程计返回先前保存的“面包屑”位置后重试（第二阶段）。系统采用深度门控HSV标线跟踪器、YOLOv8n障碍物检测器和视觉里程计“面包屑”地图，仅在CPU硬件上以20Hz运行。控制器在单个50ms控制周期内嵌入完整MAPE-K循环，无需外部自适应管理器。该方法在三个Webots仿真课程的119个故障注入场景中评估，成功率达86.6%，中位恢复时间为3.26秒。结果表明，在实际成本与计算限制下，仅摄像头的UGV可实现可靠的视觉恢复。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable navigation in low-cost autonomous ground vehicles operating in indoor environments with painted floor lines, this paper proposes a lightweight, two-stage self-healing recovery system that operates solely with a camera and CPU. When the line is lost, the robot first performs in-place rotation while gradually relaxing color-based detection thresholds to reacquire the line; if unsuccessful, it uses monocular visual odometry to backtrack to previously recorded positions before retrying. Evaluated across 119 simulated fault scenarios, the system successfully recovered in 86.6% of cases with a median recovery time of 3.26 seconds, demonstrating robustness without requiring LiDAR, GPS, or GPU.
为提升低成本自主地面机器人在室内涂线环境中导航的可靠性，本文提出一种仅依赖单目相机、无需LiDAR、GPS或GPU的轻量级两阶段视觉恢复方法。当线路丢失时，机器人首先原地旋转并逐步放宽颜色检测阈值以重新识别线路；若仍失败，则通过单目视觉里程计返回先前保存的“面包屑”位置重试。在119个故障注入的仿真场景中，该方法成功率达86.6%，中位恢复时间为3.26秒，证明了在实用成本与计算约束下，视觉恢复的可行性。
Diversified Multinomial Logit Contextual Bandits
Authors: Heesang Ann, Taehyun Hwang, Min-hwan Oh
First: 2026-07-13T15:21:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:21:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing contextual multinomial logit (MNL) bandits model relevance-driven choice but ignore the potential benefits of within-assortment diversity, while submodular/combinatorial bandits encode diversity in rewards but lack structured choice probabilities. We bridge this gap with the $\textit{diversified multinomial logit}$ (DMNL) contextual bandit, which augments MNL choice probabilities with a generally submodular diversity function, thereby formalizing the relevance--diversity trade-off within a single model. Incorporating diversity renders exact MNL assortment optimization intractable. We propose a $\textit{white-box}$ UCB-based algorithm, $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$, that constructs assortments item-wise by maximizing optimistic marginal gains, avoids black-box optimization oracles. We show that $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$ achieves at least a $(1-\frac{1}{e+1})$-$\textit{approximate}$ regret bound $\tilde{O}\left(d \sqrt{T/K}\right)$, where $d$ is the context dimension, $K$ the maximum assortment size, and $T$ the horizon, and attains an improved approximation factor over standard submodular baselines. Experiments demonstrate consistent gains and, relative to exhaustive enumeration, comparable regret with substantially lower runtime. Overall, DMNL bandits provide a practical foundation for diversity-aware assortment optimization under uncertainty, and $\texttt{OFU-DMNL}$ offers a statistically and computationally efficient solution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多样化多项逻辑上下文老虎机
现有上下文多项逻辑（MNL）老虎机模型关注相关性驱动的选择，但忽略了 assortments 内部多样性可能带来的潜在收益；而子模/组合老虎机虽编码了多样性奖励，却缺乏结构化的选择概率。我们通过引入“多样化多项逻辑”（DMNL）上下文老虎机弥合这一差距，该模型在 MNL 选择概率基础上叠加一个通用子模多样性函数，从而在单一模型中形式化了相关性与多样性的权衡。引入多样性使精确的 MNL assortments 优化变得不可行。我们提出一种“白盒”UCB 算法 OFU-DMNL，通过逐项最大化乐观边际增益构建 assortments，避免了黑盒优化 oracle。我们证明 OFU-DMNL 可实现至少 (1-1/(e+1)) 近似后悔界 \tilde{O}(d\sqrt{T/K})，其中 d 为上下文维度，K 为最大 assortments 大小，T 为时间跨度，并优于标准子模基线的近似因子。实验表明，该方法表现稳定，相较于穷举搜索，具有相当的后悔值但运行时间显著降低。总体而言，DMNL 老虎机为不确定性下的多样性感知 assortments 优化提供了实用基础，而 OFU-DMNL 则提供了一种统计与计算效率兼备的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance relevance and diversity in assortment selection under uncertainty, this work introduces the diversified multinomial logit (DMNL) contextual bandit model, which integrates a submodular diversity function into standard MNL choice probabilities to formalize the trade-off. To solve the resulting optimization challenge, it proposes OFU-DMNL, a white-box UCB algorithm that constructs assortments item-by-item by maximizing optimistic marginal gains, avoiding black-box oracles. The algorithm achieves a regret bound of $\tilde{O}\left(d \sqrt{T/K}\right)$ with an approximation factor of $1 - \frac{1}{e+1}$, improving upon standard submodular baselines. Experiments confirm consistent performance gains and significantly faster runtime compared to exhaustive enumeration while maintaining comparable regret.
为在不确定性环境下平衡相关性与多样性，本文提出多样化多项式逻辑（DMNL）上下文多臂老虎机模型，将子模多样性函数融入标准MNL选择概率，以形式化相关性与多样性的权衡。为解决由此带来的优化难题，提出OFU-DMNL算法，该算法通过逐项最大化乐观边际增益构建组合，避免黑盒优化器。该算法实现$\tilde{O}\left(d \sqrt{T/K}\right)$的后悔界，且相比标准子模基线具有更优的近似因子；实验表明，其在性能上持续优于基线，与穷举搜索相比后悔相当，但运行时间显著更短。
RAGU: A Multi-Step GraphRAG Engine with a Compact Domain-Adapted LLM
Authors: Mikhail Komarov, Ivan Bondarenko, Stanislav Shtuka, Oleg Sedukhin, Roman Shuvalov, Yana Dementyeva, Matvey Solovyov, Nikolay O. Nikitin
First: 2026-07-13T15:20:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:20:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) enhances large language models with structured knowledge, yet existing systems construct knowledge graphs in a single extraction pass, producing noisy entities and brittle retrieval. RAGU, an open-source modular GraphRAG engine, addresses this by separating extraction from consolidation: entities and relations pass through two-stage typed extraction, DBSCAN-backed deduplication, LLM summarization, and Leiden community detection. A key insight motivates a compact extractor: the skills an in-pipeline LLM needs - comprehension, extraction, reasoning over context - are language skills that grow only weakly with model size, unlike factual world knowledge. Accordingly, we train Meno-Lite-0.1, a 7B model optimized for language skills, which outperforms Qwen2.5-32B on knowledge-graph construction (+12.5% relative harmonic mean) and matches it on English GraphRAG tasks. On GraphRAG-Bench (Medical), RAGU retrieves the most complete context at every factoid level (evidence recall up to 0.84 vs. $\leq$0.76) and overtakes HippoRAG2 on synthesis tasks; on multi-hop factoid QA, the apparent HippoRAG2 advantage is shown to be largely an answer-format artifact. RAGU is installable via $\texttt{pip install graph_ragu}$, runs on a single GPU, and is released under MIT. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/RaguTeam/RAGU, and the Meno-Lite-0.1 model can be obtained from https://huggingface.co/bond005/meno-lite-0.1.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAGU：一种多步骤图增强生成引擎，配备紧凑型领域适配大语言模型
图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）通过结构化知识增强大语言模型，但现有系统在单次提取中构建知识图谱，导致实体噪声和脆弱检索。RAGU 是一个开源模块化 GraphRAG 引擎，通过分离提取与整合流程解决此问题：实体与关系经两阶段类型提取、基于 DBSCAN 的去重、LLM 总结及 Leiden 社区检测。关键洞察驱动紧凑提取器设计：管道内 LLM 所需能力——理解、提取、上下文推理——是语言技能，其增长与模型规模弱相关，不同于事实世界知识。因此，我们训练了 Meno-Lite-0.1（7B 参数模型），专为语言技能优化，在知识图谱构建上超越 Qwen2.5-32B（相对调和均值提升 12.5%），在英文 GraphRAG 任务中表现相当。在 GraphRAG-Bench（医学）上，RAGU 在每个事实层级均检索最完整上下文（证据召回率高达 0.84，对比 ≤0.76），并在合成任务中超越 HippoRAG2；在多跳事实问答中，HippoRAG2 的优势被证明主要源于答案格式偏差。RAGU 可通过 pip install graph_ragu 安装，单 GPU 即可运行，采用 MIT 许可发布。源代码公开于 https://github.com/RaguTeam/RAGU，Meno-Lite-0.1 模型可从 https://huggingface.co/bond005/meno-lite-0.1 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of single-pass knowledge graph construction in GraphRAG systems—namely noisy entities and brittle retrieval—RAGU introduces a multi-step pipeline that separates extraction from consolidation, using two-stage typed extraction, DBSCAN deduplication, LLM summarization, and Leiden community detection. It leverages a compact 7B LLM, Meno-Lite-0.1, specifically optimized for language skills rather than factual knowledge, which outperforms a larger 32B model on knowledge-graph construction and matches it on English GraphRAG tasks. Experimentally, RAGU achieves superior evidence recall (up to 0.84 on GraphRAG-Bench Medical) and outperforms HippoRAG2 on synthesis tasks, while revealing that HippoRAG2’s apparent advantage in multi-hop QA stems from answer-format artifacts.
为解决现有GraphRAG系统单次提取导致实体噪声和检索脆弱的问题，RAGU提出多阶段图构建流程，将实体与关系提取与整合分离，采用双阶段类型提取、DBSCAN去重、LLM摘要及Leiden社区检测。其核心洞察是：语言能力（如理解、提取、上下文推理）随模型规模增长缓慢，因此训练了专用于语言技能的7B模型Meno-Lite-0.1，该模型在知识图谱构建上优于32B模型（相对调和均值+12.5%），在英文GraphRAG任务中表现相当。实验表明，RAGU在GraphRAG-Bench（医学）上每层事实召回率最高（达0.84），合成任务超越HippoRAG2，且多跳问答中HippoRAG2优势实为答案格式偏差所致。
Illuminant-Adaptive 3D Lookup Tables for Camera Color Correction
Authors: Claudio Rota, Luca Cogo, Simone Bianco, Raimondo Schettini
First: 2026-07-13T15:19:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:19:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Color correction is a key component of camera image signal processing (ISP) pipelines, encompassing illuminant discounting and colorimetric mapping of device-dependent sensor responses to device-independent color spaces, such as CIE XYZ. Despite extensive research, accurate color correction remains challenging due to the non-linear relationship between camera sensor responses and CIE XYZ color space, as well as to the increasing presence of highly chromatic and spectrally complex LED illuminants. We propose a color correction framework based on illuminant-adaptive three-dimensional lookup tables (LUTs), which we call Color Correction LUT (C$^2$LUT). Our method combines a chromaticity-aware illuminant representation with a non-linear color transformation, enabling accurate correction under illuminants spanning a wide range of chromaticities and spectral complexities. We employ Tucker tensor decomposition to represent the LUTs, ensuring that computational requirements remain sufficiently low for deployment in camera ISPs. In addition, we introduce a large-scale illuminants dataset comprising 1,473 spectral power distributions, with different chromaticities and spectral profiles. Experiments across multiple cameras, illuminants, reflectance datasets, and real captured images demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods for color correction, reducing CIE $ΔE_{00}$ by up to 20% and angular error by up to 18% while remaining compatible with modern camera hardware constraints. Code and datasets are available at https://github.com/claudiom4sir/C2LUT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于光照自适应三维查找表的相机色彩校正
色彩校正是相机图像信号处理（ISP）流程中的关键环节，涵盖光照补偿及将设备依赖的传感器响应映射至设备无关的颜色空间（如CIE XYZ）。尽管已有大量研究，但准确的色彩校正仍具挑战性，原因在于相机传感器响应与CIE XYZ颜色空间之间的非线性关系，以及高色度和光谱复杂性LED光源的日益普及。我们提出一种基于光照自适应三维查找表（LUT）的色彩校正框架，称为色彩校正LUT（C²LUT）。该方法结合了色度感知的光照表示与非线性色彩变换，可在广泛色度和光谱复杂度的光照条件下实现精确校正。我们采用Tucker张量分解表示LUT，确保其计算开销足够低，适用于相机ISP部署。此外，我们构建了一个包含1,473个光谱功率分布的大规模光照数据集，涵盖不同色度和光谱特性。在多款相机、多种光照、反射率数据集及真实拍摄图像上的实验表明，该方法相较现有方法持续提升色彩校正效果，CIE ΔE₀₀降低最高达20%，角度误差降低最高达18%，同时兼容现代相机硬件约束。代码与数据集可访问：https://github.com/claudiom4sir/C2LUT。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of accurate color correction under complex and chromatic LED illuminants, this paper introduces C$^2$LUT, an illuminant-adaptive 3D lookup table framework that combines chromaticity-aware illuminant representation with non-linear color transformation. Using Tucker tensor decomposition, the method reduces computational load while maintaining high accuracy across diverse lighting conditions. Evaluated on multiple cameras and real-world datasets, C$^2$LUT consistently outperforms existing methods, reducing CIE $ΔE_{00}$ by up to 20% and angular error by up to 18%, while remaining compatible with modern camera hardware constraints.
为了解决在复杂色度LED光源下因传感器响应与CIE XYZ空间非线性映射导致的色彩校正难题，本文提出C$^2$LUT框架，结合色度感知光源表示与非线性色彩变换，构建自适应3D查找表。通过Tucker张量分解压缩查找表，确保计算开销低，适合嵌入相机ISP系统。在多款相机、多种光源及真实图像上的实验表明，该方法相较现有方法平均降低CIE $ΔE_{00}$ 20%、角度误差18%，且满足硬件约束。
INFANiTE: Implicit Neural representation for high-resolution Fetal brain spatio-temporal Atlas learNing from clinical Thick-slicE MRI
Authors: Xiaotian Hu, Mingxuan Liu, Hongjia Yang, Juncheng Zhu, Yijin Li, Yifei Chen, Haoxiang Li, Tongxi Song, Zihan Li, Yingqi Hao, Ziyu Li, Yujin Zhang, Gang Ning, Yi Liao, Haibo Qu, Qiyuan Tian
First: 2026-05-11T04:39:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:17:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spatio-temporal fetal brain atlases are important for characterizing normative neurodevelopment and identifying congenital anomalies. However, existing atlas construction pipelines necessitate days for slice-to-volume reconstruction (SVR) to generate high-resolution 3D brain volumes and several additional days for iterative volume registration, thereby rendering atlas construction from large-scale cohorts prohibitively impractical. We address these limitations with INFANiTE, an Implicit Neural Representation (INR) framework for high-resolution Fetal brain spatio-temporal Atlas learNing from clinical Thick-slicE MRI scans, bypassing both the costly SVR and the iterative non-rigid registration steps entirely, thereby substantially accelerating atlas construction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that INFANiTE outperforms existing baselines in subject consistency, reference fidelity, intrinsic quality and biological plausibility, even under challenging sparse-data settings.
