Additionally, INFANiTE reduces the end-to-end processing time (i.e., from raw scans to the final atlas) from days to hours compared to the traditional 3D volume-based pipeline (e.g., SyGN), facilitating large-scale population-level fetal brain analysis. Our code is publicly available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/INFANiTE-5D74

Direct Object-Level Reconstruction via Probabilistic Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Shuai Guo, Ao Guo, Junchao Zhao, Qi Chen, Yuxiang Qi, Zechuan Li, Dong Chen, Mingliang Xu
First: 2026-03-15T10:17:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:16:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Object-level 3D reconstruction play important roles across domains such as cultural heritage digitization, industrial manufacturing, and virtual reality. However, existing Gaussian Splatting-based approaches generally rely on full-scene reconstruction, in which substantial redundant background information is introduced, leading to increased computational and storage overhead. To address this limitation, we propose an efficient single-object 3D reconstruction method based on 2D Gaussian Splatting. By directly integrating foreground-background probability cues into Gaussian primitives and dynamically pruning low-probability Gaussians during training, the proposed method fundamentally focuses on an object of interest and improves the memory and computational efficiency. Our pipeline leverages probability masks generated by YOLO and SAM to supervise probabilistic Gaussian attributes, replacing binary masks with continuous probability values to mitigate boundary ambiguity. Additionally, we propose a dual-stage filtering strategy for training's startup to suppress background Gaussians. And, during training, rendered probability masks are conversely employed to refine supervision and enhance boundary consistency across views. Experiments conducted on the MIP-360, T&T, and NVOS datasets demonstrate that our method exhibits strong self-correction capability in the presence of mask errors and achieves reconstruction quality comparable to standard 3DGS approaches, while requiring only approximately 1/10 of their Gaussian amount. These results validate the efficiency and robustness of our method for single-object reconstruction and highlight its potential for applications requiring both high fidelity and computational efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过概率高斯点绘实现直接物体级重建
物体级3D重建在文化遗产数字化、工业制造和虚拟现实等领域具有重要作用。然而，现有基于高斯点绘的方法通常依赖全场景重建，引入大量冗余背景信息，导致计算和存储开销增加。为解决此问题，我们提出一种基于2D高斯点绘的高效单物体3D重建方法。通过将前景-背景概率线索直接集成到高斯基本体中，并在训练过程中动态剪枝低概率高斯，该方法从根本上聚焦于目标物体，提升内存与计算效率。我们的流程利用YOLO和SAM生成的概率掩码监督概率高斯属性，用连续概率值替代二值掩码以缓解边界模糊问题。此外，我们提出双阶段过滤策略用于训练初期抑制背景高斯，并在训练中利用渲染的概率掩码反向优化监督，增强多视角边界一致性。在MIP-360、T&T和NVOS数据集上的实验表明，该方法在掩码误差情况下具备强自校正能力，重建质量与标准3DGS方法相当，但仅需其约1/10的高斯数量。结果验证了该方法在单物体重建中的高效性与鲁棒性，凸显其在追求高保真与计算效率并存的应用场景中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of full-scene Gaussian Splatting methods that introduce redundant background data, this work proposes a direct object-level 3D reconstruction technique using probabilistic Gaussian Splatting. The method integrates foreground-background probability cues into Gaussian primitives and prunes low-probability Gaussians during training to focus computation on the target object. Supervised by probability masks from YOLO and SAM, the approach replaces binary masks with continuous values to reduce boundary ambiguity, and employs a dual-stage filtering strategy to suppress background Gaussians early in training. Rendered probability masks are further used to refine supervision and improve cross-view boundary consistency. Experiments on MIP-360, T&T, and NVOS show that the method achieves reconstruction quality comparable to full-scene Gaussian Splatting while using only about 1/10 the number of Gaussians, demonstrating strong self-correction under mask errors and high efficiency.
为解决传统基于高斯点云的全场景重建方法引入大量冗余背景导致计算与存储开销过大的问题，本文提出一种直接面向单个目标物体的3D重建方法，基于概率高斯点云。该方法将前景-背景概率信息直接融入高斯点云，并在训练中动态剔除低概率高斯点，聚焦目标物体。通过YOLO与SAM生成的概率掩码监督高斯点云属性，用连续概率值替代二值掩码以缓解边界模糊问题，并采用双阶段过滤策略在训练初期抑制背景高斯点。训练过程中，渲染的概率掩码反向用于优化监督，提升多视角边界一致性。在MIP-360、T&T和NVOS数据集上的实验表明，该方法在掩码误差下具备强自校正能力，重建质量与标准3DGS相当，但高斯点数量仅为后者的约十分之一，验证了其高效性与鲁棒性。
ABot-3DWorld 0: A Universal World Model to Explore Any 3D Space
Authors: Mingchao Sun, Luyang Tang, Yu Liu, Xu Yan, Zhan Li, Yunwei Zhang, Fei Yu, Zengye Ge, Yumin Liu, Jiacheng Zhang, Yongchang Zhang, Jiawei Zhang, Zhicheng Liu, Zhongxu Sun, Tianjian Ouyang, Wenzheng Chen, Shixing Yang, Nianfei Fan, Guodong Sun, Huan Li, Zheng Zhou, Yongze Li, Yingliang Peng, Mengmeng Du, Yuan Liu, Haozhe Shi, Chunnuo Gong, Chengzhen Yu, Chunxue Jia, Yang Liu, Shiying Zeng, Junnan Lai, Hang Zhang, Ning Guo, Baoquan Chen, Mu Xu, Hongyu Pan
First: 2026-07-13T15:14:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:14:48+00:00
Comments: Official Page: https://abot-world.amap.com/plaza
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present ABot-3DWorld 0, a universal multimodal 3D world model that turns text, image, and video inputs into high-fidelity, explorable 3D worlds. At the heart of our framework is a unified Spatial Generative Primitive (SGP), a compact tuple of a high-quality panorama and a spatial point cloud that delivers an efficient description of any 3D space. Multimodal inputs are first lifted into this primitive; a 3D-consistent panoramic video generator then explores the primitive along a planned trajectory; finally, our panoramic video reconstruction engine converts the generated video into a clean, photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) world. This pipeline covers two regimes: rich inputs (multi-view sets, casual video) are lifted into the SGP through a geometry-rigorous recovery that mirrors the observed scene, while a single image or sentence is completed generatively into a creative world. The result is one low-barrier engine for general 3D content creation that further anchors generated worlds to geographic points of interest, enabling map-native spatial exploration at consumer scale. Experiments show that ABot-3DWorld 0 sets the state of the art among open-source methods and demonstrates stronger scene fidelity than Marble under rich multimodal inputs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ABot-3DWorld 0：一种通用世界模型，可探索任意3D空间
我们提出了ABot-3DWorld 0，一种通用的多模态3D世界模型，可将文本、图像和视频输入转化为高保真、可探索的3D世界。该框架的核心是统一空间生成原语（SGP），即一个高质量全景图与空间点云的紧凑元组，能高效描述任意3D空间。多模态输入首先被提升至该原语；随后，一个3D一致的全景视频生成器沿规划轨迹探索该原语；最后，我们的全景视频重建引擎将生成的视频转换为干净、逼真的3D高斯点云（3DGS）世界。该流程涵盖两种模式：丰富输入（多视角集合、随意视频）通过几何严谨的恢复方式映射至SGP，以镜像观测场景；而单张图像或句子则通过生成方式扩展为创意世界。结果是一个低门槛的通用3D内容创作引擎，进一步将生成世界锚定至地理兴趣点，实现面向消费者的地图原生空间探索。实验表明，ABot-3DWorld 0在开源方法中达到最先进水平，并在丰富多模态输入下展现出比Marble更强的场景保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a universal, accessible tool to generate high-fidelity 3D worlds from diverse inputs, the authors introduce ABot-3DWorld 0, a multimodal system that converts text, images, and videos into explorable 3D environments using a Spatial Generative Primitive (SGP). The method first encodes inputs into an SGP—a compact panorama and point cloud—then generates panoramic videos along planned paths, finally reconstructing them into photorealistic 3D Gaussian Splatting worlds. For rich inputs, the system performs geometry-aware reconstruction; for sparse inputs like single images or text, it generates creative completions. Experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance among open-source methods, with superior scene fidelity compared to Marble under multimodal inputs.
为实现从多种模态输入生成可探索的3D世界，作者提出ABot-3DWorld 0系统，其核心是空间生成原语（SGP），将场景编码为全景图与点云。系统对丰富输入（如多视角图像或视频）进行几何严谨重建，对单图或文本则进行生成式扩展，再通过全景视频生成器与3D高斯点渲染引擎输出逼真3D世界。实验表明，该方法在多模态输入下场景保真度优于Marble，且在开源方法中达到当前最优，支持消费级地理空间探索。
A multi-scale feature enhanced graph neural network for fluid dynamics prediction in complex geometries
Authors: Li Xiao, Tianyu Li, Yiye Zou, Mingjie Zhang, Xiaogangd Deng
First: 2026-07-13T15:13:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:13:41+00:00
Comments: 14 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Industrial design in fields such as vehicle and aerospace engineering often relies on large-scale numerical simulations to evaluate fluid dynamics performance, which can incur substantial computational costs. Deep neural networks have shown promise in improving simulation efficiency, especially graph neural networks (GNNs), which demonstrate great potential due to their flexibility with unstructured data. However, GNNs face challenges when dealing with tasks involving complex geometries and large-scale meshes. In this paper, we propose the Multi-scale Feature Enhanced Graph Neural Network (ME-GNN) to tackle these challenges. ME-GNN employs a graph neural network with a two-step message-passing mechanism to capture detailed local features effectively. Additionally, it integrates an Attention U-Net with uniform grid discretization, enabling the extraction of both fine and coarse features. The model also utilizes K-hop sampling to construct subgraphs, facilitating efficient training on large datasets while preserving detailed local features. We evaluated ME-GNN on three benchmark datasets and achieved state-of-the-art results: a relative L2 error of 0.0196 for the velocity field and 0.0556 for the surface pressure on ShapeNet-Car, a normalized mean squared error of 0.0033 for the flow field on AirfRANS, and a relative L2 error of 0.1416 for the surface pressure on DrivAerNet.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于复杂几何流体动力学预测的多尺度特征增强图神经网络
汽车和航空航天工程等领域的工业设计常依赖大规模数值模拟来评估流体动力学性能，这会带来高昂的计算成本。深度神经网络在提升模拟效率方面展现出潜力，尤其是图神经网络（GNN），因其对非结构化数据的灵活性而具有巨大潜力。然而，GNN在处理复杂几何和大规模网格的任务时面临挑战。本文提出多尺度特征增强图神经网络（ME-GNN）以应对这些挑战。ME-GNN采用具有两阶段消息传递机制的图神经网络，有效捕捉局部细节特征；同时结合注意力U-Net与均匀网格离散化，实现细粒度与粗粒度特征的提取。模型还利用K跳采样构建子图，便于在大规模数据集上高效训练并保留局部细节特征。我们在三个基准数据集上评估ME-GNN，取得了最先进结果：在ShapeNet-Car上速度场相对L2误差为0.0196，表面压力为0.0556；在AirfRANS上流场归一化均方误差为0.0033；在DrivAerNet上表面压力相对L2误差为0.1416。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational burden of fluid dynamics simulations in complex geometries, this paper introduces ME-GNN, a graph neural network that combines a two-step message-passing mechanism for local feature extraction with an Attention U-Net for multi-scale feature integration. It further employs K-hop sampling to build efficient subgraphs, enabling scalable training while preserving fine-grained details. Evaluated on three benchmarks, ME-GNN achieved state-of-the-art performance: 0.0196 relative L2 error for velocity and 0.0556 for surface pressure on ShapeNet-Car, 0.0033 normalized MSE on AirfRANS flow field, and 0.1416 relative L2 error on DrivAerNet surface pressure.
为降低复杂几何体流体动力学模拟的计算成本，本文提出多尺度特征增强图神经网络（ME-GNN）。该模型采用两阶段消息传递机制捕捉局部细节，并结合注意力U-Net与均匀网格离散化提取多尺度特征，同时利用K-hop采样构建子图以高效训练大规模网格数据。在三个基准数据集上，ME-GNN取得最优性能：ShapeNet-Car上速度场相对L2误差0.0196、表面压力0.0556；AirfRANS上流场归一化均方误差0.0033；DrivAerNet上表面压力相对L2误差0.1416。
Losing My Composure: Predicting Compositionality Over Time
Authors: Chris Jenkins, Emma Raimundo Schulz, Filip Miletić, Sabine Schulte im Walde
First: 2026-07-13T15:11:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:11:58+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 31 tables, 10 figures. This is a pre-print under review by Computational Linguistics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We explore the phenomenon of semantic change of German and English noun compounds, with the objective of investigating and modeling gradual changes of meanings and degrees of compositionality in the past and over time. To do so, we introduce the Compositionality Trend Prediction task, which is evaluated against a novel dataset of in-context compositionality ratings sampled across several decades of diachronic corpora for 23 German and 26 English target compounds, uniquely providing per-decade ratings and corresponding trends over time. These per-decade compositionality ratings allow us to investigate empirically untested hypotheses of generalized trends in compositionality over time, such as the idea that compounds should become less compositional (less transparent) over time. Beyond our empirical observations from the diachronic compositionality annotations, we perform experiments with semantic vector representations of varying complexity, as well as several temporal granularities for training these representations on diachronic data, resulting in about 100 models of each representation type, each covering a different 1--5 decade slice of a diachronic corpus. Contrary to the decisive tendency posited in the literature, we find only a small negative trend in compositionality over time in our target compounds. In our computational experiments, we find that using models trained on narrow time slices of diachronic data (single decades, or incrementally expanding temporal windows) align better with the per-decade compositionality ratings than those trained on an entire half-century window, the latter setting being an analog for the prevalent modeling approach of training representations on an entire half of a corpus' data. Additionally, we find static representations to be competitive with contextual representations in the Compositionality Trend Prediction task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：失去我的镇定：预测随时间推移的组合性变化
我们探讨德语和英语名词复合词的语义变化现象，旨在研究和建模过去及随时间推移的语义渐变与组合性程度变化。为此，我们引入了组合性趋势预测任务，并基于一个新颖的语料库构建数据集，该数据集包含跨越数十年的历时语料中对23个德语和26个英语目标复合词的上下文组合性评分，独特地提供按十年划分的评分及相应的时间趋势。这些按十年划分的组合性评分使我们能够检验文献中尚未实证验证的组合性随时间变化的普遍趋势假设，例如化合物随时间推移应变得不那么组合性（不那么透明）。除了基于历时组合性标注的实证观察外，我们还对不同复杂度的语义向量表示进行实验，并采用多种时间粒度训练这些表示，从而构建约100个模型，每个模型覆盖历时语料中不同的1至5十年时间片。与文献中提出的决定性趋势相反，我们在目标复合词中仅发现微弱的组合性随时间下降趋势。在计算实验中，我们发现使用在窄时间片（单十年或逐步扩展的时间窗口）上训练的模型，比在半个世纪窗口上训练的模型（后者是当前主流建模方法的模拟）更符合按十年划分的组合性评分。此外，我们发现静态表示在组合性趋势预测任务中与上下文表示具有竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model how meaning and compositionality evolve in German and English noun compounds over time, the authors introduce the Compositionality Trend Prediction task using a novel diachronic dataset with per-decade compositionality ratings for 49 target compounds. They train and evaluate over 100 models using varying semantic vector representations and temporal training windows (1–5 decades), finding only a weak negative trend in compositionality over time—contrary to the literature’s assumption of decreasing compositionality. Their results show that models trained on narrow temporal slices (single decades or incremental windows) outperform those trained on broad half-century windows, and that static representations perform comparably to contextual ones in predicting compositionality trends.
为研究德语和英语名词复合词随时间演变的语义组合性变化，本研究提出组合性趋势预测任务，并构建了一个包含49个目标复合词、按十年标注组合性评分的新型历时语料库。作者训练并评估了100多个模型，使用不同复杂度的语义向量表示和时间粒度，检验组合性是否随时间递减的假设。结果发现组合性仅呈现微弱下降趋势，与主流理论预期不符。更重要的是，基于窄时间窗口（单十年或递增窗口）训练的模型表现优于基于半个世纪窗口训练的模型，表明细粒度时间建模更有效。静态表示在该任务中也与上下文表示表现相当。
PivotMerge: Bridging Heterogeneous Multimodal Pre-training via Post-Alignment Model Merging
Authors: Zibo Shao, Baochen Xiong, Xiaoshan Yang, Yaguang Song, Qimeng Zhang, Haifeng Chen, Changsheng Xu
First: 2026-04-18T09:38:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-13T15:11:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) rely on multimodal pre-training over diverse data sources, where different datasets often induce complementary cross-modal alignment capabilities. Model merging provides a cost-effective mechanism for integrating multiple expert MLLMs with complementary strengths into a unified model. However, existing model merging research mainly focuses on post-finetuning scenarios, leaving the pre-training stage largely unexplored. We argue that the core of MLLM pre-training lies in establishing effective cross-modal alignment, which bridges visual and textual representations into a unified semantic space. Motivated by this insight, we introduce the post-alignment merging task, which aims to integrate cross-modal alignment capabilities learned from heterogeneous multimodal pre-training. This setting introduces two key challenges: cross-domain parameter interference, where parameter updates learned from different data distributions conflict during merging, and layer-wise alignment contribution disparity, where different layers and projectors contribute unevenly to cross-modal alignment. To address them, we propose \textbf{PivotMerge}, a post-alignment merging framework for cross-modal projectors. PivotMerge incorporates two key components: Shared-space Decomposition and Filtering, which disentangles shared alignment patterns from domain-specific variations and suppresses conflicting directions, and Alignment-guided Layer-wise Merging, which assigns layer-specific merging weights based on differing alignment contributions. We construct systematic CC12M-based post-alignment merging scenarios for evaluation. Extensive experiments on multiple multimodal benchmarks show that PivotMerge consistently outperforms existing baselines, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalization ability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PivotMerge：通过后对齐模型融合弥合异构多模态预训练的鸿沟
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）依赖于多样数据源上的多模态预训练，不同数据集往往能诱导互补的跨模态对齐能力。模型融合提供了一种低成本机制，用于将多个具备互补优势的专家MLLMs整合为统一模型。然而，现有模型融合研究主要聚焦于后微调场景，预训练阶段尚待探索。我们认为，MLLM预训练的核心在于建立有效的跨模态对齐，将视觉与文本表征桥接至统一语义空间。受此启发，我们引入后对齐融合任务，旨在整合异构多模态预训练中习得的跨模态对齐能力。该设定带来两大关键挑战：跨域参数干扰，即不同数据分布习得的参数更新在融合时冲突；层间对齐贡献不均，即不同层与投影器对跨模态对齐的贡献不均衡。为应对这些问题，我们提出\textbf{PivotMerge}，一种面向跨模态投影器的后对齐融合框架。PivotMerge包含两个关键组件：共享空间分解与过滤，用于分离共享对齐模式与领域特异性变化并抑制冲突方向；对齐引导的层间融合，根据各层对跨模态对齐贡献差异分配层特定融合权重。我们构建基于CC12M的系统化后对齐融合评估场景。在多个多模态基准上的大量实验表明，PivotMerge持续优于现有基线，验证了其有效性与泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to integrate complementary cross-modal alignment capabilities from heterogeneous multimodal pre-training data, the paper introduces PivotMerge, a post-alignment model merging framework designed to combine expert MLLMs without requiring fine-tuning. It addresses two key challenges: cross-domain parameter interference and uneven layer-wise alignment contributions, by decomposing shared alignment patterns and filtering domain-specific noise, then assigning layer-specific merging weights based on alignment effectiveness. Evaluated on CC12M-based scenarios across multiple multimodal benchmarks, PivotMerge consistently outperforms existing baselines, demonstrating strong generalization and effectiveness in merging pre-trained models.
为整合异构多模态预训练数据中互补的跨模态对齐能力，作者提出PivotMerge框架，旨在无需微调即可合并多个专家级多模态大语言模型。该方法通过共享空间分解与过滤分离通用对齐模式与领域特异性噪声，并通过对齐引导的层间融合机制，根据各层对跨模态对齐的贡献动态分配融合权重。在基于CC12M构建的多个多模态基准测试中，PivotMerge持续优于现有基线方法，展现出良好的泛化能力和有效性。
